E           def project_features_to_original_holes(features, width, height):
E               """Project supported Phase6 features into the original Renderer's hole DTO.
E           
E               Raw Phase6 feature objects are never edited. Profile/custom holes stay in the
E               bundle but are intentionally not falsified as circles/rectangles for preview.
E               """
E               from ae_engine.sheetmetal_features import CircleFeature, RectFeature, feature_finished_point
E           
E               holes = []
E               for index, feature in enumerate(features or (), start=1):
E                   if not isinstance(feature, (CircleFeature, RectFeature)):
E                       continue
E                   center = feature_finished_point(feature, float(width), float(height))
E                   if isinstance(feature, CircleFeature):
E                       hole = {
E                           "type": "圓孔", "name": f"孔{len(holes)+1}",
E                           "x": _ui_len(center.x), "y": _ui_len(center.y),
E                           "d1": _ui_len(feature.diameter), "d2": 0,
E                       }
E                   else:
E                       hole = {
E                           "type": "方孔", "name": f"孔{len(holes)+1}",
E                           "x": _ui_len(center.x), "y": _ui_len(center.y),
E                           "d1": _ui_len(feature.width), "d2": _ui_len(feature.height),
E                       }
E                   holes.append(hole)
E               return holes

E           def _phase6_resolve_profile_key(active_dict: Mapping[str, object], requested: object) -> str:
E               """Resolve stale notebook/renderer labels to a real editable profile key."""
E               key = str(requested)
E               if key in active_dict:
E                   return key
E               if "X" in active_dict:
E                   return "X"
E               try:
E                   return next(iter(active_dict))
E               except StopIteration:
E                   raise KeyError(key)

E                               return None

E                           if is_box:
E                               # Never fall back from a known structural Phase6 row to raw
E                               # positional mirroring: after asymmetric add/remove history
E                               # that is exactly how FW/D/Z fields became cross-wired.
E                               if source_key in pair:
E                                   self.save(); self.update_cb()
E                                   return None

E                   # leaves typed edits only in Tk variables. Phase6 must persist every

E               def on_tab(self, event):
E                   # Programmatic tab selection during part switching already renders the
E                   # selected profile in rebuild_tabs().  Tk still emits TabChanged later;
E                   # do not redraw/schedule another full 3D update when the key did not
E                   # actually change.  Real operator tab clicks still follow the original
E                   # path below.
E                   try:
E                       idx = self.nb.index("current")
E                   except Exception:
E                       return
E                   if not (0 <= idx < len(self.tabs)):
E                       return
E                   key = self.tabs[idx]
E                   if key == self.state.active_bend:
E                       return
E                   self.state.active_bend = key
E                   self.render()
E                   self._phase6_refresh_symmetry_bar()
E                   self.update_cb()

E               def save(self):

E                   if vault_key and active_key == "X":

E           class Phase6FoldDesignerApp(original.MainApp):
E               """Original MainApp loaded with Phase6 data; Renderer is untouched."""
E           
E               def __init__(self, root, snapshot: Mapping[str, object]):
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot = dict(snapshot)
E                   self._phase6_sync_ready = False
E                   self._phase6_last_w = None
E                   self._phase6_last_d = None
E                   self._phase6_destroying = False
E                   saved_bending_ui = original.BendingUI
E                   original.BendingUI = Phase6BendingUI
E                   try:
E                       super().__init__(root)
E                   finally:
E                       original.BendingUI = saved_bending_ui
E                   # Tk ``after`` jobs belong to the Tcl interpreter, not to the Toplevel
E                   # that scheduled them.  Destroying the designer therefore does not
E                   # automatically clear Python-side job ownership.  Bind cleanup to the
E                   # designer root itself so direct ``Toplevel.destroy()`` callers and the
E                   # normal GUI close path share the same teardown invariant.
E                   self.root.bind("<Destroy>", self._phase6_on_root_destroy, add="+")
E                   self.load_phase6_snapshot(snapshot)
E           
E               def _phase6_cancel_owned_tk_jobs(self):
E                   for attr in ("_job", "_phase6_settings_debounce_job"):
E                       job = getattr(self, attr, None)
E                       if job is not None:
E                           try:
E                               self.root.after_cancel(job)
E                           except Exception:
E                               pass
E                       setattr(self, attr, None)
E                   if hasattr(self, "_phase6_pending_settings"):
E                       self._phase6_pending_settings = {}
E           
E               def _phase6_on_root_destroy(self, event):
E                   if getattr(event, "widget", None) is not self.root:
E                       return
E                   self._phase6_destroying = True
E                   self._phase6_cancel_owned_tk_jobs()
E           
E               def load_phase6_snapshot(self, snapshot: Mapping[str, object]):
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot = dict(snapshot)
E                   stored = snapshot.get("box_body_profile")
E                   if stored:
E                       self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"] = merge_box_body_profile(stored, snapshot)
E                   else:
E                       self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"] = build_box_body_profile(snapshot)
E                   endcap = build_endcap_profile(snapshot)
E                   self.state.profiles_vault["封頭"] = clone_profile(endcap)
E                   self.state.profiles_vault["封尾"] = clone_profile(endcap)
E                   self.state.w = original.get_int(snapshot.get("w", self.state.w))
E                   self.state.h = original.get_int(snapshot.get("h", self.state.h))
E                   self.state.d = original.get_int(snapshot.get("d", self.state.d))
E                   self.v_w.set(str(self.state.w))
E                   self.v_h.set(str(self.state.h))
E                   self.v_d.set(str(self.state.d))
E                   self.state.struct_mode = "vault"
E                   self.v_mode.set("vault")
E                   self.bend_ui.rebuild_tabs()
E                   self._phase6_last_w = self.state.w
E                   self._phase6_last_d = self.state.d
E                   self._phase6_sync_ready = True
E                   self.do_update()
E           
E               def _sync_dwd_with_top_whd(self):
E                   profile = self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])
E                   w_segments = [seg for seg in profile if seg.get("core") == "W"]
E                   d_segments = [seg for seg in profile if seg.get("core") == "D"]
E                   if len(w_segments) != 1 or len(d_segments) != 2:
E                       return
E           
E                   top_w = original.get_int(self.v_w.get())
E                   top_d = original.get_int(self.v_d.get())
E                   core_w = original.get_int(engine_segment_length_to_ui(w_segments[0]))
E                   d_values = [original.get_int(engine_segment_length_to_ui(seg)) for seg in d_segments]
E           
E                   last_w = self._phase6_last_w
E                   last_d = self._phase6_last_d
E                   if top_w != last_w:
E                       w_segments[0]["len"] = ui_segment_length_to_engine(w_segments[0], top_w)
E                       core_w = top_w
E                   elif core_w != last_w:
E                       self.v_w.set(str(core_w))
E                       top_w = core_w
E           
E                   if top_d != last_d:
E                       for seg in d_segments:
E                           seg["len"] = ui_segment_length_to_engine(seg, top_d)
E                       d_value = top_d
E                   else:
E                       changed = [value for value in d_values if value != last_d]
E                       d_value = changed[0] if changed else d_values[0]
E                       for seg in d_segments:
E                           seg["len"] = ui_segment_length_to_engine(seg, d_value)
E                       if d_value != top_d:
E                           self.v_d.set(str(d_value))
E           
E                   self._phase6_last_w = top_w
E                   self._phase6_last_d = d_value
E           
E               def do_update(self):
E                   if getattr(self, "_phase6_sync_ready", False):
E                       self._sync_dwd_with_top_whd()
E                   return original.MainApp.do_update(self)
E           
E               def export_phase6_snapshot(self) -> dict:
E                   self.bend_ui.save()
E                   result = dict(self._phase6_input_snapshot)
E                   result.update(read_box_body_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"], self._phase6_input_snapshot))
E                   head = read_endcap_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["封頭"])
E                   tail = read_endcap_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["封尾"])
E                   if head != tail:
E                       raise ValueError("Phase6 目前封頭/封尾共用同一組折彎值，兩個分頁必須一致")
E                   result.update(head)
E                   result["h"] = original.get_int(self.v_h.get())
E                   result["box_body_profile"] = clone_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"])
E                   result["head_profile"] = clone_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["封頭"])
E                   result["tail_profile"] = clone_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["封尾"])
E                   if hasattr(self, "designer_workspace"):
E                       result["box_body_structure"] = self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state()
E                   return result
E           
E           # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
E           # FIX11 multi-part bundle helpers. These adapt data only; original Renderer is
E           # deliberately untouched.
E           # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
E           
E           def _three_segment_profile(left, center, right, *, left_key=None, center_key=None, right_key=None):
E               items = [
E                   {"len": _ui_len(left), "angle": -90},
E                   {"len": _ui_len(center), "angle": -90},
E                   {"len": _ui_len(right)},
E               ]
E               for item, key in zip(items, (left_key, center_key, right_key)):
E                   if key:
E                       item["phase6_key"] = key
E               return items
E           
E           
E           def build_standard_part_profiles(snapshot: Mapping[str, object], part_key: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
E               """Build the original designer's X/Y profiles for one non-vault panel."""
E               dims = dict((snapshot.get("part_dimensions") or {}).get(part_key, {}) or {})
E               w = _num(dims.get("width", snapshot.get("w", 500)), 500)
E               h = _num(dims.get("height", snapshot.get("h", 600)), 600)
E               if part_key == "door":
E                   t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
E                   outside_add = max(0.0, 2.0 * t)
E                   profiles = {
E                       "X": _three_segment_profile(
E                           snapshot.get("door_fold_l", 20), max(0.0, w - outside_add), snapshot.get("door_fold_r", 20),
E                           left_key="door_fold_l", center_key="door_face_w", right_key="door_fold_r",
E                       ),
E                       "Y": _three_segment_profile(
E                           snapshot.get("door_fold_b", 20), max(0.0, h - outside_add), snapshot.get("door_fold_t", 20),
E                           left_key="door_fold_b", center_key="door_face_h", right_key="door_fold_t",
E                       ),
E                   }
E                   profiles["X"][1]["core"] = "門包外 W"
E                   profiles["Y"][1]["core"] = "門包外 H"
E                   profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
E                   profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
E                   return profiles
E               if part_key == "base_plate":
E                   bend = snapshot.get("base_plate_bend", 20)
E                   profiles = {
E                       "X": _three_segment_profile(bend, w, bend, left_key="base_bend_l", center_key="base_face_w", right_key="base_bend_r"),
E                       "Y": _three_segment_profile(bend, h, bend, left_key="base_bend_b", center_key="base_face_h", right_key="base_bend_t"),
E                   }
E                   t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
E                   profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
E                   profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
E                   return profiles
E               if part_key == "indicator_box":
E                   # part_dimensions carries the real unfolded blank size.  The original
E                   # Renderer must receive the real BEND-line span (blank - both folds),
E                   # while FIX13 shows the operator the outside finished-face dimension.
E                   # For this four-side box that outside dimension is BEND span + 1T.
E                   fold = _num(snapshot.get("indicator_box_fold", 49), 49)
E                   t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
E                   x_core = max(0.0, w - 2.0 * fold)
E                   y_core = max(0.0, h - 2.0 * fold)
E                   profiles = {
E                       "X": _three_segment_profile(fold, x_core, fold, left_key="ib_fold_l", center_key="ib_face_w", right_key="ib_fold_r"),
E                       "Y": _three_segment_profile(fold, y_core, fold, left_key="ib_fold_b", center_key="ib_face_h", right_key="ib_fold_t"),
E                   }
E                   profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
E                   profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
E                   return profiles
E               if part_key == "indicator_door":
E                   # The small door's part_dimensions is likewise the unfolded blank.
E                   # Its Door contract defines finished outside size as BEND span + 2T.
E                   fold = _num(snapshot.get("indicator_door_fold", 19), 19)
E                   t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
E                   x_core = max(0.0, w - 2.0 * fold)
E                   y_core = max(0.0, h - 2.0 * fold)
E                   profiles = {
E                       "X": _three_segment_profile(fold, x_core, fold, left_key="id_fold_l", center_key="id_face_w", right_key="id_fold_r"),
E                       "Y": _three_segment_profile(fold, y_core, fold, left_key="id_fold_b", center_key="id_face_h", right_key="id_fold_t"),
E                   }
E                   profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
E                   profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
E                   return profiles
E               # Generic Unknown add: start from a simple editable X/Y panel without
E               # inventing manufacturing policy.
E               t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
E               profiles = {
E                   "X": _three_segment_profile(25, w, 25),
E                   "Y": _three_segment_profile(25, h, 25),
E               }
E               profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
E               profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
E               return profiles
E           
E           
E           def _profile_value(profile, key, default=0):
E               for seg in profile or ():
E                   if seg.get("phase6_key") == key:
E                       return _ui_len(seg.get("len"))
E               return _ui_len(default)
E           
E           
E           def read_standard_part_profiles(part_key, profiles, original_snapshot):
E               """Return only Phase6 values that have an authoritative reverse mapping."""
E               x = list((profiles or {}).get("X", ()))
E               y = list((profiles or {}).get("Y", ()))
E               if part_key == "door":
E                   return {
E                       "door_fold_l": _profile_value(x, "door_fold_l", original_snapshot.get("door_fold_l", 20)),
E                       "door_fold_r": _profile_value(x, "door_fold_r", original_snapshot.get("door_fold_r", 20)),
E                       "door_fold_b": _profile_value(y, "door_fold_b", original_snapshot.get("door_fold_b", 20)),
E                       "door_fold_t": _profile_value(y, "door_fold_t", original_snapshot.get("door_fold_t", 20)),
E                   }
E               if part_key == "base_plate":
E                   vals = [
E                       _profile_value(x, "base_bend_l", original_snapshot.get("base_plate_bend", 20)),
E                       _profile_value(x, "base_bend_r", original_snapshot.get("base_plate_bend", 20)),
E                       _profile_value(y, "base_bend_b", original_snapshot.get("base_plate_bend", 20)),
E                       _profile_value(y, "base_bend_t", original_snapshot.get("base_plate_bend", 20)),
E                   ]
E                   if len(set(vals)) != 1:
E                       raise ValueError("底板四邊折彎目前由 Phase6 共用一個 bend 值，四邊必須相同")
E                   return {"base_plate_bend": vals[0]}
E               if part_key == "indicator_box":
E                   vals = [
E                       _profile_value(x, "ib_fold_l", original_snapshot.get("indicator_box_fold", 49)),
E                       _profile_value(x, "ib_fold_r", original_snapshot.get("indicator_box_fold", 49)),
E                       _profile_value(y, "ib_fold_b", original_snapshot.get("indicator_box_fold", 49)),
E                       _profile_value(y, "ib_fold_t", original_snapshot.get("indicator_box_fold", 49)),
E                   ]
E                   if len(set(vals)) != 1:
E                       raise ValueError("指示燈盒四邊折彎必須相同")
E                   return {"indicator_box_fold": vals[0]}
E               if part_key == "indicator_door":
E                   vals = [
E                       _profile_value(x, "id_fold_l", original_snapshot.get("indicator_door_fold", 19)),
E                       _profile_value(x, "id_fold_r", original_snapshot.get("indicator_door_fold", 19)),
E                       _profile_value(y, "id_fold_b", original_snapshot.get("indicator_door_fold", 19)),
E                       _profile_value(y, "id_fold_t", original_snapshot.get("indicator_door_fold", 19)),
E                   ]
E                   if len(set(vals)) != 1:
E                       raise ValueError("指示燈小門四邊折彎必須相同")
E                   return {"indicator_door_fold": vals[0]}
E               return {}
E           
E           
E           def _part_preview_size(snapshot, key):
E               dims = dict((snapshot.get("part_dimensions") or {}).get(key, {}) or {})
E               return (
E                   _num(dims.get("width", snapshot.get("w", 500)), 500),
E                   _num(dims.get("height", snapshot.get("h", 600)), 600),
E               )
E           
E           
E           def _copy_features(snapshot, key):
E               return list((snapshot.get("part_features") or {}).get(key, ()) or ())
E           
E           
E           
E           
E           
E           def _merge_keyed_profiles(existing_profiles, default_profiles):
E               """Refresh Phase6 keyed segments while preserving arbitrary extra folds."""
E               result = {}
E               for axis in ("X", "Y"):
E                   existing = clone_profile((existing_profiles or {}).get(axis, ()))
E                   defaults = clone_profile((default_profiles or {}).get(axis, ()))
E                   if not existing:
E                       result[axis] = defaults
E                       continue
E                   default_by_key = {
E                       seg.get("phase6_key"): seg for seg in defaults if seg.get("phase6_key")
E                   }
E                   existing_keys = {seg.get("phase6_key") for seg in existing if seg.get("phase6_key")}
E                   if not set(default_by_key).issubset(existing_keys):
E                       result[axis] = defaults
E                       continue
E                   for seg in existing:
E                       key = seg.get("phase6_key")
E                       source = default_by_key.get(key)
E                       if source is None:
E                           continue
E                       for name in ("len", "ui_len_add", "core"):
E                           if name in source:
E                               seg[name] = source[name]
E                           else:
E                               seg.pop(name, None)
E                   result[axis] = existing
E               return result
E           
E           
E           def _hide_original_global_dimension_controls(root_widget):
E               """Hide the prototype W/H/D structure block; Phase6 settings center owns it."""
E               for child in root_widget.winfo_children():
E                   try:
E                       text = str(child.cget("text"))
E                   except Exception:
E                       text = ""
E                   if "結構模式與空間約束" in text:
E                       manager = child.winfo_manager()
E                       if manager == "pack":
E                           child.pack_forget()
E                       elif manager == "grid":
E                           child.grid_remove()
E                       return True
E               return False
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_recalculate_part_dimensions(self):
E               values = self._settings_values
E               snapshot = self._phase6_input_snapshot
E               snapshot.update(values)
E               w = _num(values.get("w", snapshot.get("w", 500)), 500)
E               h = _num(values.get("h", snapshot.get("h", 600)), 600)
E               d = _num(values.get("d", snapshot.get("d", 200)), 200)
E               t = _num(values.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2)), 2)
E               fw = _num(values.get("fw", snapshot.get("fw", 25)), 25)
E               dims = {key: dict(value) for key, value in (snapshot.get("part_dimensions") or {}).items()}
E               dims["box_body"] = {"width": w, "height": h}
E               dims["head"] = {"width": w, "height": d}
E               dims["tail"] = {"width": w, "height": d}
E               door_w = max(1.0, w - (fw + 2.0 * t) * 2.0 - 2.0 * _num(values.get("door_gap_w", 3.5), 3.5))
E               door_h = max(1.0, h - (fw + 2.0 * t) * 2.0 - 2.0 * _num(values.get("door_gap_h", 3.5), 3.5))
E               dims["door"] = {"width": door_w, "height": door_h}
E               base_w = max(1.0, w - _num(values.get("base_plate_shrink_left", 55), 55) - _num(values.get("base_plate_shrink_right", 55), 55))
E               base_h = max(1.0, h - _num(values.get("base_plate_shrink_top", 55), 55) - _num(values.get("base_plate_shrink_bottom", 55), 55))
E               dims["base_plate"] = {"width": base_w, "height": base_h}
E               snapshot["part_dimensions"] = dims
E               return dims
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_refresh_profiles_from_settings(self):
E               """Push settings-center values into the existing fold profiles losslessly."""
E               snapshot = self._phase6_input_snapshot
E               snapshot.update(self._settings_values)
E               _phase6_recalculate_part_dimensions(self)
E           
E               current_box = self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])
E               self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"] = merge_box_body_profile(current_box, snapshot)
E           
E               _phase6_sync_authoritative_derived_parts(self)
E               for key in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   if key == "box_body" or _phase6_is_derived_physical_part_key(key):
E                       continue
E                   defaults = (
E                       build_endcap_xy_profiles(snapshot, part_key=key)
E                       if key in {"head", "tail"}
E                       else build_standard_part_profiles(snapshot, key)
E                   )
E                   existing = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(key, {}) or {}
E                   merged = _merge_keyed_profiles(existing, defaults)
E                   merged = (
E                       _phase6_normalize_endcap_profile_order(merged, snapshot, key)
E                       if key in {"head", "tail"} else merged
E                   )
E                   self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, merged)
E           
E               active = self.designer_workspace.active_part or "box_body"
E               if active == "box_body":
E                   self.state.phase6_fold_ui_profiles = {"X": self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"]}
E               elif active in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   profiles = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(active, {}) or {}
E                   self.state.profiles["X"] = clone_profile(profiles.get("X", []))
E                   self.state.profiles["Y"] = clone_profile(profiles.get("Y", []))
E               try:
E                   self.bend_ui.render()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_apply_setting_updates(self, updates, *, notify=True):
E               clean = {}
E               for key, raw in dict(updates or {}).items():
E                   if key not in self._settings_values:
E                       continue
E                   if key == "ui_text_size":
E                       clean[key] = normalize_ui_text_size(raw)
E                   elif isinstance(self._settings_values.get(key), bool):
E                       clean[key] = bool(raw)
E                   else:
E                       try:
E                           clean[key] = float(raw)
E                       except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                           continue
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_external_apply_guard", False):
E                   clean = {key: value for key, value in clean.items() if self._settings_values.get(key) != value}
E               if not clean:
E                   return {}
E           
E               # A legitimate global W edit is a commit seam, not a geometry-resolver
E               # repair path. Preserve the operator's last W-split driver and derive the
E               # complementary widths here; if the new W cannot produce a legal split,
E               # reject/revert W while leaving the strict resolver fail-closed.
E               if "w" in clean:
E                   previous_w = float((getattr(self, "_phase6_box_whd", {}) or {}).get(
E                       "w", getattr(getattr(self, "state", None), "w", clean["w"])
E                   ))
E                   try:
E                       structure = reconcile_box_body_structure_for_total_w_change(
E                           self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state(), clean["w"]
E                       )
E                   except Exception as exc:
E                       clean.pop("w", None)
E                       self._settings_values["w"] = previous_w
E                       self._phase6_input_snapshot["w"] = previous_w
E                       _phase6_box_structure_error(self, exc)
E                       self._phase6_settings_guard = True
E                       try:
E                           if hasattr(self, "v_w"):
E                               self.v_w.set(_setting_number_text(previous_w))
E                           var = getattr(self, "left_global_vars", {}).get("w")
E                           if var is not None:
E                               var.set(_setting_number_text(previous_w))
E                       finally:
E                           self._phase6_settings_guard = False
E                       if not clean:
E                           return {}
E                   else:
E                       structure = self.designer_workspace.set_box_body_structure_state(structure)
E                       self._phase6_input_snapshot["box_body_structure"] = deepcopy(structure)
E           
E               self._phase6_applying_settings = True
E               try:
E                   try:
E                       self._save_current_part()
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E               finally:
E                   self._phase6_applying_settings = False
E               self._settings_values.update(clean)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot.update(clean)
E               if "t" in clean:
E                   self.state.phase6_thickness = float(clean["t"])
E               if "ui_text_size" in clean:
E                   key = normalize_ui_text_size(clean["ui_text_size"])
E                   self._settings_values["ui_text_size"] = key
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot["ui_text_size"] = key
E                   if hasattr(self, "_ui_text_controller"):
E                       self._ui_text_controller.apply(key)
E                   self.state.ui_text_scale = getattr(self, "_ui_text_controller", None).factor if hasattr(self, "_ui_text_controller") else 1.0
E                   var = getattr(self, "ui_text_size_var", None)
E                   if var is not None and var.get() != ui_text_size_label(key):
E                       var.set(ui_text_size_label(key))
E               self._phase6_box_whd.update({
E                   key: _ui_len(clean[key]) for key in ("w", "h", "d") if key in clean
E               })
E               self._phase6_settings_guard = True
E               try:
E                   if "w" in clean: self.v_w.set(_setting_number_text(clean["w"]))
E                   if "h" in clean: self.v_h.set(_setting_number_text(clean["h"]))
E                   if "d" in clean: self.v_d.set(_setting_number_text(clean["d"]))
E                   for key, var in getattr(self, "left_global_vars", {}).items():
E                       if key not in clean:
E                           continue
E                       if key == "ui_text_size":
E                           var.set(ui_text_size_label(clean[key]))
E                       elif isinstance(var, original.tk.BooleanVar):
E                           var.set(bool(clean[key]))
E                       else:
E                           var.set(_setting_number_text(clean[key]))
E                   _phase6_refresh_profiles_from_settings(self)
E                   for key, var in getattr(self, "setting_vars", {}).items():
E                       if key in clean:
E                           if key == "ui_text_size":
E                               var.set(ui_text_size_label(clean[key]))
E                           elif isinstance(var, original.tk.BooleanVar):
E                               var.set(bool(clean[key]))
E                           else:
E                               var.set(_setting_number_text(clean[key]))
E               finally:
E                   self._phase6_settings_guard = False
E               legacy_job = getattr(self, "_job", None)
E               if legacy_job is not None:
E                   try:
E                       self.root.after_cancel(legacy_job)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   self._job = None
E               try:
E                   self.do_update()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               if (
E                   notify
E                   and not getattr(self, "_phase6_transactional_mode", False)
E                   and self._settings_change_callback is not None
E               ):
E                   self._settings_change_callback(dict(self._settings_values))
E               return clean
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_flush_pending_settings(self):
E               job = getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_debounce_job", None)
E               if job is not None:
E                   try:
E                       self.root.after_cancel(job)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   self._phase6_settings_debounce_job = None
E               pending = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_pending_settings", {}) or {})
E               self._phase6_pending_settings = {}
E               if not pending:
E                   return {}
E               return _phase6_apply_setting_updates(self, pending, notify=True)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_stage_setting_update(self, key, value):
E               # Equivalent values are not writes: no pending job, no revision, no echo.
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_destroying", False):
E                   return
E               if self._settings_values.get(key) == value:
E                   return
E               # State changes immediately; expensive profile rebuild + 3D/main-GUI redraw
E               # is coalesced until the operator pauses typing.
E               self._settings_values[key] = value
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot[key] = value
E               self._phase6_pending_settings[key] = value
E               job = getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_debounce_job", None)
E               if job is not None:
E                   try:
E                       self.root.after_cancel(job)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E               self._phase6_settings_debounce_job = self.root.after(150, self.flush_pending_settings)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_setting_var_changed(self, key, var, spec):
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_guard", False) or getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_rendering", False):
E                   return
E               raw = var.get()
E               if spec.kind == "bool":
E                   value = bool(raw)
E               elif spec.kind == "choice":
E                   value = normalize_ui_text_size(raw) if spec.key == "ui_text_size" else str(raw)
E               else:
E                   try:
E                       value = float(raw)
E                   except (TypeError, ValueError, original.tk.TclError):
E                       return
E               _phase6_stage_setting_update(self, key, value)
E           
E           
E           
E           _CORNER_KEYS = ("top_left", "top_right", "bottom_left", "bottom_right")
E           _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS = {"top": ("top_left", "top_right"), "bottom": ("bottom_left", "bottom_right")}
E           _CORNER_TYPE_IDS = tuple(item.value for item in EDITABLE_CORNER_TYPE_IDS)
E           _CORNER_TYPE_LABEL_BY_ID = {item.value: CORNER_TYPE_LABELS[item] for item in EDITABLE_CORNER_TYPE_IDS}
E           _CORNER_TYPE_BY_LABEL = {label: type_id for type_id, label in _CORNER_TYPE_LABEL_BY_ID.items()}
E           _CORNER_MODE_LABEL = {
E               CrossCornerMode.STANDARD: "標準",
E               CrossCornerMode.RETAIN: "單邊留肉",
E               CrossCornerMode.EXTRA_CUT: "多切",
E           }
E           _CORNER_MODE_BY_LABEL = {value: key for key, value in _CORNER_MODE_LABEL.items()}
E           _CORNER_DIRECTION_LABEL = {
E               CornerDirection.WIDTH: "寬",
E               CornerDirection.HEIGHT: "高",
E               CornerDirection.BOTH: "寬＋高",
E           }
E           _CORNER_DIRECTION_BY_LABEL = {value: key for key, value in _CORNER_DIRECTION_LABEL.items()}
E           
E           # 只供畫面顯示的固定製造 policy 摘要；真正幾何仍以引擎 policy 為準。
E           _FIXED_CORNER_SUMMARIES = {
E               "door": "十字截角｜單邊留肉 1T",
E               "base_plate": "十字截角｜標準",
E               "indicator_box": "十字截角｜單邊留肉 1T（固定）",
E               "indicator_door": "十字截角｜單邊留肉 1T（固定）",
E               "head": "上方：嵌入貼外型（貼外留肉 1T／嵌入留肉 0.5T／深度 2T）｜下方：十字截角 多切 0.5T（寬＋高）",
E               "tail": "上方：嵌入貼外型（貼外留肉 1T／嵌入留肉 0.5T／深度 2T）｜下方：十字截角 多切 0.5T（寬＋高）",
E           }
E           
E           
E           
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_ensure_corner_part(self, part_key):
E               state = self._phase6_corner_state.setdefault(str(part_key), {})
E               for corner_key in _CORNER_KEYS:
E                   state[corner_key] = _phase6_selection_to_raw(_phase6_selection_from_raw(state.get(corner_key)))
E               pairs = self._phase6_corner_pair_same.setdefault(str(part_key), {})
E               pairs.setdefault("top", True); pairs.setdefault("bottom", True)
E               return state, pairs
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_notify_corner_change(self):
E               """Corner edits are production edits: publish them to canonical state now."""
E               if _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None).get() if getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None) is not None else ""):
E                   self._corner_transaction_unknown_state = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_state)
E                   self._corner_transaction_unknown_pairs = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_pair_same)
E               _phase6_publish_live_state(self)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_type_editable(self, part_key):
E               return bool(getattr(self, "_corner_editable", False)) and part_key not in {"indicator_box", "indicator_door"}
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_parameters_unlockable(self, part_key):
E               return part_key not in {GLOBAL_CONTEXT, "box_body", "indicator_box", "indicator_door"}
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_parameters_unlocked(self, part_key):
E               # 3D 右上只有一個參數鎖。解鎖後目前板件的結構／進階／截角細部
E               # 一次展開；不再維護每個板件各自一顆細部參數鎖。
E               return bool(getattr(self, "_phase6_parameters_unlocked", False))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_parameters_editable(self, part_key):
E               return _phase6_corner_parameters_unlockable(self, part_key) and _phase6_corner_parameters_unlocked(self, part_key)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_parameter_summary(selection):
E               selection = normalize_corner_selection(selection)
E               if selection.type_id is CornerTypeId.CROSS:
E                   mode = _CORNER_MODE_LABEL[selection.cross_mode]
E                   if selection.cross_mode is CrossCornerMode.STANDARD:
E                       return mode
E                   direction = _CORNER_DIRECTION_LABEL[selection.direction]
E                   return f"{mode}｜{direction}｜{_setting_number_text(selection.amount_t)}T"
E               if selection.type_id is CornerTypeId.OVERLAY:
E                   return f"留肉（高）｜{_setting_number_text(selection.amount_t)}T"
E               if selection.type_id is CornerTypeId.INSERT:
E                   return f"多切（高）｜{_setting_number_text(selection.amount_t)}T"
E               return (
E                   f"貼外留肉 {_setting_number_text(selection.amount_t)}T｜"
E                   f"嵌入留肉 {_setting_number_text(selection.secondary_retain_t)}T｜"
E                   f"深度 {_setting_number_text(selection.secondary_depth_t)}T"
E               )
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_toggle_corner_parameter_lock(self):
E               return _phase6_toggle_parameter_panel(self)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_pair_var_changed(self, part_key, pair_key, var):
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_guard", False) or getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_rendering", False):
E                   return
E               if not _phase6_corner_parameters_editable(self, part_key):
E                   return
E               state, pairs = _phase6_ensure_corner_part(self, part_key)
E               enabled = bool(var.get())
E               pairs[pair_key] = enabled
E               if enabled:
E                   left_key, right_key = _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS[pair_key]
E                   state[right_key] = dict(state[left_key])
E               _phase6_notify_corner_change(self)
E               _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, part_key)
E               _phase6_render_settings_context(self, part_key)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_targets(pairs, target_key):
E               return _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS[target_key] if target_key in _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS else (target_key,)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_type_selected(self, part_key, target_key):
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_guard", False) or getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_rendering", False):
E                   return
E               if not _phase6_corner_type_editable(self, part_key):
E                   return
E               state, pairs = _phase6_ensure_corner_part(self, part_key)
E               var = self.corner_type_vars.get(target_key)
E               if var is None:
E                   return
E               type_id = _CORNER_TYPE_BY_LABEL.get(str(var.get()).strip())
E               if type_id is None:
E                   return
E               current_key = _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS[target_key][0] if target_key in _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS else target_key
E               current = _phase6_selection_from_raw(state[current_key])
E               selection = current if current.type_id.value == type_id else CornerTypeSelection(CornerTypeId(type_id))
E               raw = _phase6_selection_to_raw(selection)
E               for corner_key in _phase6_corner_targets(pairs, target_key):
E                   state[corner_key] = dict(raw)
E               _phase6_notify_corner_change(self)
E               _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, part_key)
E               _phase6_render_settings_context(self, part_key)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_mode_selected(self, part_key, target_key):
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_guard", False) or getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_rendering", False):
E                   return
E               if not _phase6_corner_parameters_editable(self, part_key):
E                   return
E               state, pairs = _phase6_ensure_corner_part(self, part_key)
E               current_key = _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS[target_key][0] if target_key in _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS else target_key
E               current = _phase6_selection_from_raw(state[current_key])
E               if current.type_id is not CornerTypeId.CROSS:
E                   return
E               var = self.corner_mode_vars.get(target_key)
E               mode = _CORNER_MODE_BY_LABEL.get(str(var.get()).strip()) if var is not None else None
E               if mode is None:
E                   return
E               selection = CornerTypeSelection(CornerTypeId.CROSS, cross_mode=mode)
E               raw = _phase6_selection_to_raw(selection)
E               for corner_key in _phase6_corner_targets(pairs, target_key):
E                   state[corner_key] = dict(raw)
E               _phase6_notify_corner_change(self)
E               _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, part_key)
E               _phase6_render_settings_context(self, part_key)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_target_var_changed(self, part_key, target_key):
E               """Commit semantic parameter widgets without exposing legacy X/Y rotation."""
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_guard", False) or getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_rendering", False):
E                   return
E               if not _phase6_corner_parameters_editable(self, part_key):
E                   return
E               state, pairs = _phase6_ensure_corner_part(self, part_key)
E               current_key = _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS[target_key][0] if target_key in _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS else target_key
E               current = _phase6_selection_from_raw(state[current_key])
E               try:
E                   amount_var = self.corner_amount_vars.get(target_key)
E                   amount = float(amount_var.get()) if amount_var is not None else current.amount_t
E                   if current.type_id is CornerTypeId.CROSS:
E                       mode_var = self.corner_mode_vars.get(target_key)
E                       mode = _CORNER_MODE_BY_LABEL.get(str(mode_var.get()).strip(), current.cross_mode) if mode_var is not None else current.cross_mode
E                       if mode is CrossCornerMode.STANDARD:
E                           selection = CornerTypeSelection(CornerTypeId.CROSS, cross_mode=mode)
E                       else:
E                           direction_var = self.corner_direction_vars.get(target_key)
E                           direction = _CORNER_DIRECTION_BY_LABEL.get(str(direction_var.get()).strip()) if direction_var is not None else current.direction
E                           selection = CornerTypeSelection(CornerTypeId.CROSS, cross_mode=mode, direction=direction, amount_t=amount)
E                   elif current.type_id is CornerTypeId.OVERLAY:
E                       selection = CornerTypeSelection(CornerTypeId.OVERLAY, amount_t=amount)
E                   elif current.type_id is CornerTypeId.INSERT:
E                       selection = CornerTypeSelection(CornerTypeId.INSERT, amount_t=amount)
E                   else:
E                       retain_var = self.corner_secondary_retain_vars.get(target_key)
E                       depth_var = self.corner_secondary_depth_vars.get(target_key)
E                       selection = CornerTypeSelection(
E                           CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY,
E                           amount_t=amount,
E                           secondary_retain_t=float(retain_var.get()),
E                           secondary_depth_t=float(depth_var.get()),
E                       )
E               except (TypeError, ValueError, original.tk.TclError):
E                   return
E               raw = _phase6_selection_to_raw(selection)
E               for corner_key in _phase6_corner_targets(pairs, target_key):
E                   state[corner_key] = dict(raw)
E               _phase6_notify_corner_change(self)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, value):
E               text = str(value or "").strip()
E               explicit = str(getattr(self, "_baseline_unknown_value", "") or "").strip()
E               return (
E                   text == CUSTOM_MODEL_NAME
E                   or text in LEGACY_CUSTOM_MODEL_NAMES
E                   or (explicit and text == explicit)
E               )
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_should_show_baseline_data(self, context, baseline_specs):
E               """Return whether the current settings page has meaningful baseline-only data."""
E               context = str(context or GLOBAL_CONTEXT)
E               if context == GLOBAL_CONTEXT:
E                   return False
E               model_var = getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None)
E               model = model_var.get() if model_var is not None else ""
E               if _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, model):
E                   return False
E               return bool(
E                   baseline_specs
E                   or context in {"box_body", "head", "tail", "door", "indicator_door"}
E               )
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_invalidate_corner_pages(self):
E               cache = getattr(self, "_settings_page_cache", None) or {}
E               for context in list(cache):
E                   if context == GLOBAL_CONTEXT:
E                       continue
E                   _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, context)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_known_model_corner_state(self):
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               model_var = getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None)
E               model = str(snapshot.get("model") or snapshot.get("cabinet_type") or "").strip()
E               try:
E                   live_model = str(model_var.get() or "").strip() if model_var is not None else ""
E               except Exception:
E                   live_model = ""
E               if live_model:
E                   model = live_model
E               try:
E                   from ae_engine.cabinet_types.registry import resolve_cabinet_type
E                   family = resolve_cabinet_type(model).canonical_name
E               except Exception:
E                   family = "金庫型"
E               return known_model_corner_state(
E                   getattr(self, "available_parts", KNOWN_PARTS),
E                   cabinet_family=family,
E               )
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_baseline_model_changed(self, *_args):
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_baseline_guard", False):
E                   return
E               self._phase6_corner_param_unlocked = {}
E               new_model = str(self.baseline_model_var.get() or "").strip()
E               old_model = str(getattr(self, "_phase6_baseline_last_model", "") or "").strip()
E               if _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, old_model):
E                   self._corner_transaction_unknown_state = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_state)
E                   self._corner_transaction_unknown_pairs = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_pair_same)
E           
E               editable = _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, new_model)
E               if not editable and new_model and new_model != old_model:
E                   fixed = _phase6_known_model_corner_state(self)
E                   self._phase6_corner_guard = True
E                   try:
E                       for part_key, corners in fixed.items():
E                           state = self._phase6_corner_state.setdefault(part_key, {})
E                           for corner_key, selection in corners.items():
E                               state[corner_key] = _phase6_selection_to_raw(selection)
E                           pairs = self._phase6_corner_pair_same.setdefault(part_key, {})
E                           pairs["top"] = True
E                           pairs["bottom"] = True
E                   finally:
E                       self._phase6_corner_guard = False
E               if editable and old_model and not _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, old_model):
E                   # 「自訂」不是另一套預設；它從目前已知固定板件的實際截角規則開始。
E                   fixed = _phase6_known_model_corner_state(self)
E                   self._phase6_corner_guard = True
E                   try:
E                       for part_key, corners in fixed.items():
E                           state = self._phase6_corner_state.setdefault(part_key, {})
E                           for corner_key, selection in corners.items():
E                               state[corner_key] = _phase6_selection_to_raw(selection)
E                           pairs = self._phase6_corner_pair_same.setdefault(part_key, {})
E                           pairs["top"] = True
E                           pairs["bottom"] = True
E                       self._corner_transaction_unknown_state = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_state)
E                       self._corner_transaction_unknown_pairs = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_pair_same)
E                   finally:
E                       self._phase6_corner_guard = False
E               self._corner_editable = editable
E           
E               # The baseline/model selector is the one Cabinet Family Source of Truth.
E               # Apply the family topology before refreshing the permanent structure row;
E               # otherwise switching 金庫型 -> 受電箱 inside the open 3D designer leaves
E               # the visible row and workspace on stale integral state.
E               old_snapshot_model = str((getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}).get("model") or old_model or "").strip()
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["model"] = new_model
E               try:
E                   if new_model == "受電箱":
E                       if old_snapshot_model != "受電箱":
E                           self._phase6_non_receiving_structure_state = self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state()
E                           # Selecting the receiving family is an explicit family change,
E                           # so apply its operator defaults to the live W/H/D/settings
E                           # instead of changing only the topology label.
E                           defaults = cabinet_family_policy.apply_fresh_family_defaults(
E                               self._phase6_input_snapshot, new_model
E                           )
E                           self._phase6_input_snapshot.update(defaults)
E                           family_values = {
E                               key: value for key, value in defaults.items()
E                               if key in getattr(self, "_settings_values", {})
E                           }
E                           _phase6_store_editor_values(self, family_values, notify=True)
E                           # The currently active BoxBody editor still owns legacy v_w/v_h/v_d
E                           # and Fold Chain controls. Rebase those immediately as part of
E                           # the same family transaction; otherwise the next part switch
E                           # saves the stale vault 400/600/250 values back over the new
E                           # receiving defaults.
E                           self.state.w = original.get_int(defaults["w"])
E                           self.state.h = original.get_int(defaults["h"])
E                           self.state.d = original.get_int(defaults["d"])
E                           self.v_w.set(str(self.state.w))
E                           self.v_h.set(str(self.state.h))
E                           self.v_d.set(str(self.state.d))
E                           self._phase6_last_w = self.state.w
E                           self._phase6_last_d = self.state.d
E                           _phase6_refresh_profiles_from_settings(self)
E                           # 2026-09-03 receiving contract: a *fresh* family switch
E                           # materializes 包覆貼外 as the canonical intent. Saved project
E                           # payloads are restored by the project-load path and do not use
E                           # this fresh-default transaction as migration truth.
E                           fresh_intent = cabinet_family_policy.fresh_assembly_intent(new_model)
E                           self._phase6_assembly_type = fresh_intent
E                           self._phase6_input_snapshot["assembly_type"] = fresh_intent
E                           _phase6_sync_joint_state_for_intent(self, fresh_intent)
E                           assembly_var = getattr(self, "assembly_type_var", None)
E                           if assembly_var is not None:
E                               assembly_var.set(ASSEMBLY_TYPE_LABELS[fresh_intent])
E                           self._phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state = normalize_endcap_bottom_wrap_state(
E                               {"model": new_model}
E                           )
E                           self._phase6_input_snapshot["endcap_bottom_wrap"] = deepcopy(
E                               self._phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state
E                           )
E                       structure = cabinet_family_policy.resolve_box_body_structure_state(
E                           new_model, self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state()
E                       )
E                       self.designer_workspace.set_box_body_structure_state(structure)
E                       self._phase6_input_snapshot["box_body_structure"] = deepcopy(structure)
E                   elif old_snapshot_model == "受電箱":
E                       previous = getattr(self, "_phase6_non_receiving_structure_state", None)
E                       if previous:
E                           self.designer_workspace.set_box_body_structure_state(previous)
E                           self._phase6_input_snapshot["box_body_structure"] = deepcopy(previous)
E               except Exception:
E                   # Rendering/validation will surface a real structure error; do not invent
E                   # a second fallback structure here.
E                   pass
E               self._phase6_baseline_last_model = new_model
E               _phase6_sync_authoritative_derived_parts(self)
E               _phase6_refresh_persistent_structure_controls(self)
E           
E               # 快取頁面依 _corner_editable 決定是否建立可編輯截角控制項。
E               # Baseline changes are explicit user actions, so rebuilding those pages here
E               # is correct; ordinary part switching still reuses the cache.
E               _phase6_invalidate_corner_pages(self)
E               if hasattr(self, "settings_center") and getattr(self, "active_part_key", None) is not None:
E                   _phase6_render_settings_context(self, getattr(self, "settings_context", self.active_part_key))
E               if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                   self.settings_status_var.set(
E                       "自訂：沿用目前資料並即時同步主畫面"
E                       if editable else "已選基準型號；截角修改即時同步主畫面"
E                   )
E               submit = getattr(self, "submit_update_intent", None)
E               if callable(submit):
E                   submit("baseline", commit=True)
E               else:
E                   _phase6_publish_live_state(self, force=True)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_collect_workspace_state(self):
E               active = self.designer_workspace.active_part
E               live_active_profiles = None
E               # The visible fold editor is live canonical state.  The adapter only supplies
E               # that editor payload; DesignerWorkspace owns how it projects the shared schema.
E               if active and active != "box_body":
E                   profiles = getattr(self.state, "profiles", {}) or {}
E                   live_x = profiles.get("X", ()) or ()
E                   live_y = profiles.get("Y", ()) or ()
E                   if live_x or live_y:
E                       live_active_profiles = {
E                           "X": clone_profile(live_x),
E                           "Y": clone_profile(live_y),
E                       }
E               owner = self.designer_workspace.export_shared_snapshot(
E                   live_active_profiles=live_active_profiles
E               )
E               return {
E                   "assembly_type": assembly_intent_value(getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY)),
E                   "endcap_fw": deepcopy(
E                       getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", None)
E                       or normalize_endcap_fw_state(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E                   ),
E                   "box_body_profile": clone_profile(self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])),
E                   **owner,
E               }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_relief_polygon_coords(geometry):
E               if geometry is None or getattr(geometry, "is_empty", True):
E                   return []
E               if getattr(geometry, "geom_type", "") == "Polygon":
E                   polygons = [geometry]
E               else:
E                   polygons = [
E                       geom for geom in getattr(geometry, "geoms", ())
E                       if getattr(geom, "geom_type", "") == "Polygon" and float(geom.area) > 1e-9
E                   ]
E               out = []
E               for polygon in polygons:
E                   coords = list(polygon.exterior.coords)
E                   if coords and coords[0] == coords[-1]:
E                       coords = coords[:-1]
E                   if len(coords) >= 3:
E                       out.append([[float(x), float(y)] for x, y in coords])
E               return out
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_relief_profile_fingerprint(profile):
E               rows = []
E               for raw in list(profile or ()):
E                   row = dict(raw or {})
E                   try:
E                       length = round(float(row.get("len", row.get("length", 0.0)) or 0.0), 6)
E                   except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                       length = 0.0
E                   angle = row.get("angle")
E                   try:
E                       angle = None if angle is None else round(float(angle), 6)
E                   except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                       angle = None
E                   rows.append((
E                       str(row.get("phase6_key") or ""), length, angle,
E                       str(row.get("core") or ""),
E                   ))
E               return tuple(rows)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_current_relief_source_signature(self, required):
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import RELIEF_CONTRACT_VERSION
E           
E               source = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               source.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               source.update(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_whd", {}) or {}))
E               source["assembly_type"] = assembly_intent_value(getattr(
E                   self, "_phase6_assembly_type", CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY
E               ))
E               scalar_keys = (
E                   "w", "h", "d", "t", "fw", "zl1", "zl2", "zr1", "zr2",
E                   "yl1", "yr1", "ytop1", "ybottom1", "assembly_type",
E               )
E               result = {key: deepcopy(source.get(key)) for key in scalar_keys if key in source}
E               box_profile = clone_profile(
E                   (getattr(self.state, "profiles_vault", {}) or {}).get("箱身", ()) or ()
E               )
E               formed_left, formed_right = formed_box_body_fw_widths(
E                   box_profile, float(source.get("t", 0.0) or 0.0)
E               )
E               from ae_engine.assembly_joint import resolved_joint_graph_fingerprint
E               graph_snapshot = migrate_legacy_snapshot_joints(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}))
E               structure_state = deepcopy(self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state() or {})
E               import hashlib as _hashlib, json as _json
E               structure_payload = _json.dumps(structure_state, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str)
E               family_name = str(source.get("model") or source.get("cabinet_type") or "")
E               result.update({
E                   "relief_contract_version": RELIEF_CONTRACT_VERSION,
E                   "joint_graph_fingerprint": resolved_joint_graph_fingerprint(graph_snapshot),
E                   "family_structure_fingerprint": _hashlib.sha256(structure_payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
E                   "cabinet_family": family_name,
E                   "box_body_formed_fw": {
E                       "left": None if formed_left is None else float(formed_left),
E                       "right": None if formed_right is None else float(formed_right),
E                   },
E                   "box_body_profile": box_profile,
E                   "part_profiles": {
E                       key: deepcopy(self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(key, {}) or {})
E                       for key in required
E                   },
E               })
E               return result
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_relief_source_matches_current(saved_source, current_source, required):
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import RELIEF_CONTRACT_VERSION
E           
E               saved = dict(saved_source or {})
E               current = dict(current_source or {})
E               if not saved:
E                   return False
E               try:
E                   if int(saved.get("relief_contract_version", 0) or 0) != RELIEF_CONTRACT_VERSION:
E                       return False
E                   if int(current.get("relief_contract_version", 0) or 0) != RELIEF_CONTRACT_VERSION:
E                       return False
E               except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                   return False
E               saved_formed = dict(saved.get("box_body_formed_fw") or {})
E               current_formed = dict(current.get("box_body_formed_fw") or {})
E               for side in ("left", "right"):
E                   try:
E                       if abs(float(saved_formed[side]) - float(current_formed[side])) > 1e-6:
E                           return False
E                   except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
E                       return False
E               for key in (
E                   "w", "h", "d", "t", "fw", "zl1", "zl2", "zr1", "zr2",
E                   "yl1", "yr1", "ytop1", "ybottom1",
E               ):
E                   if key not in saved or key not in current:
E                       continue
E                   try:
E                       if abs(float(saved[key]) - float(current[key])) > 1e-6:
E                           return False
E                   except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                       return False
E               saved_graph = str(saved.get("joint_graph_fingerprint") or "")
E               current_graph = str(current.get("joint_graph_fingerprint") or "")
E               if not saved_graph or not current_graph or saved_graph != current_graph:
E                   return False
E               saved_structure = str(saved.get("family_structure_fingerprint") or "")
E               current_structure = str(current.get("family_structure_fingerprint") or "")
E               if not saved_structure or not current_structure or saved_structure != current_structure:
E                   return False
E               if str(saved.get("cabinet_family") or "") != str(current.get("cabinet_family") or ""):
E                   return False
E               # A matching geometry signature is insufficient when the Certified Rule
E               # itself was revised or withdrawn.  Replay only active rule revisions.
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import certified_rule_revision_exists
E               saved_rules = dict(saved.get("registry_rules") or {})
E               for part_key in required:
E                   rule = dict(saved_rules.get(str(part_key)) or {})
E                   rule_id = str(rule.get("rule_id") or "")
E                   if not rule_id:
E                       continue
E                   try:
E                       revision = int(rule.get("revision", 0) or 0)
E                   except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                       return False
E                   if revision <= 0 or not certified_rule_revision_exists(rule_id, revision):
E                       return False
E               if "box_body_profile" in saved:
E                   if _phase6_relief_profile_fingerprint(saved.get("box_body_profile")) != _phase6_relief_profile_fingerprint(current.get("box_body_profile")):
E                       return False
E               saved_parts = dict(saved.get("part_profiles") or {})
E               current_parts = dict(current.get("part_profiles") or {})
E               for key in required:
E                   saved_axes = dict(saved_parts.get(key) or {})
E                   current_axes = dict(current_parts.get(key) or {})
E                   for axis in ("X", "Y"):
E                       if axis not in saved_axes:
E                           continue
E                       if _phase6_relief_profile_fingerprint(saved_axes.get(axis)) != _phase6_relief_profile_fingerprint(current_axes.get(axis)):
E                           return False
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_serialize_assembly_relief_state(self):
E               """Serialize only an atomic verified EndCap relief transaction.
E           
E               If a solve is partial/failed, preserve the previously committed relief
E               state (if any).  Never manufacture a Head-new/Tail-old state.
E               """
E               enabled_var = getattr(self, "assembly_ignore_fixed_corner_var", None)
E               fallback_enabled = bool(enabled_var.get()) if enabled_var is not None else True
E               solutions = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_last_relief_solutions", {}) or {})
E               available = set(getattr(getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None), "available_parts", ()) or ())
E               required = [key for key in ("head", "tail") if key in available]
E               atomic_committable = bool(required) and all(
E                   key in solutions and _phase6_solution_is_committable(solutions[key])
E                   for key in required
E               )
E               source_signature = dict(_phase6_current_relief_source_signature(self, required) or {})
E               if not atomic_committable:
E                   prior = deepcopy((getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}).get("assembly_relief") or {})
E                   if prior and _phase6_relief_source_matches_current(prior.get("source"), source_signature, required):
E                       return prior
E                   return {
E                       "enabled": False,
E                       "fallback_enabled": fallback_enabled,
E                       "clearance": _phase6_assembly_relief_clearance(self),
E                       "source": {},
E                       "parts": {},
E                   }
E               source_signature["registry_rules"] = {
E                   key: {
E                       "rule_id": str(getattr(solutions[key], "rule_id", "") or ""),
E                       "revision": int(getattr(solutions[key], "rule_revision", 0) or 0),
E                   }
E                   for key in required
E               }
E               parts = {}
E               for key in required:
E                   solution = solutions[key]
E                   measurements = []
E                   for item in tuple(getattr(solution, "corner_reliefs", ()) or ()):
E                       m = getattr(item, "measurement", None)
E                       if m is None:
E                           continue
E                       measurements.append({
E                           "corner_name": str(getattr(m, "corner_name", getattr(item, "corner_name", ""))),
E                           "primary_u": float(m.primary_u),
E                           "primary_v": float(m.primary_v),
E                           "secondary_u": None if m.secondary_u is None else float(m.secondary_u),
E                           "secondary_depth": None if m.secondary_depth is None else float(m.secondary_depth),
E                           "clearance_a": float(getattr(m, "clearance_a", 0.0)),
E                       })
E                   parts[key] = {
E                       "verified": bool(getattr(solution, "verified", False)),
E                       "canonical_accepted": True,
E                       "trust_level": str(getattr(solution, "trust_level", "PROVISIONAL_3D") or "PROVISIONAL_3D"),
E                       "rule_id": getattr(solution, "rule_id", None),
E                       "rule_revision": getattr(solution, "rule_revision", None),
E                       "joint_signature": [dict(item) for item in tuple(getattr(solution, "joint_signature", ()) or ())],
E                       "shadow_validation": deepcopy(getattr(solution, "shadow_validation", None)),
E                       "cuts": _phase6_relief_polygon_coords(getattr(solution, "cut_polygon_2d", None)),
E                       "measurements": measurements,
E                   }
E               return {
E                   "enabled": True,
E                   "fallback_enabled": fallback_enabled,
E                   "clearance": _phase6_assembly_relief_clearance(self),
E                   "source": source_signature,
E                   "parts": parts,
E               }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_transaction_payload(self):
E               return {
E                   "model": str(self.baseline_model_var.get() or "").strip(),
E                   "settings": dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {})),
E                   "assembly_type": assembly_intent_value(getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY)),
E                   "endcap_fw": deepcopy(
E                       getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", None)
E                       or normalize_endcap_fw_state(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E                   ),
E                   "corner_state": deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {})),
E                   "corner_pair_same": deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_pair_same", {})),
E                   "active_part": getattr(self, "active_part_key", None),
E                   "assembly_relief": _phase6_serialize_assembly_relief_state(self),
E                   "workspace": _phase6_collect_workspace_state(self),
E               }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_publish_live_state(self, *, force=False):
E               """Publish one revision only when authoritative live state actually changes."""
E               callback = getattr(self, "_live_sync_callback", None)
E               if (not callable(callback) or getattr(self, "_phase6_live_sync_guard", False)
E                       or getattr(self, "_phase6_initializing", False)
E                       or not getattr(self, "_phase6_sync_ready", False)
E                       or not hasattr(self, "baseline_model_var")
E                       or not hasattr(self, "designer_workspace")):
E                   return False
E               state = _phase6_corner_transaction_payload(self)
E               fingerprint = stable_fingerprint(state)
E           
E               # Initialization can legitimately solve/normalize a verified assembly relief
E               # while live publication is suppressed.  In that case the designer's
E               # last-live fingerprint already describes the canonical state, but the host
E               # snapshot may still carry the older relief contract.  ``force`` never
E               # bypasses anti-echo for an equivalent host; it only repairs that proven
E               # host/canonical relief delta.
E               input_snapshot = getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E               host_relief_present = isinstance(input_snapshot, Mapping) and "assembly_relief" in input_snapshot
E               host_relief = deepcopy(input_snapshot.get("assembly_relief") or {}) if host_relief_present else {}
E               canonical_relief = deepcopy(state.get("assembly_relief") or {})
E               force_host_relief_sync = bool(
E                   force
E                   and host_relief_present
E                   and stable_fingerprint(host_relief) != stable_fingerprint(canonical_relief)
E               )
E           
E               if (fingerprint == getattr(self, "_phase6_last_live_fingerprint", None)
E                       and not force_host_relief_sync):
E                   return False
E               previous = getattr(self, "_phase6_last_live_state", None) or {}
E               if force_host_relief_sync:
E                   previous = deepcopy(state)
E                   previous["assembly_relief"] = host_relief
E               delta = mapping_delta(previous, state)
E               if not delta and previous:
E                   return False
E               revision = int(getattr(self, "_phase6_sync_revision", 0) or 0) + 1
E               transaction_id = (
E                   str(getattr(self, "_phase6_active_transaction_id", "") or "").strip()
E                   or f"fold_designer:{revision}"
E               )
E               payload = deepcopy(state)
E               payload.update({
E                   "origin": "fold_designer",
E                   "revision": revision,
E                   "transaction_id": transaction_id,
E                   "delta": deepcopy(delta),
E                   "fingerprint": fingerprint,
E               })
E               self._phase6_live_sync_guard = True
E               try:
E                   callback(deepcopy(payload))
E                   self._phase6_sync_revision = revision
E                   self._phase6_last_live_state = deepcopy(state)
E                   self._phase6_last_live_fingerprint = fingerprint
E                   self._phase6_last_live_payload = deepcopy(payload)
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot["assembly_relief"] = deepcopy(state.get("assembly_relief") or {})
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                       self.settings_status_var.set(f"即時同步失敗：{exc}")
E                   return False
E               finally:
E                   self._phase6_live_sync_guard = False
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_diagnostic_snapshot(self):
E               """Capture the exact draft + Final Part Geometry currently used by 3D."""
E               model_var = getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None)
E               model = str(
E                   model_var.get() if model_var is not None
E                   else getattr(self, "_phase6_baseline_initial_model", "") or ""
E               ).strip()
E               active_part = self.designer_workspace.active_part
E               payload = _phase6_scene_query_payload(self) if active_part else {}
E               settings = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               settings.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               settings.update(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_whd", {}) or {}))
E               context = DiagnosticSnapshotContext(
E                   model=model,
E                   active_part=active_part,
E                   settings=settings,
E                   corner_state=getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {}) or {},
E                   corner_pair_same=getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_pair_same", {}) or {},
E                   workspace=_phase6_collect_workspace_state(self),
E                   active_part_payload=payload,
E               )
E               provider = (lambda: _phase6_query_final_render_data(self)) if active_part else None
E               return build_active_diagnostic_snapshot(context, provider)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_project_snapshot(self):
E               """Capture one complete, reloadable Phase6 workspace plus all-part diagnostics."""
E               from phase6_project_file import PROJECT_SCHEMA
E           
E               try:
E                   self._save_current_part(notify=False)
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               model_var = getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None)
E               model = str(
E                   model_var.get() if model_var is not None
E                   else getattr(self, "_phase6_baseline_initial_model", "") or ""
