E               ).strip()
E               owner_workspace = self.designer_workspace.snapshot()
E               workspace = _phase6_collect_workspace_state(self)
E               snapshot = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               snapshot.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               snapshot.update(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_whd", {}) or {}))
E               snapshot.update({
E                   "model": model,
E                   "assembly_type": assembly_intent_value(getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY)),
E                   "endcap_fw": deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", normalize_endcap_fw_state(snapshot))),
E                   "settings": deepcopy(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}),
E                   "corner_state": deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {}) or {}),
E                   "corner_pair_same": deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_pair_same", {}) or {}),
E                   "existing_parts": list(owner_workspace["existing_parts"]),
E                   "active_part": owner_workspace["active_part"],
E                   "workspace": workspace,
E                   "box_body_profile": clone_profile(workspace.get("box_body_profile", [])),
E                   "part_profiles": deepcopy(workspace.get("part_profiles", {})),
E                   "part_features": deepcopy(owner_workspace["part_features"]),
E                   "part_face_features": deepcopy(owner_workspace["part_face_features"]),
E                   "assembly_relief": _phase6_serialize_assembly_relief_state(self),
E               })
E           
E               callback = getattr(self, "_scene_query_callback", None)
E               final_geometry = collect_final_geometry_diagnostics(
E                   list(owner_workspace["existing_parts"]),
E                   lambda key: _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, key),
E                   callback if callable(callback) else None,
E               )
E           
E               return {
E                   "schema": PROJECT_SCHEMA,
E                   "saved_at": datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
E                   "model": model,
E                   "snapshot": snapshot,
E                   "final_geometry": final_geometry,
E               }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_save_diagnostic_file(self):
E               """儲存 staged 折彎診斷工作區，但不提交 main GUI transaction。"""
E               from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox
E           
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_pending_settings", None):
E                   try:
E                       self.flush_pending_settings()
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E               try:
E                   self._save_current_part(notify=False)
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E               model_var = getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None)
E               model = str(model_var.get() if model_var is not None else "").strip() or "自訂"
E               part = PART_LABELS.get(getattr(self, "active_part_key", None), "工作區")
E               safe_model = "".join(ch if ch not in '\\/:*?"<>|' else "_" for ch in model)
E               initial = f"Phase6折彎_{safe_model}_{part}.json"
E               path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
E                   parent=self.root,
E                   title="存檔：折彎診斷工作檔",
E                   defaultextension=".json",
E                   filetypes=[("Phase6 折彎診斷 JSON", "*.json"), ("所有檔案", "*.*")],
E                   initialfile=initial,
E               )
E               if not path:
E                   return None
E               try:
E                   target = _phase6_write_diagnostic_json(path, _phase6_build_diagnostic_snapshot(self))
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                       self.settings_status_var.set(f"已存檔：{target.name}（未提交主畫面）")
E                   return str(target)
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   messagebox.showerror("存檔失敗", f"無法儲存折彎診斷檔：\n{exc}", parent=self.root)
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_load_project_file(self):
E               """透過 parent GUI 載入 .p6fold，並建立新的 3D 工作區。"""
E               from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox
E               from phase6_project_file import PROJECT_EXTENSION, read_project
E           
E               path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
E                   parent=self.root,
E                   title="讀檔：Phase6 折彎專案",
E                   filetypes=[("Phase6 折彎專案", f"*{PROJECT_EXTENSION}"), ("所有檔案", "*.*")],
E               )
E               if not path:
E                   return None
E           
E               callback = getattr(self, "_project_load_callback", None)
E               if not callable(callback):
E                   messagebox.showerror("讀檔失敗", "目前工作區沒有可用的專案讀檔入口。", parent=self.root)
E                   return None
E           
E               try:
E                   # 在目前交易式 3D 視窗被替換前先驗證檔案。
E                   # authoritative restore 由 parent callback 執行，確保主 GUI、2D、
E                   # 板件存在狀態、3D 與輸出鏈都從同一來源重新載入。
E                   read_project(path)
E                   callback(str(path))
E                   return str(path)
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   try:
E                       messagebox.showerror("讀檔失敗", f"無法讀取 Phase6 專案：\n{exc}", parent=self.root)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_save_project_file(self, *, save_as=False):
E               """儲存 Phase6 專案；連接 main GUI 時一律委派 committed ownership。"""
E               callback = getattr(self, "_project_save_callback", None)
E               if callable(callback):
E                   return callback(
E                       save_as=bool(save_as),
E                       active_part=getattr(self, "active_part_key", None),
E                   )
E           
E               from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox
E               from phase6_project_file import PROJECT_EXTENSION, write_project
E           
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_pending_settings", None):
E                   try:
E                       self.flush_pending_settings()
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E           
E               current = str(getattr(self, "_phase6_current_project_path", "") or "").strip()
E               path = current
E               if save_as or not path:
E                   model_var = getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None)
E                   model = str(model_var.get() if model_var is not None else "").strip() or "自訂"
E                   safe_model = "".join(ch if ch not in '\\/:*?"<>|' else "_" for ch in model)
E                   initial = Path(current).name if current else f"{safe_model}{PROJECT_EXTENSION}"
E                   path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
E                       parent=self.root,
E                       title="另存新檔：Phase6 專案" if save_as else "儲存專案：Phase6",
E                       defaultextension=PROJECT_EXTENSION,
E                       filetypes=[("Phase6 折彎專案", f"*{PROJECT_EXTENSION}"), ("所有檔案", "*.*")],
E                       initialfile=initial,
E                   )
E                   if not path:
E                       return None
E           
E               try:
E                   payload = _phase6_build_project_snapshot(self)
E                   # Runtime-only host path must never become mechanical project data.
E                   payload.get("snapshot", {}).pop("_runtime_project_path", None)
E                   target = write_project(path, payload)
E                   self._phase6_current_project_path = str(target)
E                   callback = getattr(self, "_project_path_change_callback", None)
E                   if callable(callback):
E                       callback(str(target))
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                       self.settings_status_var.set(f"已存專案：{target.name}（全部板件）")
E                   return str(target)
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   messagebox.showerror("存檔失敗", f"無法儲存 Phase6 專案：\n{exc}", parent=self.root)
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_save_project_file_as(self):
E               return _phase6_save_project_file(self, save_as=True)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_confirm_corner_transaction(self):
E               self.flush_pending_settings()
E               # Capture the visible fold editor only after a part has actually been selected.
E               # Confirming directly from the landing view must not fabricate a Box Body edit.
E               if getattr(self, "active_part_key", None) is not None:
E                   self._save_current_part(notify=False)
E               if _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, self.baseline_model_var.get()):
E                   self._corner_transaction_unknown_state = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_state)
E                   self._corner_transaction_unknown_pairs = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_pair_same)
E               callback = getattr(self, "_transaction_confirm_callback", None)
E               if callback is None:
E                   return False
E               try:
E                   callback(_phase6_corner_transaction_payload(self))
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                       self.settings_status_var.set(f"確定失敗：{exc}")
E                   return False
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_cancel_corner_transaction(self):
E               callback = getattr(self, "_transaction_cancel_callback", None)
E               if callback is None:
E                   return False
E               try:
E                   callback()
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                       self.settings_status_var.set(f"取消失敗：{exc}")
E                   return False
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_export_workspace_state_if_dirty(self):
E               """只在折彎工作區結構真的改變時保存結構。
E           
E               一般數值設定本來就會即時同步；這個輕量快照只負責讓自訂段落拓撲與
E               新增板件在關閉 3D 視窗後仍保留。它不包含也不提交基準檔／截角交易資料，
E               也不執行舊版完整匯出 adapter。
E               """
E               if not bool(getattr(self, "_phase6_workspace_dirty", False)):
E                   return None
E               pending = getattr(self, "_job", None)
E               if pending:
E                   try:
E                       self.root.after_cancel(pending)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   self._job = None
E               self._save_current_part(notify=False)
E               owner = self.designer_workspace.snapshot()
E               result = {
E                   "box_body_profile": clone_profile(self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])),
E                   "existing_parts": list(owner["existing_parts"]),
E                   "active_part": owner["active_part"],
E                   "part_profiles": deepcopy(owner["part_profiles"]),
E                   "box_body_structure": deepcopy(owner.get("box_body_structure") or self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state()),
E               }
E               self.designer_workspace.mark_clean()
E               return result
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_refresh_active_endcap_from_linked(self, linked):
E               key = str(self.designer_workspace.active_part or "")
E               if key not in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS or key not in linked:
E                   return
E               profiles = linked[key]
E               self.state.profiles = {
E                   "X": clone_profile(profiles.get("X", ())),
E                   "Y": clone_profile(profiles.get("Y", ())),
E               }
E               try:
E                   self.bend_ui.rebuild_tabs()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_commit_endcap_fw_state(self):
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(self._phase6_endcap_fw_state)
E               self.designer_workspace.mark_dirty()
E               linked = _phase6_rebuild_linked_endcaps(self)
E               _phase6_refresh_active_endcap_from_linked(self, linked)
E               try:
E                   self.do_update()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               return linked
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_set_endcap_fw_follow(self, part_key, follow_box):
E               part_key = str(part_key)
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               snapshot.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               box_fw = _num(snapshot.get("fw", 25), 25)
E               set_endcap_fw_follow(self._phase6_endcap_fw_state, part_key, bool(follow_box), box_fw=box_fw)
E               return _phase6_commit_endcap_fw_state(self)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_set_endcap_fw_override(self, part_key, value):
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               snapshot.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               commit_endcap_fw(
E                   self._phase6_endcap_fw_state, str(part_key), _num(value),
E                   box_fw=_num(snapshot.get("fw", 25), 25),
E               )
E               return _phase6_commit_endcap_fw_state(self)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_endcap_fw_follow_selected(self, part_key, follow_var, value_var, value_widget):
E               follow = bool(follow_var.get())
E               _phase6_set_endcap_fw_follow(self, part_key, follow)
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               snapshot.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               effective = resolve_endcap_fw(snapshot, part_key, state=self._phase6_endcap_fw_state)
E               value_var.set(_setting_number_text(effective))
E               try:
E                   value_widget.configure(state=("disabled" if follow else "normal"))
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_endcap_fw_value_selected(self, part_key, value_var):
E               try:
E                   value = float(value_var.get())
E               except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                   return
E               _phase6_set_endcap_fw_override(self, part_key, value)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_endcap_fw_settings(self, parent, part_key, start_row):
E               part_key = str(part_key)
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               snapshot.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               state = self._phase6_endcap_fw_state.setdefault(
E                   part_key, {"follow_box": True, "value": _num(snapshot.get("fw", 25), 25)}
E               )
E               follow = bool(state.get("follow_box", True))
E               effective = resolve_endcap_fw(snapshot, part_key, state=self._phase6_endcap_fw_state)
E           
E               box = original.ttk.LabelFrame(parent, text="邊框寬度 FW", padding=4)
E               box.grid(row=start_row, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=(6, 2))
E               follow_var = original.tk.BooleanVar(value=follow)
E               value_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_setting_number_text(effective))
E               check = original.ttk.Checkbutton(box, text="跟隨箱身 FW", variable=follow_var)
E               check.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 8))
E               entry = original.ttk.Entry(box, textvariable=value_var, width=9, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E               entry.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 6))
E               entry.configure(state="normal")
E               check.configure(state="disabled")
E               entry.bind("<Return>", lambda _e: _phase6_on_endcap_fw_value_selected(self, part_key, value_var))
E               entry.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda _e: _phase6_on_endcap_fw_value_selected(self, part_key, value_var))
E               original.ttk.Label(box, text="直接修改：先改一端會帶另一端；再改另一端後各自獨立").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E               return start_row + 1, follow_var, value_var, entry
E           
E           
E           _BOX_STRUCTURE_LABELS = {
E               BoxBodyStructureType.INTEGRAL: "一體成型",
E               BoxBodyStructureType.TWO_PIECE_W_SPLIT: "二件式（W 二分）",
E               BoxBodyStructureType.THREE_PIECE_W_SPLIT: "三件式（W 三分）",
E               BoxBodyStructureType.THREE_PIECE_SIDE_BACK_SPLIT: "三件式（側背分離）",
E           }
E           _BOX_STRUCTURE_LABEL_TO_TYPE = {label: key for key, label in _BOX_STRUCTURE_LABELS.items()}
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_box_structure_state(self):
E               return normalize_box_body_structure_state(self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state())
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_commit_box_structure_state(self, state, *, rebuild=False):
E               state = self.designer_workspace.set_box_body_structure_state(state)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["box_body_structure"] = deepcopy(state)
E               # 結構型態/尺寸是 manufacturing geometry signature 的一部分。切換後不可
E               # 繼續重用上一型態的 resolved geometry，否則 3D 會停在舊結構。
E               self._phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_geometry = None
E               self._phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_signature = None
E               self.designer_workspace.mark_dirty()
E               if rebuild:
E                   def refresh():
E                       try:
E                           _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, "box_body")
E                           _phase6_render_settings_context(self, "box_body")
E                       except Exception:
E                           pass
E                   root = getattr(self, "root", None)
E                   if root is not None and hasattr(root, "after_idle"):
E                       root.after_idle(refresh)
E                   else:
E                       refresh()
E               try:
E                   self.do_update()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               return state
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_box_structure_error(self, exc):
E               var = getattr(self, "settings_status_var", None)
E               if var is not None:
E                   var.set(f"箱身結構設定無效：{exc}")
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_toggle_box_structure_lock(self):
E               state = _phase6_box_structure_state(self)
E               _phase6_commit_box_structure_state(self, set_structure_locked(state, not bool(state.get("locked", True))), rebuild=True)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_select_box_structure_type(self, var):
E               type_id = _BOX_STRUCTURE_LABEL_TO_TYPE.get(str(var.get()).strip())
E               if type_id is None:
E                   return
E               state = _phase6_box_structure_state(self)
E               # 受電箱的側背分離是 Family 固定拓撲；選單仍常駐顯示，但不可切換。
E               if cabinet_family_policy.family_fixes_box_body_structure(
E                   getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E               ):
E                   try:
E                       var.set(_BOX_STRUCTURE_LABELS[BoxBodyStructureType(state["active_type"])])
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   return
E               try:
E                   next_state = activate_structure_with_defaults(
E                       state, type_id, _phase6_box_structure_w(self)
E                   )
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   _phase6_box_structure_error(self, exc)
E                   return
E               _phase6_commit_box_structure_state(self, next_state, rebuild=True)
E               _phase6_refresh_persistent_structure_controls(self)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_box_structure_w(self):
E               values = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_whd", {}) or {})
E               values.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               return float(values.get("w", getattr(getattr(self, "state", None), "w", 500.0)))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_box_structure_d(self):
E               values = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_whd", {}) or {})
E               values.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               return float(values.get("d", getattr(getattr(self, "state", None), "d", 200.0)))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_toggle_structure_advanced(self, type_id):
E               flags = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_structure_advanced_open", {}) or {})
E               key = BoxBodyStructureType(type_id).value
E               flags[key] = not bool(flags.get(key, False))
E               self._phase6_box_structure_advanced_open = flags
E               _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, "box_body")
E               _phase6_render_settings_context(self, "box_body")
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_apply_box_structure_numeric(self, type_id, field, var):
E               state = _phase6_box_structure_state(self)
E               try:
E                   raw = str(var.get()).strip()
E                   value = float(raw)
E                   total_w = _phase6_box_structure_w(self)
E                   if type_id is BoxBodyStructureType.TWO_PIECE_W_SPLIT and field in {"left", "right"}:
E                       state = set_two_piece_width(state, total_w, field, value)
E                   elif type_id is BoxBodyStructureType.THREE_PIECE_W_SPLIT and field in {"left", "middle", "right"}:
E                       state = set_three_piece_width(state, total_w, field, value)
E                   elif field == "seam_bend":
E                       state = set_join_seam_bend(state, type_id, value)
E                   elif type_id is BoxBodyStructureType.THREE_PIECE_SIDE_BACK_SPLIT and field == "side_rear_bend":
E                       state = set_side_back_geometry(state, side_rear_bend=value)
E                   elif type_id is BoxBodyStructureType.THREE_PIECE_SIDE_BACK_SPLIT and field == "back_width_comp_t":
E                       state = set_side_back_geometry(state, back_width_comp_t=value)
E                   else:
E                       if field in {"endcap_extra_relief", "endcap_single_side_meat_t", "baseplate_relief_length", "baseplate_single_side_meat_t"} and value < 0:
E                           raise ValueError("截角／避讓參數不得小於 0")
E                       state = update_structure_config(state, type_id, {field: value})
E                   _phase6_commit_box_structure_state(self, state, rebuild=True)
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   _phase6_box_structure_error(self, exc)
E                   # Recreate from canonical state so an invalid typed value never becomes UI truth.
E                   _phase6_commit_box_structure_state(self, state, rebuild=True)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_structure_entry(box, row, label, value, callback, *, suffix="mm"):
E               original.ttk.Label(box, text=label).grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="w", padx=(0, 6), pady=2)
E               var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_setting_number_text(value))
E               entry = original.ttk.Entry(box, textvariable=var, width=10, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E               entry.grid(row=row, column=1, sticky="w", pady=2)
E               original.ttk.Label(box, text=suffix).grid(row=row, column=2, sticky="w", padx=(5, 12), pady=2)
E               entry.bind("<Return>", lambda _e: callback(var))
E               entry.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda _e: callback(var))
E               return var, entry
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_box_structure_settings(self, parent, start_row):
E               state = _phase6_box_structure_state(self)
E               active = BoxBodyStructureType(state["active_type"])
E               frame = original.ttk.LabelFrame(parent, text="結構參數", padding=5)
E               frame.grid(row=start_row, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=(6, 2))
E           
E               cfg = state["configs"][active.value]
E               row = 0
E               total_w = _phase6_box_structure_w(self)
E               if active is BoxBodyStructureType.TWO_PIECE_W_SPLIT:
E                   left, right = resolve_two_piece_widths(state, total_w)
E                   _phase6_structure_entry(frame, row, "W 左", left, lambda v: _phase6_apply_box_structure_numeric(self, active, "left", v)); row += 1
E                   _phase6_structure_entry(frame, row, "W 右", right, lambda v: _phase6_apply_box_structure_numeric(self, active, "right", v)); row += 1
E               elif active is BoxBodyStructureType.THREE_PIECE_W_SPLIT:
E                   left, middle, right = resolve_three_piece_widths(state, total_w)
E                   for label, field, value in (("W 左", "left", left), ("W 中", "middle", middle), ("W 右", "right", right)):
E                       _phase6_structure_entry(frame, row, label, value, lambda v, f=field: _phase6_apply_box_structure_numeric(self, active, f, v)); row += 1
E               elif active is BoxBodyStructureType.THREE_PIECE_SIDE_BACK_SPLIT:
E                   _phase6_structure_entry(frame, row, "側板後折", cfg.get("side_rear_bend", 15), lambda v: _phase6_apply_box_structure_numeric(self, active, "side_rear_bend", v)); row += 1
E                   original.ttk.Label(
E                       frame, text=f"側板成型深度 D：{_setting_number_text(_phase6_box_structure_d(self))} mm"
E                   ).grid(row=row, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="w", pady=2); row += 1
E                   _phase6_structure_entry(frame, row, "後面板寬補償", cfg.get("back_width_comp_t", 0.5), lambda v: _phase6_apply_box_structure_numeric(self, active, "back_width_comp_t", v), suffix="T"); row += 1
E                   back_w = total_w - float(cfg.get("back_width_comp_t", 0.5)) * float((getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}).get("t", 2.0))
E                   original.ttk.Label(frame, text=f"後面板成型寬：{_setting_number_text(back_w)} mm").grid(row=row, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="w", pady=2); row += 1
E           
E               if active is not BoxBodyStructureType.INTEGRAL:
E                   try:
E                       render_data = _phase6_query_final_render_data(self)
E                       role_labels = {
E                           "left": "左箱身", "middle": "中箱身", "right": "右箱身",
E                           "left_side": "左側板", "back": "後面板", "right_side": "右側板",
E                       }
E                       for piece in tuple(getattr(render_data, "pieces", ()) or ()):
E                           formed_w, formed_h = piece.formed_outer_dimensions
E                           material_w, material_h = piece.material_dimensions
E                           label = role_labels.get(str(piece.role), str(piece.role))
E                           original.ttk.Label(
E                               frame,
E                               text=(
E                                   f"{label}｜包外尺寸：{_setting_number_text(formed_w)} × {_setting_number_text(formed_h)} mm"
E                                   f"；料尺寸：{_setting_number_text(material_w)} × {_setting_number_text(material_h)} mm"
E                               ),
E                           ).grid(row=row, column=0, columnspan=4, sticky="w", pady=2)
E                           row += 1
E                   except Exception as exc:
E                       original.ttk.Label(
E                           frame, text=f"逐片尺寸：無法解析（{exc}）", foreground="#b45309"
E                       ).grid(row=row, column=0, columnspan=4, sticky="w", pady=2)
E                       row += 1
E           
E               if active in {BoxBodyStructureType.TWO_PIECE_W_SPLIT, BoxBodyStructureType.THREE_PIECE_W_SPLIT}:
E                   _phase6_structure_entry(frame, row, "中央接合折邊", cfg.get("seam_bend", 12), lambda v: _phase6_apply_box_structure_numeric(self, active, "seam_bend", v)); row += 1
E                   seam = float(cfg.get("seam_bend", 12))
E                   if seam >= 50:
E                       original.ttk.Label(frame, text=f"⚠ 中央接合折邊 {seam:g} mm 已達 50 mm 以上，請確認尺寸是否合理。", foreground="#b45309").grid(row=row, column=0, columnspan=4, sticky="w", pady=(2, 4)); row += 1
E                   advanced_flags = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_structure_advanced_open", {}) or {})
E                   advanced_open = bool(advanced_flags.get(active.value, False))
E                   original.ttk.Button(
E                       frame, text=("▼ 截角／避讓" if advanced_open else "▶ 截角／避讓"),
E                       command=lambda t=active: _phase6_toggle_structure_advanced(self, t),
E                   ).grid(row=row, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(5, 0)); row += 1
E                   advanced = original.ttk.LabelFrame(frame, text="截角／避讓", padding=4)
E                   if advanced_open:
E                       advanced.grid(row=row, column=0, columnspan=4, sticky="ew", pady=(3, 0))
E                   row += 1
E                   ar = 0
E                   for label, field, value, suffix in (
E                       ("封頭尾額外避讓", "endcap_extra_relief", cfg.get("endcap_extra_relief", 5), "mm"),
E                       ("封頭尾單邊留肉", "endcap_single_side_meat_t", cfg.get("endcap_single_side_meat_t", 0.5), "T"),
E                       ("底板避讓總長", "baseplate_relief_length", cfg.get("baseplate_relief_length", 20), "mm"),
E                       ("底板單邊留肉", "baseplate_single_side_meat_t", cfg.get("baseplate_single_side_meat_t", 0.5), "T"),
E                   ):
E                       _phase6_structure_entry(advanced, ar, label, value, lambda v, f=field: _phase6_apply_box_structure_numeric(self, active, f, v), suffix=suffix); ar += 1
E               return start_row + 1
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_joint_rows(self):
E               snapshot = migrate_legacy_snapshot_joints(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}))
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot.update({
E                   "assembly_joint_schema_version": snapshot["assembly_joint_schema_version"],
E                   "assembly_joints": deepcopy(snapshot["assembly_joints"]),
E               })
E               return list(self._phase6_input_snapshot["assembly_joints"])
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_add_user_joint(
E               self, *, subject_part, target_part, relation, subject_region="", target_region="",
E               clearance_policy="ZERO", contact_mode="AUTO", preserve_side="AUTO", relief_intent="AUTO",
E               solver_constraints=None,
E           ):
E               rows = _phase6_joint_rows(self)
E               rel = relation if isinstance(relation, AssemblyJointRelation) else AssemblyJointRelation(str(relation))
E               import uuid
E               joint = AssemblyJoint(
E                   joint_id=f"user:{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
E                   subject_part=str(subject_part), target_part=str(target_part),
E                   subject_region=str(subject_region or ""), target_region=str(target_region or ""),
E                   relation=rel, contact_mode=str(contact_mode or "AUTO"),
E                   preserve_side=str(preserve_side or "AUTO"), relief_intent=str(relief_intent or "AUTO"),
E                   clearance_policy=str(clearance_policy or "ZERO"),
E                   solver_constraints=dict(solver_constraints or {}),
E                   source=AssemblyJointSource.USER_ADDED,
E               )
E               key = (joint.subject_part, joint.target_part, joint.subject_region, joint.target_region, joint.relation.value)
E               for raw in rows:
E                   existing = AssemblyJoint.from_dict(raw)
E                   other = (existing.subject_part, existing.target_part, existing.subject_region, existing.target_region, existing.relation.value)
E                   if other == key:
E                       raise ValueError("相同 AssemblyJoint 已存在")
E               rows.append(joint.to_dict())
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["assembly_joints"] = rows
E               return joint.to_dict()
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_delete_user_joint(self, joint_id):
E               rows = _phase6_joint_rows(self)
E               found = None
E               kept = []
E               for raw in rows:
E                   if str(raw.get("joint_id")) == str(joint_id):
E                       found = AssemblyJoint.from_dict(raw)
E                       continue
E                   kept.append(raw)
E               if found is None:
E                   return False
E               if found.source is not AssemblyJointSource.USER_ADDED:
E                   raise ValueError("只有 USER_ADDED Joint 可以刪除")
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["assembly_joints"] = kept
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_sync_joint_state_for_intent(self, type_id):
E               snapshot = deepcopy(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}))
E               workspace = getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None)
E               parts = tuple(getattr(workspace, "available_parts", ()) or snapshot.get("existing_parts", ()) or ())
E               if parts:
E                   snapshot["existing_parts"] = list(parts)
E               snapshot = migrate_legacy_snapshot_joints(snapshot)
E               snapshot = sync_snapshot_intent_joints(snapshot, type_id)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot.update({
E                   "assembly_joint_schema_version": snapshot["assembly_joint_schema_version"],
E                   "assembly_joints": deepcopy(snapshot["assembly_joints"]),
E                   "assembly_type": snapshot["assembly_type"],
E               })
E               return tuple(snapshot["assembly_joints"])
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_assembly_type_selected(self, *_args):
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_rendering", False):
E                   return
E               var = getattr(self, "assembly_type_var", None)
E               type_id = ASSEMBLY_LABEL_TO_TYPE.get(str(var.get()).strip()) if var is not None else None
E               if type_id is None:
E                   return
E               self._phase6_assembly_type = type_id
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["assembly_type"] = assembly_intent_value(type_id)
E               _phase6_sync_joint_state_for_intent(self, type_id)
E               # Normal preset selection updates Intent + Joint Graph only. CornerState is
E               # a legacy/manual projection and must not be rewritten by this UI action.
E               self.designer_workspace.mark_dirty()
E               # The box-body page owns the live assembly Combobox that fired this event.
E               # Destroying/rebuilding that page from inside <<ComboboxSelected>> destroys
E               # the widget while Tk is still dispatching its event (visible as the whole
E               # selector/page disappearing on Windows).  Only dependent EndCap pages need
E               # rebuilding because their top CornerType controls changed.
E               for context in ("head", "tail"):
E                   _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, context)
E               _phase6_rebuild_linked_endcaps(self)
E               try:
E                   self.do_update()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E           
E           _ENDCAP_EDGE_LABELS = {
E               "TOP": "上", "BOTTOM": "下", "LEFT": "左", "RIGHT": "右",
E           }
E           _ENDCAP_RELATION_LABELS = {
E               AssemblyJointRelation.INSERT: "嵌入",
E               AssemblyJointRelation.OVERLAY: "貼外",
E               AssemblyJointRelation.INSERT_OVERLAY: "嵌入貼外",
E               AssemblyJointRelation.WRAP: "包覆",
E           }
E           _ENDCAP_LABEL_TO_RELATION = {label: relation for relation, label in _ENDCAP_RELATION_LABELS.items()}
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_endcap_joint_policy_rows(self, part_key):
E               part_key = str(part_key)
E               if part_key not in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS:
E                   return ()
E               snapshot = migrate_legacy_snapshot_joints(
E                   dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               )
E               intent_id = assembly_intent_value(
E                   snapshot.get("assembly_type")
E                   or getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY)
E               )
E               record = get_assembly_intent(intent_id)
E               rows = []
E               for edge in ("TOP", "BOTTOM", "LEFT", "RIGHT"):
E                   policy = record.edge_policy(edge)
E                   relation = edge_relation_for_part(snapshot, part_key, edge) or policy.default_relation
E                   rows.append({
E                       "edge": edge,
E                       "relation": relation,
E                       "value": _ENDCAP_RELATION_LABELS[relation],
E                       "allowed": tuple(_ENDCAP_RELATION_LABELS[item] for item in policy.allowed_relations),
E                       "editable": bool(policy.editable),
E                   })
E               return tuple(rows)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_endcap_edge_relation_selected(self, part_key, edge):
E               part_key = str(part_key)
E               edge = str(edge).upper()
E               var = dict(getattr(self, "endcap_joint_vars", {}) or {}).get(edge)
E               if var is None:
E                   return None
E               relation = _ENDCAP_LABEL_TO_RELATION.get(str(var.get()).strip())
E               if relation is None:
E                   return None
E               snapshot = set_part_edge_relation(
E                   dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}),
E                   part_key, edge, relation,
E               )
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot.update({
E                   "assembly_joint_schema_version": snapshot["assembly_joint_schema_version"],
E                   "assembly_joints": deepcopy(snapshot["assembly_joints"]),
E                   "assembly_type": snapshot.get("assembly_type", self._phase6_input_snapshot.get("assembly_type")),
E               })
E               workspace = getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None)
E               if workspace is not None:
E                   workspace.mark_dirty()
E               try:
E                   self.do_update()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               return relation
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_endcap_joint_settings(self, parent, part_key, start_row):
E               rows = _phase6_endcap_joint_policy_rows(self, part_key)
E               if not rows:
E                   return start_row
E               box = original.ttk.LabelFrame(parent, text="四邊組合", padding=4)
E               box.grid(row=start_row, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=(5, 2))
E               self.endcap_joint_vars = {}
E               self.endcap_joint_widgets = {}
E               self.endcap_joint_allowed = {}
E               for col in range(4):
E                   box.columnconfigure(col, weight=1)
E               for col, row in enumerate(rows):
E                   edge = row["edge"]
E                   cell = original.ttk.Frame(box)
E                   cell.grid(row=0, column=col, sticky="ew", padx=2)
E                   original.ttk.Label(cell, text=_ENDCAP_EDGE_LABELS[edge]).pack(anchor="w")
E                   var = original.tk.StringVar(value=row["value"])
E                   widget = build_choice_menubutton(
E                       cell, variable=var, values=row["allowed"],
E                       state=("normal" if row["editable"] else "disabled"), width=9,
E                       command=lambda p=part_key, e=edge: _phase6_on_endcap_edge_relation_selected(self, p, e),
E                   )
E                   widget.pack(fill=original.tk.X)
E                   self.endcap_joint_vars[edge] = var
E                   self.endcap_joint_widgets[edge] = widget
E                   self.endcap_joint_allowed[edge] = tuple(row["allowed"])
E               return start_row + 1
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_assembly_settings(self, parent, start_row):
E               # 組合方式已移到右側 3D 常駐控制列。保留函式只為舊 caller 相容，
E               # 不再在可鎖定的參數面板重複建立第二份 UI。
E               return start_row
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_box_symmetry_changed(self):
E               """Keep the restored Phase6 box-body symmetry control authoritative."""
E               var = getattr(self, "v_sy", None)
E               if var is None:
E                   return
E               try:
E                   self.state.symmetric = bool(var.get())
E               except Exception:
E                   return
E               self.designer_workspace.mark_dirty()
E           
E               # v_sy already owns an original trace to queue_update().  When this command
E               # callback is invoked by the restored checkbox, cancel that queued duplicate
E               # redraw and perform the update once with the new authoritative state.
E               pending = getattr(self, "_job", None)
E               root = getattr(self, "root", None)
E               if pending and root is not None:
E                   try:
E                       root.after_cancel(pending)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   self._job = None
E               try:
E                   self.do_update()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_box_symmetry_settings(self, parent, start_row):
E               box = original.ttk.LabelFrame(parent, text="箱身折彎", padding=4)
E               box.grid(row=start_row, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=(6, 2))
E               if not hasattr(self, "v_sy"):
E                   self.v_sy = original.tk.BooleanVar(value=bool(getattr(self.state, "symmetric", True)))
E               original.ttk.Checkbutton(
E                   box,
E                   text="對稱折彎",
E                   variable=self.v_sy,
E                   command=lambda: _phase6_on_box_symmetry_changed(self),
E               ).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 8))
E               original.ttk.Label(box, text="開啟時，箱身兩側對應折彎同步修改").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E               return start_row + 1
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_commit_receiving_bottom_wrap_controls(self, part_key, reserve_u_var, reserve_v_var):
E               """Commit receiving WRAP reserve values without owning the Joint relation."""
E               if str(part_key) not in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS:
E                   return None
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               if not cabinet_family_policy.supports_bottom_wrap_controls(snapshot):
E                   return None
E               current = resolve_endcap_bottom_wrap(
E                   snapshot, str(part_key),
E                   state=getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state", None),
E               )
E               # A stale settings page may outlive an Assembly Intent change.  Never let
E               # reserve editing recreate WRAP after the graph changed away from it.
E               if not bool(current.get("enabled", False)):
E                   return None
E               try:
E                   reserve_u = max(0.0, float(reserve_u_var.get()))
E                   reserve_v = max(0.0, float(reserve_v_var.get()))
E               except (TypeError, ValueError, original.tk.TclError):
E                   return None
E               state = getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state", None)
E               if not isinstance(state, dict):
E                   state = normalize_endcap_bottom_wrap_state(snapshot)
E                   self._phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state = state
E               commit_endcap_bottom_wrap(
E                   state, str(part_key), reserve_u=reserve_u, reserve_v=reserve_v,
E               )
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["endcap_bottom_wrap"] = deepcopy(state)
E               self._phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_geometry = None
E               self._phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_signature = None
E               for key in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS:
E                   _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, key)
E               self.designer_workspace.mark_dirty()
E               self.do_update()
E               return resolve_endcap_bottom_wrap(self._phase6_input_snapshot, str(part_key), state=state)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_receiving_bottom_wrap_settings(self, parent, part_key, start_row):
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               if (
E                   str(part_key) not in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS
E                   or not cabinet_family_policy.supports_bottom_wrap_controls(snapshot)
E               ):
E                   return start_row, None, None, None, None
E               item = resolve_endcap_bottom_wrap(
E                   snapshot, str(part_key),
E                   state=getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state", normalize_endcap_bottom_wrap_state(snapshot)),
E               )
E               if not bool(item.get("enabled", False)):
E                   return start_row, None, None, None, None
E               box = original.ttk.LabelFrame(parent, text="下方包覆貼外預留", padding=4)
E               box.grid(row=start_row, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=(6, 2))
E               reserve_u_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_setting_number_text(item["reserve_u"]))
E               reserve_v_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_setting_number_text(item["reserve_v"]))
E               original.ttk.Label(box, text="X 預留 (mm)").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="e")
E               u_entry = original.ttk.Entry(box, textvariable=reserve_u_var, width=8, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E               u_entry.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(3, 10))
E               original.ttk.Label(box, text="Y 預留 (mm)").grid(row=0, column=2, sticky="e")
E               v_entry = original.ttk.Entry(box, textvariable=reserve_v_var, width=8, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E               v_entry.grid(row=0, column=3, padx=3)
E               for entry in (u_entry, v_entry):
E                   entry.bind("<Return>", lambda _e: _phase6_commit_receiving_bottom_wrap_controls(
E                       self, part_key, reserve_u_var, reserve_v_var
E                   ))
E                   entry.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda _e: _phase6_commit_receiving_bottom_wrap_controls(
E                       self, part_key, reserve_u_var, reserve_v_var
E                   ))
E               original.ttk.Label(
E                   box, text="WRAP 關係由組合方式／Joint Graph 決定；此處只調整預留。封頭/封尾先連動，修改另一端後才獨立。"
E               ).grid(row=1, column=0, columnspan=4, sticky="w", pady=(3, 0))
E               return start_row + 1, None, reserve_u_var, reserve_v_var, box
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_corner_settings(self, parent, part_key, start_row):
E               self.corner_pair_vars = {}
E               self.corner_pair_checkbuttons = {}
E               self.corner_type_vars = {}
E               self.corner_mode_vars = {}
E               self.corner_direction_vars = {}
E               self.corner_amount_vars = {}
E               self.corner_secondary_retain_vars = {}
E               self.corner_secondary_depth_vars = {}
E               self.corner_detail_frames = {}
E               self.fixed_corner_summary_var = original.tk.StringVar(value="")
E               self.corner_param_lock_button = None
E               if part_key == GLOBAL_CONTEXT or part_key == "box_body":
E                   return start_row
E           
E               # Shared indicator parts are factory-fixed and never unlockable.
E               if part_key in {"indicator_box", "indicator_door"}:
E                   summary = _FIXED_CORNER_SUMMARIES.get(part_key, "")
E                   if summary:
E                       box = original.ttk.LabelFrame(parent, text="截角類型（固定 / 唯讀）", padding=4)
E                       box.grid(row=start_row, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=(6, 2))
E                       self.fixed_corner_summary_var.set(summary)
E                       original.ttk.Label(box, textvariable=self.fixed_corner_summary_var, wraplength=900).pack(anchor=original.tk.W)
E                       return start_row + 1
E                   return start_row
E           
E               type_editable = _phase6_corner_type_editable(self, part_key)
E               params_unlocked = _phase6_corner_parameters_unlocked(self, part_key)
E               params_editable = _phase6_corner_parameters_editable(self, part_key)
E           
E               if not type_editable and not params_unlocked:
E                   # A read-only settings page is a view. Building it must not materialize
E                   # default Corner state into the canonical manufacturing snapshot, or a
E                   # pure part switch invalidates the manufacturing cache.
E                   summary = _FIXED_CORNER_SUMMARIES.get(part_key, "")
E                   if summary:
E                       box = original.ttk.LabelFrame(parent, text="截角類型（固定 / 唯讀）", padding=4)
E                       box.grid(row=start_row, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=(6, 2))
E                       self.fixed_corner_summary_var.set(summary)
E                       original.ttk.Label(box, textvariable=self.fixed_corner_summary_var, wraplength=900).pack(anchor=original.tk.W)
E                       return start_row + 1
E           
E               state, pairs = _phase6_ensure_corner_part(self, part_key)
E               box = original.ttk.LabelFrame(
E                   parent,
E                   text="截角類型" if type_editable else "截角類型（基準預設）",
E                   padding=4,
E               )
E               box.grid(row=start_row, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=(6, 2))
E           
E               for pair_key in ("top", "bottom"):
E                   row = original.ttk.Frame(box)
E                   row.pack(fill=original.tk.X, pady=3)
E                   original.ttk.Label(row, text="上方" if pair_key == "top" else "下方", width=6).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E                   same_var = original.tk.BooleanVar(master=row, value=bool(pairs[pair_key]))
E                   self.corner_pair_vars[pair_key] = same_var
E                   same_cb = original.ttk.Checkbutton(
E                       row, text="左右相同", variable=same_var,
E                       command=lambda p=part_key, pair=pair_key, v=same_var: _phase6_corner_pair_var_changed(self, p, pair, v),
E                   )
E                   same_cb.configure(state=("normal" if params_editable else "disabled"))
E                   self.corner_pair_checkbuttons[pair_key] = same_cb
E                   if params_unlocked:
E                       same_cb.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 6))
E                   targets = (pair_key,) if pairs[pair_key] else _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS[pair_key]
E           
E                   for target_key in targets:
E                       physical = _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS[target_key][0] if target_key in _CORNER_PAIR_KEYS else target_key
E                       selection = _phase6_selection_from_raw(state[physical])
E                       target = original.ttk.Frame(row)
E                       target.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(3, 8))
E                       if len(targets) > 1:
E                           original.ttk.Label(target, text="左" if target_key.endswith("left") else "右").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w")
E           
E                       type_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_CORNER_TYPE_LABEL_BY_ID[selection.type_id.value])
E                       self.corner_type_vars[target_key] = type_var
E                       top_assembly_owned = part_key in {"head", "tail"} and pair_key == "top"
E                       type_cb = build_choice_menubutton(
E                           target,
E                           variable=type_var,
E                           values=tuple(_CORNER_TYPE_LABEL_BY_ID.values()),
E                           state=("normal" if type_editable and not top_assembly_owned else "disabled"),
E                           width=12,
E                           command=lambda p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_type_selected(self, p, t),
E                       )
E                       type_cb.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=2, sticky="w")
E           
E                       subrow = original.ttk.Frame(target)
E                       self.corner_detail_frames[target_key] = subrow
E                       amount_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_setting_number_text(selection.amount_t if selection.amount_t is not None else 1.0))
E                       self.corner_amount_vars[target_key] = amount_var
E           
E                       if params_unlocked:
E                           subrow.grid(row=1, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(2, 0))
E                       else:
E                           original.ttk.Label(
E                               target,
E                               text=_phase6_corner_parameter_summary(selection),
E                           ).grid(row=1, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(2, 0))
E           
E                       if selection.type_id is CornerTypeId.CROSS:
E                           mode_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_CORNER_MODE_LABEL[selection.cross_mode])
E                           self.corner_mode_vars[target_key] = mode_var
E                           mode_cb = build_choice_menubutton(
E                               subrow,
E                               variable=mode_var,
E                               values=("標準", "單邊留肉", "多切"),
E                               state=("normal" if params_editable else "disabled"),
E                               width=9,
E                               command=lambda p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_mode_selected(self, p, t),
E                           )
E                           mode_cb.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 3))
E                           if selection.cross_mode is not CrossCornerMode.STANDARD:
E                               direction_values = ("寬", "高") if selection.cross_mode is CrossCornerMode.RETAIN else ("寬＋高", "寬", "高")
E                               direction_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_CORNER_DIRECTION_LABEL[selection.direction])
E                               self.corner_direction_vars[target_key] = direction_var
E                               direction_cb = build_choice_menubutton(
E                                   subrow,
E                                   variable=direction_var,
E                                   values=direction_values,
E                                   state=("normal" if params_editable else "disabled"),
E                                   width=7,
E                                   command=lambda p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_target_var_changed(self, p, t),
E                               )
E                               direction_cb.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=3)
E                               entry = original.ttk.Entry(subrow, textvariable=amount_var, width=6, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E                               entry.configure(state=("normal" if params_editable else "disabled"))
E                               entry.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(3, 1))
E                               original.ttk.Label(subrow, text="T").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E                               entry.bind("<Return>", lambda _e, p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_target_var_changed(self, p, t))
E                               entry.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda _e, p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_target_var_changed(self, p, t))
E                       elif selection.type_id in (CornerTypeId.OVERLAY, CornerTypeId.INSERT):
E                           action = "留肉（高）" if selection.type_id is CornerTypeId.OVERLAY else "多切（高）"
E                           original.ttk.Label(subrow, text=action).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 3))
E                           entry = original.ttk.Entry(subrow, textvariable=amount_var, width=6, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E                           entry.configure(state=("normal" if params_editable else "disabled"))
E                           entry.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(3, 1))
E                           original.ttk.Label(subrow, text="T").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E                           entry.bind("<Return>", lambda _e, p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_target_var_changed(self, p, t))
E                           entry.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda _e, p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_target_var_changed(self, p, t))
E                       else:
E                           original.ttk.Label(subrow, text="貼外留肉（高）").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 2))
E                           primary = original.ttk.Entry(subrow, textvariable=amount_var, width=5, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E                           primary.configure(state=("normal" if params_editable else "disabled"))
E                           primary.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(1, 1)); original.ttk.Label(subrow, text="T").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E                           retain_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_setting_number_text(selection.secondary_retain_t))
E                           depth_var = original.tk.StringVar(value=_setting_number_text(selection.secondary_depth_t))
E                           self.corner_secondary_retain_vars[target_key] = retain_var
E                           self.corner_secondary_depth_vars[target_key] = depth_var
E                           original.ttk.Label(subrow, text="  嵌入留肉").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E                           retain = original.ttk.Entry(subrow, textvariable=retain_var, width=5, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E                           retain.configure(state=("normal" if params_editable else "disabled"))
E                           retain.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(1, 1)); original.ttk.Label(subrow, text="T  深度").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E                           depth = original.ttk.Entry(subrow, textvariable=depth_var, width=5, justify=original.tk.CENTER)
E                           depth.configure(state=("normal" if params_editable else "disabled"))
E                           depth.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(1, 1)); original.ttk.Label(subrow, text="T").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E                           for entry in (primary, retain, depth):
E                               entry.bind("<Return>", lambda _e, p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_target_var_changed(self, p, t))
E                               entry.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda _e, p=part_key, t=target_key: _phase6_corner_target_var_changed(self, p, t))
E               return start_row + 1
E           
E           def _phase6_apply_external_assembly_type(self, type_id):
E               stable = assembly_intent_value(type_id)
E               self._phase6_assembly_type = (
E                   stable if stable == "WRAP_OVERLAY" else CornerTypeId(stable)
E               )
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["assembly_type"] = stable
E               _phase6_sync_joint_state_for_intent(self, self._phase6_assembly_type)
E               legacy_projection = legacy_corner_projection_for_intent(stable)
E               apply_box_assembly_type_to_raw_state(
E                   self._phase6_corner_state, self._phase6_corner_pair_same, legacy_projection,
E                   reset_bottom_defaults=(stable == CornerTypeId.OVERLAY.value),
E               )
E               for context in ("box_body", "head", "tail"):
E                   _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, context)
E               if getattr(self, "active_part_key", None) is not None:
E                   _phase6_render_settings_context(self, getattr(self, "settings_context", self.active_part_key))
E               self.do_update()
E               return self._phase6_assembly_type
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_apply_external_corner_state(self, corner_state, corner_pair_same):
E               self._phase6_corner_guard = True
E               try:
E                   self._phase6_corner_state = deepcopy(corner_state or {})
E                   self._phase6_corner_pair_same = deepcopy(corner_pair_same or {})
E               finally:
E                   self._phase6_corner_guard = False
E               context = getattr(self, "settings_context", GLOBAL_CONTEXT)
E               if context != GLOBAL_CONTEXT:
E                   _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, context)
E                   _phase6_render_settings_context(self, context)
E

E           _SETTINGS_EXTENSION_MAP_ATTRS = (
E               "corner_pair_vars",
E               "corner_pair_checkbuttons",
E               "corner_type_vars",
E               "corner_mode_vars",
E               "corner_direction_vars",
E               "corner_amount_vars",
E               "corner_secondary_retain_vars",
E               "corner_secondary_depth_vars",
E               "corner_detail_frames",
E               "endcap_joint_vars",
E               "endcap_joint_widgets",
E               "endcap_joint_allowed",
E           )

E           
E           def _phase6_render_settings_panel_extensions(self, parent, context, start_row):
E               old = {name: getattr(self, name, None) for name in _SETTINGS_EXTENSION_MAP_ATTRS}
E               old_summary = getattr(self, "fixed_corner_summary_var", None)
E               old_lock_button = getattr(self, "corner_param_lock_button", None)
E               for name in _SETTINGS_EXTENSION_MAP_ATTRS:
E                   setattr(self, name, {})
E               self.fixed_corner_summary_var = original.tk.StringVar(value="")
E               self.corner_param_lock_button = None
E               endcap_fw_follow_var = None
E               endcap_fw_value_var = None
E               endcap_fw_widget = None
E               bottom_wrap_enabled_var = None
E               bottom_wrap_reserve_u_var = None
E               bottom_wrap_reserve_v_var = None
E               bottom_wrap_widget = None
E               try:
E                   next_row = int(start_row)
E                   if context == "box_body":
E                       next_row = _phase6_build_box_structure_settings(self, parent, next_row)
E                   elif context in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS:
E                       next_row = _phase6_build_endcap_joint_settings(self, parent, context, next_row)
E                       next_row, endcap_fw_follow_var, endcap_fw_value_var, endcap_fw_widget = _phase6_build_endcap_fw_settings(
E                           self, parent, context, next_row
E                       )
E                       next_row, bottom_wrap_enabled_var, bottom_wrap_reserve_u_var, bottom_wrap_reserve_v_var, bottom_wrap_widget = (
E                           _phase6_build_receiving_bottom_wrap_settings(self, parent, context, next_row)
E                       )
E                   next_row = _phase6_build_corner_settings(self, parent, context, next_row)
E                   state = {name: dict(getattr(self, name, {}) or {}) for name in _SETTINGS_EXTENSION_MAP_ATTRS}
E                   state.update({
E                       "fixed_corner_summary_var": self.fixed_corner_summary_var,
E                       "corner_param_lock_button": self.corner_param_lock_button,
E                       "endcap_fw_follow_var": endcap_fw_follow_var,
E                       "endcap_fw_value_var": endcap_fw_value_var,
E                       "endcap_fw_widget": endcap_fw_widget,
E                       "bottom_wrap_enabled_var": bottom_wrap_enabled_var,
E                       "bottom_wrap_reserve_u_var": bottom_wrap_reserve_u_var,
E                       "bottom_wrap_reserve_v_var": bottom_wrap_reserve_v_var,
E                       "bottom_wrap_widget": bottom_wrap_widget,
E                   })
E                   return SettingsPanelExtensionResult(next_row=next_row, state=state)
E               finally:
E                   for name, value in old.items():
E                       setattr(self, name, value if value is not None else {})
E                   self.fixed_corner_summary_var = old_summary
E                   self.corner_param_lock_button = old_lock_button
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_sync_settings_panel_extension(self, state, context):
E               state = dict(state or {})
E               for name in _SETTINGS_EXTENSION_MAP_ATTRS:
E                   setattr(self, name, dict(state.get(name, {}) or {}))
E               self.fixed_corner_summary_var = state.get("fixed_corner_summary_var") or original.tk.StringVar(value="")
E               self.corner_param_lock_button = state.get("corner_param_lock_button")
E               self.bottom_wrap_enabled_var = state.get("bottom_wrap_enabled_var")
E               self.bottom_wrap_reserve_u_var = state.get("bottom_wrap_reserve_u_var")
E               self.bottom_wrap_reserve_v_var = state.get("bottom_wrap_reserve_v_var")
E               self.bottom_wrap_widget = state.get("bottom_wrap_widget")
E               if context in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS and state.get("endcap_fw_follow_var") is not None:
E                   snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E                   snapshot.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E                   fw_state = self._phase6_endcap_fw_state.setdefault(
E                       context, {"follow_box": True, "value": _num(snapshot.get("fw", 25), 25)}
E                   )
E                   follow = bool(fw_state.get("follow_box", True))
E                   effective = resolve_endcap_fw(snapshot, context, state=self._phase6_endcap_fw_state)
E                   state["endcap_fw_follow_var"].set(follow)
E                   state["endcap_fw_value_var"].set(_setting_number_text(effective))
E                   try:
E                       state["endcap_fw_widget"].configure(state="normal")
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E           
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_ensure_settings_panel(self):
E               panel = getattr(self, "settings_panel", None)
E               if panel is not None:
E                   return panel
E               panel = Phase6SettingsPanel(
E                   values_snapshot=lambda: dict(self._settings_values),
E                   stage_setting_update=lambda key, value: _phase6_stage_setting_update(self, key, value),
E                   flush_settings=lambda: _phase6_flush_pending_settings(self),
E                   save_defaults=lambda context: _phase6_save_settings_context_as_defaults(self, context),
E                   query_baseline_rows=(
E                       (lambda context, model, values: self._baseline_data_query_callback(context, model, values))
E                       if self._baseline_data_query_callback is not None else None
E                   ),
E                   is_unknown_baseline=lambda model: _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, model),
E                   should_show_baseline_data=lambda context, specs: _phase6_should_show_baseline_data(self, context, specs),
E                   part_labels=PART_LABELS,
E                   render_context_extensions=lambda parent, context, row: _phase6_render_settings_panel_extensions(self, parent, context, row),
E                   sync_context_extension=lambda state, context: _phase6_sync_settings_panel_extension(self, state, context),
E                   baseline_model_changed=lambda: _phase6_on_baseline_model_changed(self),
E                   ui_text_size_changed=lambda key: _phase6_apply_ui_text_size(self, key),
E               )
E               self.settings_panel = panel
E               return panel
E

E               self.setting_vars = panel.setting_vars
E               self._settings_page_cache = panel.page_cache
E               self._settings_current_page = panel.current_page
E               self.settings_context = panel.settings_context
E               self.advanced_settings_visible = panel.advanced_settings_visible
E               self.advanced_settings_frame = panel.advanced_settings_frame
E               self.advanced_toggle_button = panel.advanced_toggle_button
E               self.baseline_data_frame = panel.baseline_data_frame
E               self.baseline_data_toggle_button = panel.baseline_data_toggle_button
E               self.baseline_setting_cells = panel.baseline_setting_cells
E               self.left_global_controls = panel.left_global_controls
E               self.left_global_vars = panel.left_global_vars
E               self.left_global_cells = panel.left_global_cells
E               self.baseline_model_var = panel.baseline_model_var
E               self.baseline_model_combo = panel.baseline_model_combo
E               self.ui_text_size_var = panel.ui_text_size_var

E           def _phase6_apply_external_settings(self, updates):
E               self._phase6_external_apply_guard = True
E               try:
E                   return _phase6_apply_setting_updates(self, updates, notify=False)
E               finally:
E                   self._phase6_external_apply_guard = False
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_apply_external_sync(self, envelope):
E               """Ingest one Main-GUI revision without echoing it back to the host."""
E               envelope = dict(envelope or {})
E               if str(envelope.get("origin") or "") != "main_gui":
E                   return {}
E               try:
E                   revision = int(envelope.get("revision", 0) or 0)
E               except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                   return {}
E               last_revision = int(getattr(self, "_phase6_last_external_revision", 0) or 0)
E               if revision <= last_revision:
E                   return {}
E               self._phase6_last_external_revision = revision
E               self._phase6_last_external_transaction_id = str(envelope.get("transaction_id") or "")
E               delta = dict(envelope.get("delta") or {})
E               settings = dict(delta.get("settings") or {})
E               if not settings:
E                   return {}
E               return _phase6_apply_external_settings(self, settings)
E

E           def _phase6_apply_ui_text_size(self, key):
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_settings_guard", False):
E                   return
E               key = normalize_ui_text_size(key)
E               self._settings_values["ui_text_size"] = key
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["ui_text_size"] = key
E               self._ui_text_controller.apply(key)
E               self.state.ui_text_scale = self._ui_text_controller.factor
E               callback = getattr(self, "_ui_text_size_change_callback", None)
E               if callback is not None:
E                   callback(key)
E               try:
E                   self.bend_ui.render()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               try:
E                   self.renderer.render()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_ui_text_size_changed(self, *_args):
E               panel = getattr(self, "settings_panel", None)
E               var = getattr(panel, "ui_text_size_var", None) if panel is not None else getattr(self, "ui_text_size_var", None)
E               if var is None:
E                   return
E               return _phase6_apply_ui_text_size(self, normalize_ui_text_size(var.get()))
