E           def _phase6_is_derived_physical_part_key(value):
E               key = str(value or "")
E               return key.startswith("box_body:divider:") or (key.startswith("inner_door:") and key.endswith("_frame"))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_part_label(value):
E               key = str(value or "")
E               if key in PART_LABELS:
E                   return PART_LABELS[key]
E               if key.startswith("box_body:divider:"):
E                   axis = "橫向" if ":HORIZONTAL:" in key else ("直向" if ":VERTICAL:" in key else "")
E                   return f"箱身中隔（{axis}）" if axis else "箱身中隔"
E               if key.startswith("inner_door:") and key.endswith("_frame"):
E                   side = key.rsplit(":", 1)[-1].removesuffix("_frame")
E                   side_label = {"top": "上框", "bottom": "下框", "left": "左框", "right": "右框"}.get(side, "框")
E                   door_id = key.split(":", 2)[1]
E                   door_label = {"upper": "上層內門", "lower": "下層內門"}.get(door_id, "內門")
E                   return f"{door_label}{side_label}"
E               return key
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_operator_label(value):
E               raw = str(value or "")
E               if raw in PART_LABELS or _phase6_is_derived_physical_part_key(raw):
E                   return _phase6_part_label(raw)
E               return _PHASE6_OPERATOR_LABELS.get(raw, raw)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_operator_text(value):
E               text = str(value or "")
E               # Longer tokens first so INSERT_OVERLAY is not partially replaced by INSERT.
E               replacements = sorted(_PHASE6_OPERATOR_LABELS.items(), key=lambda item: len(item[0]), reverse=True)
E               for raw, label in replacements:
E                   text = text.replace(raw, label)
E               for raw, label in PART_LABELS.items():
E                   text = text.replace(raw, label)
E               return text
E           
E           
E           _PHASE6_FORMULA_DISPLAY_TOKENS = {
E               "effective_mating_width": "有效接合寬",
E               "mating_width": "成型接合寬",
E               "side_fold": "側折",
E               "rear_bend": "後折",
E               "reserve_u": "X預留",
E               "reserve_v": "Y預留",
E               "ybottom1": "下折",
E               "ytop1": "上折",
E               "clearance": "間隙",
E               "fold_u": "X向折邊",
E               "fold_v": "Y向折邊",
E               "FW": "框寬",
E               "T": "板厚",
E           }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_formula_display(value):
E               text = str(value or "")
E               for raw, label in sorted(_PHASE6_FORMULA_DISPLAY_TOKENS.items(), key=lambda item: len(item[0]), reverse=True):
E                   text = re.sub(rf"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_]){re.escape(raw)}(?![A-Za-z0-9_])", label, text)
E               return text
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_formula_raw(value):
E               text = str(value or "")
E               for raw, label in sorted(_PHASE6_FORMULA_DISPLAY_TOKENS.items(), key=lambda item: len(item[1]), reverse=True):
E                   text = text.replace(label, raw)
E               return text
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_preconditions_display(value):
E               tokens = [token.strip() for token in str(value or "").split(",") if token.strip()]
E               return "、".join(_phase6_operator_label(token) for token in tokens)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_preconditions_raw(value):
E               reverse = {label: raw for raw, label in _PHASE6_OPERATOR_LABELS.items()}
E               tokens = [token.strip() for token in re.split(r"[,，、]", str(value or "")) if token.strip()]
E               return ",".join(reverse.get(token, token) for token in tokens)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_bind_translated_var(raw_var, display_var, to_display, to_raw):
E               busy = {"value": False}
E               def raw_changed(*_args):
E                   if busy["value"]:
E                       return
E                   busy["value"] = True
E                   try:
E                       display_var.set(to_display(raw_var.get()))
E                   finally:
E                       busy["value"] = False
E               def display_changed(*_args):
E                   if busy["value"]:
E                       return
E                   busy["value"] = True
E                   try:
E                       raw_var.set(to_raw(display_var.get()))
E                   finally:
E                       busy["value"] = False
E               raw_var.trace_add("write", raw_changed)
E               display_var.trace_add("write", display_changed)
E               raw_changed()
E               display_var._phase6_raw_var = raw_var
E               return display_var
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_form_choice(parent, variable, choices, *, width=18):
E               display_var = original.tk.StringVar(master=parent, value=_phase6_operator_label(variable.get()))
E               button = original.ttk.Menubutton(parent, textvariable=display_var, width=width)
E               menu = original.tk.Menu(button, tearoff=False)
E           
E               def choose(raw):
E                   variable.set(str(raw))
E                   display_var.set(_phase6_operator_label(raw))
E           
E               for choice in choices:
E                   menu.add_command(label=_phase6_operator_label(choice), command=lambda v=str(choice): choose(v))
E               button.configure(menu=menu)
E           
E               def sync_display(*_args):
E                   value = _phase6_operator_label(variable.get())
E                   if display_var.get() != value:
E                       display_var.set(value)
E           
E               variable.trace_add("write", sync_display)
E               button._phase6_display_var = display_var
E               button._phase6_raw_var = variable
E               return button
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_collect_rule_form(self):
E               intent = str(self.relief_registry_intent_var.get() or "INSERT_OVERLAY")
E               topology = int(float(self.relief_registry_topology_var.get() or (2 if intent == "INSERT_OVERLAY" else 1)))
E               main_target = str(self.relief_registry_target_role_var.get() or "BOX_SIDE")
E               region = str(self.relief_registry_joint_face_var.get() or "TOP")
E               signature = [{
E                   "relation": intent,
E                   "subject_role": str(self.relief_registry_part_role_var.get() or "HEAD_OR_TAIL"),
E                   "target_role": main_target,
E                   "subject_region": region,
E                   "target_region": "MATING_ZONE" if intent != "OVERLAY" else "OUTER_SURFACE",
E               }]
E               extra = str(self.relief_registry_extra_joint_var.get() or "NONE")
E               if extra != "NONE":
E                   signature.append({
E                       "relation": extra,
E                       "subject_role": str(self.relief_registry_part_role_var.get() or "HEAD_OR_TAIL"),
E                       "target_role": str(self.relief_registry_extra_target_role_var.get() or "REAR_PANEL"),
E                       "subject_region": region,
E                       "target_region": "WRAP_ZONE" if extra == "WRAP" else "MATING_ZONE",
E                   })
E               formula = {
E                   "primary_u": str(self.relief_registry_primary_u_var.get()).strip(),
E                   "primary_v": str(self.relief_registry_primary_v_var.get()).strip(),
E               }
E               if topology == 2:
E                   formula["secondary_u"] = str(self.relief_registry_secondary_u_var.get()).strip()
E                   formula["secondary_depth"] = str(self.relief_registry_secondary_depth_var.get()).strip()
E               preconditions = [v.strip() for v in str(self.relief_registry_preconditions_var.get() or "").split(",") if v.strip()]
E               return {
E                   "rule_id": str(self.relief_registry_rule_id_var.get() or "").strip(),
E                   "cabinet_family": str(self.relief_registry_family_var.get() or "ANY").strip(),
E                   "part_role": str(self.relief_registry_part_role_var.get() or "HEAD_OR_TAIL").strip(),
E                   "joint_face": region,
E                   "assembly_intent": intent,
E                   "joint_signature": signature,
E                   "topology_levels": topology,
E                   "preconditions": preconditions,
E                   "formula": formula,
E                   "symmetry": str(self.relief_registry_symmetry_var.get() or "MIRROR_IF_GEOMETRY_SYMMETRIC"),
E                   "source": str(self.relief_registry_source_var.get() or "").strip(),
E               }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_sample_variables(self):
E               values = {}
E               mapping = {
E                   "T": self.relief_registry_sample_t_var,
E                   "FW": self.relief_registry_sample_fw_var,
E                   "side_fold": self.relief_registry_sample_side_var,
E                   "ytop1": self.relief_registry_sample_ytop_var,
E                   "mating_width": self.relief_registry_sample_mating_var,
E               }
E               for key, var in mapping.items():
E                   values[key] = float(var.get())
E               values["effective_mating_width"] = values["mating_width"]
E               values["fold_u"] = values["side_fold"]
E               values["fold_v"] = values["ytop1"]
E               values["clearance"] = 0.0
E               return values
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_validate_formula_form(self):
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import evaluate_relief_formula_record, _validate_editable_rule_record
E               try:
E                   record = _validate_editable_rule_record(_phase6_registry_collect_rule_form(self))
E                   result = evaluate_relief_formula_record(record, _phase6_registry_sample_variables(self))
E                   text = f"公式有效：{result['primary_u']:.3f}×{result['primary_v']:.3f}"
E                   if result.get("secondary_u") is not None:
E                       text += f" + {result['secondary_u']:.3f}×{result['secondary_depth']:.3f}"
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(text)
E                   self._phase6_last_rule_form_result = result
E                   return result
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(f"公式錯誤：{exc}")
E                   self._phase6_last_rule_form_result = None
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_preview_2d(self):
E               result = _phase6_registry_validate_formula_form(self)
E               canvas = getattr(self, "relief_registry_preview_canvas", None)
E               if canvas is None:
E                   return result
E               canvas.delete("all")
E               canvas.create_rectangle(15, 15, 225, 145, outline="#777")
E               if not result:
E                   return None
E               pu, pv = float(result["primary_u"]), float(result["primary_v"])
E               scale = min(180.0 / max(pu, 1.0), 95.0 / max(pv + float(result.get("secondary_depth") or 0), 1.0))
E               x0, y0 = 20.0, 140.0
E               canvas.create_rectangle(x0, y0 - pv * scale, x0 + pu * scale, y0, outline="#222", width=2)
E               if result.get("secondary_u") is not None:
E                   su, sd = float(result["secondary_u"]), float(result["secondary_depth"])
E                   canvas.create_rectangle(x0, y0 - (pv + sd) * scale, x0 + su * scale, y0 - pv * scale, outline="#222", width=2)
E               return result
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_candidate_form_is_current(self):
E               saved = getattr(self, "_phase6_registry_candidate_record", None)
E               if not isinstance(saved, Mapping):
E                   return False
E               try:
E                   current = _phase6_registry_collect_rule_form(self)
E               except Exception:
E                   return False
E               return deepcopy(dict(current)) == deepcopy(dict(saved))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_require_current_candidate(self):
E               candidate_id = str(getattr(self, "_phase6_registry_candidate_id", "") or "")
E               if not candidate_id:
E                   raise ValueError("請先儲存候選")
E               if not _phase6_registry_candidate_form_is_current(self):
E                   raise ValueError("表單已變更；請重新儲存候選後再驗證")
E               return candidate_id
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_save_candidate_form(self):
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import save_relief_rule_candidate
E               result = _phase6_registry_validate_formula_form(self)
E               if result is None:
E                   return None
E               try:
E                   record = _phase6_registry_collect_rule_form(self)
E                   item = save_relief_rule_candidate(record)
E                   self._phase6_registry_candidate_id = item["candidate_id"]
E                   self._phase6_registry_candidate_record = deepcopy(dict(record))
E                   self._phase6_registry_regression_evidence = {"candidate_id": item["candidate_id"]}
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set("候選已儲存（尚未認證）")
E                   return item
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(f"候選儲存失敗：{exc}")
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_run_formula_matrix(self):
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import evaluate_relief_formula_record, _validate_editable_rule_record
E               try:
E                   candidate_id = _phase6_registry_require_current_candidate(self)
E                   record = _validate_editable_rule_record(_phase6_registry_collect_rule_form(self))
E                   base = _phase6_registry_sample_variables(self)
E                   samples = []
E                   for t in (max(0.5, base["T"] * 0.75), base["T"], base["T"] * 1.25):
E                       for fw in (max(t * 2, base["FW"] * 0.8), base["FW"], base["FW"] * 1.2):
E                           variables = dict(base, T=t, FW=fw)
E                           evaluate_relief_formula_record(record, variables)
E                           samples.append(variables)
E                   evidence = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_registry_regression_evidence", {}) or {})
E                   evidence.update({"matrix_passed": True, "cases": len(samples), "candidate_id": candidate_id})
E                   self._phase6_registry_regression_evidence = evidence
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(
E                       f"公式矩陣通過：{len(samples)} cases；3D零穿透=" + ("是" if evidence.get("zero_penetration") else "尚未")
E                   )
E                   return evidence
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(f"回歸失敗：{exc}")
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_validate_candidate_3d(self, record, *, candidate_id):
E               """Shadow-validate exactly one editable candidate without mutating the registry."""
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import evaluate_editable_endcap_rule_record, _validate_editable_rule_record
E               from ae_engine.assembly_collision import solve_world_backprojected_endcap_relief
E           
E               candidate_id = str(candidate_id or "").strip()
E               if not candidate_id:
E                   raise ValueError("candidate-specific 3D validation requires candidate_id")
E               raw = _validate_editable_rule_record(record)
E               intent = str(raw.get("assembly_intent") or "").strip()
E               current_intent = str(getattr(getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", None), "value", getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", "")) or "")
E               if current_intent and intent and current_intent != intent:
E                   raise ValueError(f"candidate assembly_intent={intent} does not match current assembly={current_intent}")
E           
E               callback = getattr(self, "_scene_query_callback", None)
E               if callback is None:
E                   raise RuntimeError("3D final-scene provider is not connected")
E               available = set(getattr(getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None), "available_parts", ()) or ())
E               if "box_body" not in available:
E                   raise ValueError("candidate-specific 3D validation requires box_body")
E           
E               def _base_part(part_key):
E                   payload = _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, part_key)
E                   payload["_use_committed_relief"] = False
E                   render_data = callback(part_key, payload)
E                   if render_data is None or getattr(render_data, "material", None) is None:
E                       raise ValueError(f"candidate validation render data unavailable: {part_key}")
E                   x_profile, y_profile = _phase6_mesh_profiles_for_part(self, part_key, render_data.material)
E                   return render_data, tuple(dict(seg) for seg in x_profile), tuple(dict(seg) for seg in y_profile)
E           
E               body_render, body_x, _body_y = _base_part("box_body")
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               settings = dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {})
E               thickness = _num(settings.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2.0)), 2.0)
E               dims = _phase6_operator_finished_dimensions(self)
E               clearance = _phase6_assembly_relief_clearance(self)
E               solutions = {}
E               validated_parts = []
E               for part_key in ("head", "tail"):
E                   if part_key not in available:
E                       continue
E                   end_render, end_x, end_y = _base_part(part_key)
E                   ephemeral = evaluate_editable_endcap_rule_record(
E                       raw,
E                       endcap_render_data=end_render,
E                       box_body_x_profile=body_x,
E                       endcap_x_profile=end_x,
E                       endcap_y_profile=end_y,
E                       sheet_thickness=thickness,
E                   )
E                   if ephemeral is None:
E                       raise ValueError(f"candidate formula does not apply to {part_key}")
E                   solution = solve_world_backprojected_endcap_relief(
E                       box_body_render_data=body_render,
E                       endcap_render_data=end_render,
E                       box_body_x_profile=body_x,
E                       endcap_x_profile=end_x,
E                       endcap_y_profile=end_y,
E                       finished_dimensions=dims,
E                       endcap_placement=_PHASE6_ASSEMBLY_PLACEMENTS.get(part_key, "top" if part_key == "head" else "bottom"),
E                       sheet_thickness=thickness,
E                       clearance=clearance,
E                       assembly_intent=intent,
E                       cabinet_family=_phase6_current_cabinet_family(self),
E                       allow_3d_fallback=False,
E                       certified_result_override=ephemeral,
E                   )
E                   validated_parts.append(part_key)
E                   solutions[part_key] = {
E                       "verified": bool(getattr(solution, "verified", False)),
E                       "trust_level": str(getattr(solution, "trust_level", "") or ""),
E                       "rule_id": getattr(solution, "rule_id", None),
E                       "rule_revision": getattr(solution, "rule_revision", None),
E                       "residual_pair_count": int(getattr(getattr(solution, "residual_projection", None), "pair_count", 0) or 0),
E                   }
E           
E               zero = bool(validated_parts) and all(bool(item["verified"]) for item in solutions.values())
E               return {
E                   "candidate_specific": True,
E                   "candidate_id": candidate_id,
E                   "zero_penetration": bool(zero),
E                   "validated_parts": validated_parts,
E                   "solutions": solutions,
E               }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_preview_assembly_3d(self):
E               try:
E                   candidate_id = _phase6_registry_require_current_candidate(self)
E                   record = deepcopy(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_registry_candidate_record", {}) or {}))
E                   evidence3d = _phase6_registry_validate_candidate_3d(
E                       self, record, candidate_id=candidate_id
E                   )
E                   evidence = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_registry_regression_evidence", {}) or {})
E                   evidence.update(dict(evidence3d or {}))
E                   self._phase6_registry_regression_evidence = evidence
E                   zero = bool(evidence.get("zero_penetration"))
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(
E                       "候選專屬組合3D驗證：" + ("零非法穿透" if zero else "仍有非法穿透")
E                   )
E                   return zero
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(f"組合3D驗證失敗：{exc}")
E                   return False
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_promote_form(self):
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import promote_relief_rule_candidate
E               try:
E                   candidate_id = _phase6_registry_require_current_candidate(self)
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(str(exc))
E                   return None
E               evidence = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_registry_regression_evidence", {}) or {})
E               try:
E                   promoted = promote_relief_rule_candidate(candidate_id, regression_evidence=evidence)
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(f"已認證新版次：{promoted['revision']}")
E                   _phase6_registry_refresh_rule_tree(self)
E                   return promoted
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   self.relief_registry_status_var.set(f"不可認證：{exc}")
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_refresh_rule_tree(self):
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import load_external_relief_rule_records
E               tree = getattr(self, "relief_registry_rule_tree", None)
E               if tree is None:
E                   return ()
E               for item in tree.get_children():
E                   tree.delete(item)
E               rows = load_external_relief_rule_records()
E               active_rows = [row for row in rows if bool(row.get("active", True))]
E               for row in active_rows:
E                   tree.insert("", "end", iid=f"{row['rule_id']}@{row['revision']}", values=(
E                       _phase6_operator_label(row["rule_id"]), row["revision"], _phase6_operator_label(row.get("trust_level", "")),
E                       _phase6_operator_label(row.get("assembly_intent", "")), row.get("topology_levels", ""),
E                   ))
E               self._phase6_registry_rule_records = {f"{row['rule_id']}@{row['revision']}": row for row in rows}
E               return rows
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_registry_rule_selected(self, *_args):
E               tree = getattr(self, "relief_registry_rule_tree", None)
E               if tree is None or not tree.selection():
E                   return
E               key = tree.selection()[0]
E               raw = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_registry_rule_records", {}).get(key) or {})
E               if not raw:
E                   return
E               formula = dict(raw.get("formula", {}) or {})
E               self.relief_registry_rule_name_var.set(_phase6_operator_label(raw.get("rule_id", "")))
E               setters = (
E                   (self.relief_registry_rule_id_var, raw.get("rule_id", "")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_family_var, raw.get("cabinet_family", "ANY")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_part_role_var, raw.get("part_role", "HEAD_OR_TAIL")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_joint_face_var, raw.get("joint_face", "TOP")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_intent_var, raw.get("assembly_intent", "INSERT_OVERLAY")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_topology_var, raw.get("topology_levels", 2)),
E                   (self.relief_registry_primary_u_var, formula.get("primary_u", "")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_primary_v_var, formula.get("primary_v", "")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_secondary_u_var, formula.get("secondary_u", "")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_secondary_depth_var, formula.get("secondary_depth", "")),
E                   (self.relief_registry_preconditions_var, ",".join(str(v) for v in (raw.get("preconditions", ()) or ()))),
E                   (self.relief_registry_source_var, str(raw.get("source", ""))),
E               )
E               for var, value in setters:
E                   var.set(str(value))
E               sig = list(raw.get("joint_signature", ()) or ())
E               extra = sig[1].get("relation") if len(sig) > 1 else "NONE"
E               self.relief_registry_extra_joint_var.set(str(extra))
E               if len(sig) > 1:
E                   self.relief_registry_extra_target_role_var.set(str(sig[1].get("target_role", "REAR_PANEL")))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_joint_form_refresh(self):
E               tree = getattr(self, "relief_joint_tree", None)
E               if tree is None:
E                   return ()
E               rows = _phase6_joint_rows(self)
E               for item in tree.get_children():
E                   tree.delete(item)
E               for row in rows:
E                   tree.insert("", "end", iid=str(row["joint_id"]), values=(
E                       _phase6_operator_label(row.get("subject_part", "")), _phase6_operator_label(row.get("target_part", "")),
E                       _phase6_operator_label(row.get("relation", "")), _phase6_operator_label(row.get("source", "")),
E                       _phase6_operator_label(row.get("subject_region", "")), _phase6_operator_label(row.get("target_region", "")),
E                   ))
E               return rows
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_joint_form_add(self):
E               try:
E                   row = _phase6_add_user_joint(
E                       self,
E                       subject_part=self.relief_joint_subject_var.get(),
E                       target_part=self.relief_joint_target_var.get(),
E                       relation=self.relief_joint_relation_var.get(),
E                       subject_region=self.relief_joint_subject_region_var.get(),
E                       target_region=self.relief_joint_target_region_var.get(),
E                       clearance_policy=self.relief_joint_clearance_var.get(),
E                       solver_constraints={"topology_levels": int(self.relief_joint_topology_var.get())},
E                   )
E                   self.relief_joint_status_var.set(
E                       ("外側包覆：主動板件包覆接合板件；" if row["relation"] == "WRAP" else "") + "已新增接合規則"
E                   )
E                   _phase6_joint_form_refresh(self)
E                   return row
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   self.relief_joint_status_var.set(f"新增失敗：{exc}")
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_joint_form_delete(self):
E               tree = getattr(self, "relief_joint_tree", None)
E               if tree is None or not tree.selection():
E                   self.relief_joint_status_var.set("請先選擇接合規則")
E                   return False
E               joint_id = tree.selection()[0]
E               try:
E                   result = _phase6_delete_user_joint(self, joint_id)
E                   self.relief_joint_status_var.set("已刪除" if result else "找不到接合規則")
E                   _phase6_joint_form_refresh(self)
E                   return result
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   self.relief_joint_status_var.set(f"不可刪除：{exc}")
E                   return False
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_open_relief_registry_form(self):
E               existing = getattr(self, "relief_registry_window", None)
E               try:
E                   if existing is not None and existing.winfo_exists():
E                       existing.deiconify(); existing.lift(); return existing
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               win = original.tk.Toplevel(self.root)
E               win.title("PHASE6 截角資料庫 / 組合接合")
E               win.geometry("1120x720")
E               self.relief_registry_window = win
E               notebook = original.ttk.Notebook(win)
E               notebook.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True, padx=8, pady=8)
E               self.relief_registry_notebook = notebook
E           
E               rules_tab = original.ttk.Frame(notebook, padding=8)
E               joints_tab = original.ttk.Frame(notebook, padding=8)
E               notebook.add(rules_tab, text="截角公式")
E               notebook.add(joints_tab, text="組合接合")
E           
E               # ----- Rules tab -----
E               left = original.ttk.Frame(rules_tab); left.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, fill=original.tk.Y, padx=(0, 8))
E               cols = ("id", "rev", "trust", "intent", "topology")
E               tree = original.ttk.Treeview(left, columns=cols, show="headings", height=24)
E               widths = {"id":280,"rev":45,"trust":105,"intent":120,"topology":55}
E               labels = {"id":"規則名稱","rev":"版次","trust":"認證狀態","intent":"組合方式","topology":"級數"}
E               for col in cols:
E                   tree.heading(col, text=labels[col]); tree.column(col, width=widths[col], stretch=(col=="id"))
E               tree.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True)
E               tree.bind("<<TreeviewSelect>>", lambda _e: _phase6_registry_rule_selected(self))
E               self.relief_registry_rule_tree = tree
E           
E               form = original.ttk.Frame(rules_tab); form.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True)
E               vars_defaults = {
E                   "rule_id":"USER_RULE_001", "rule_name":"自訂截角規則", "family":"ANY", "part_role":"HEAD_OR_TAIL", "joint_face":"TOP",
E                   "intent":"INSERT_OVERLAY", "topology":"2", "target_role":"BOX_SIDE", "extra_joint":"NONE",
E                   "extra_target_role":"REAR_PANEL", "primary_u":"side_fold + FW", "primary_v":"ytop1 + FW - T",
E                   "secondary_u":"side_fold + 0.5*T", "secondary_depth":"2*T",
E                   "preconditions":"ytop1_present,x_folded", "symmetry":"MIRROR_IF_GEOMETRY_SYMMETRIC", "source":"",
E                   "sample_t":"2", "sample_fw":"25", "sample_side":"15", "sample_ytop":"16", "sample_mating":"50",
E               }
E               for name, default in vars_defaults.items():
E                   setattr(self, f"relief_registry_{name}_var", original.tk.StringVar(value=default))
E               self.relief_registry_status_var = original.tk.StringVar(value="請先驗證公式")
E           
E               row = 0
E               def entry(label, var, width=28):
E                   nonlocal row
E                   original.ttk.Label(form, text=label).grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="w", padx=3, pady=2)
E                   widget = original.ttk.Entry(form, textvariable=var, width=width)
E                   widget.grid(row=row, column=1, columnspan=3, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=2); row += 1
E                   return widget
E               original.ttk.Label(form, text="規則名稱").grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="w", padx=3, pady=2)
E               original.ttk.Entry(
E                   form, textvariable=self.relief_registry_rule_name_var, width=28, state="readonly"
E               ).grid(row=row, column=1, columnspan=3, sticky="ew", padx=3, pady=2)
E               row += 1
E               original.ttk.Label(form, text="盤體條件").grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="w",padx=3,pady=2)
E               _phase6_form_choice(form,self.relief_registry_family_var,("ANY","金庫型","受電箱"),width=14).grid(row=row,column=1,sticky="w")
E               original.ttk.Label(form, text="板件角色").grid(row=row,column=2,sticky="e")
E               _phase6_form_choice(form,self.relief_registry_part_role_var,("HEAD_OR_TAIL","HEAD","TAIL"),width=18).grid(row=row,column=3,sticky="ew"); row+=1
E               original.ttk.Label(form,text="截角／接合位置").grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="w")
E               _phase6_form_choice(form,self.relief_registry_joint_face_var,("TOP","BOTTOM"),width=18).grid(row=row,column=1,sticky="ew")
E               original.ttk.Label(form,text="組合方式").grid(row=row,column=2,sticky="e")
E               _phase6_form_choice(form,self.relief_registry_intent_var,("INSERT","OVERLAY","INSERT_OVERLAY"),width=16).grid(row=row,column=3,sticky="ew"); row+=1
E               original.ttk.Label(form,text="截角級數").grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="w")
E               _phase6_form_choice(form,self.relief_registry_topology_var,("1","2"),width=8).grid(row=row,column=1,sticky="w")
E               original.ttk.Label(form,text="主要接合對象").grid(row=row,column=2,sticky="e")
E               _phase6_form_choice(form,self.relief_registry_target_role_var,("BOX_SIDE","REAR_PANEL"),width=18).grid(row=row,column=3,sticky="ew"); row+=1
E               original.ttk.Label(form,text="附加接合方式").grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="w")
E               _phase6_form_choice(form,self.relief_registry_extra_joint_var,("NONE","WRAP","INSERT","OVERLAY","INSERT_OVERLAY"),width=16).grid(row=row,column=1,sticky="ew")
E               original.ttk.Label(form,text="附加接合對象").grid(row=row,column=2,sticky="e")
E               _phase6_form_choice(form,self.relief_registry_extra_target_role_var,("REAR_PANEL","BOX_SIDE"),width=18).grid(row=row,column=3,sticky="ew"); row+=1
E               # Formulas and preconditions keep stable evaluator tokens internally while
E               # the operator edits Traditional-Chinese aliases.
E               for raw_name in ("primary_u", "primary_v", "secondary_u", "secondary_depth"):
E                   raw_var = getattr(self, f"relief_registry_{raw_name}_var")
E                   display_var = original.tk.StringVar(master=form)
E                   _phase6_bind_translated_var(raw_var, display_var, _phase6_formula_display, _phase6_formula_raw)
E                   setattr(self, f"relief_registry_{raw_name}_display_var", display_var)
E               self.relief_registry_preconditions_display_var = original.tk.StringVar(master=form)
E               _phase6_bind_translated_var(
E                   self.relief_registry_preconditions_var, self.relief_registry_preconditions_display_var,
E                   _phase6_preconditions_display, _phase6_preconditions_raw,
E               )
E               entry("第一級 X 公式", self.relief_registry_primary_u_display_var)
E               entry("第一級 Y 公式", self.relief_registry_primary_v_display_var)
E               entry("第二級 X 公式", self.relief_registry_secondary_u_display_var)
E               entry("第二級深度公式", self.relief_registry_secondary_depth_display_var)
E               entry("適用條件", self.relief_registry_preconditions_display_var)
E               entry("公式來源／備註", self.relief_registry_source_var)
E           
E               help_box = original.ttk.LabelFrame(form, text="公式變數說明", padding=6)
E               help_box.grid(row=row, column=0, columnspan=4, sticky="ew", pady=(5, 3)); row += 1
E               help_lines = (
E                   "板厚：目前板件厚度。",
E                   "名義框寬：封頭／封尾自身的框寬參數；不等於箱身成型後實際占位。",
E                   "側折：封頭／封尾 X 向側邊折彎基底；貼外沒有 X 折時為 0。",
E                   "上折：封頭／封尾 Y 向第一折尺寸。",
E                   "成型接合寬：接合對象折好後真正需要避讓的寬度，例如貼外取箱身成型框寬。",
E                   "第一級 X/Y：主要截角的 X/Y 切除量；第二級 X/深度：二級截角的內側位置與深度。",
E                   "嵌入、貼外、嵌入貼外、外側包覆皆以同一接合語意資料層保存。",
E               )
E               for help_row, text in enumerate(help_lines):
E                   original.ttk.Label(help_box, text=text, wraplength=660, justify="left").grid(
E                       row=help_row, column=0, sticky="w", pady=1
E                   )

E               sample = original.ttk.LabelFrame(form,text="即時公式預覽",padding=5); sample.grid(row=row,column=0,columnspan=4,sticky="ew",pady=5); row+=1
E               for col,(label,var) in enumerate((("板厚",self.relief_registry_sample_t_var),("名義框寬",self.relief_registry_sample_fw_var),("側折",self.relief_registry_sample_side_var),("上折",self.relief_registry_sample_ytop_var),("成型接合寬",self.relief_registry_sample_mating_var))):
E                   original.ttk.Label(sample,text=label).grid(row=0,column=col*2,sticky="e")
E                   original.ttk.Entry(sample,textvariable=var,width=7).grid(row=0,column=col*2+1,sticky="w",padx=(2,6))
E               canvas=original.tk.Canvas(form,width=245,height=160,background="white",highlightthickness=1,highlightbackground="#aaa")
E               canvas.grid(row=row,column=0,columnspan=4,sticky="w",pady=4); self.relief_registry_preview_canvas=canvas; row+=1
E               actions=original.ttk.Frame(form); actions.grid(row=row,column=0,columnspan=4,sticky="ew",pady=4); row+=1

E               _phase6_form_choice(jf,self.relief_joint_topology_var,("1","2"),width=8).grid(row=3,column=4,sticky="w",padx=3)
E               buttons=original.ttk.Frame(joints_tab); buttons.pack(fill=original.tk.X,pady=6)
E               original.ttk.Button(buttons,text="新增接合",command=lambda:_phase6_joint_form_add(self)).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT,padx=2)
E               original.ttk.Button(buttons,text="刪除使用者接合",command=lambda:_phase6_joint_form_delete(self)).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT,padx=2)
E               original.ttk.Label(buttons,textvariable=self.relief_joint_status_var).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT,padx=10)
E           
E               _phase6_registry_refresh_rule_tree(self)
E               _phase6_joint_form_refresh(self)
E               return win

E           def _phase6_hide_original_visual_controls(root_widget):
E               for child in root_widget.winfo_children():
E                   try:
E                       text = str(child.cget("text"))
E                   except Exception:
E                       text = ""
E                   if "3D 視覺調整" in text:
E                       manager = child.winfo_manager()
E                       if manager == "pack":
E                           child.pack_forget()
E                       elif manager == "grid":
E                           child.grid_remove()
E                       return True
E               return False
E

E           def _phase6_toggle_fullscreen(self):
E               root = self.root
E               enabled = bool(getattr(self, "_phase6_fullscreen", False))
E               if not enabled:
E                   try:
E                       self._phase6_restore_geometry = root.geometry()
E                   except Exception:
E                       self._phase6_restore_geometry = None
E                   applied = False
E                   try:
E                       root.state("zoomed")
E                       applied = True
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   if not applied:
E                       try:
E                           root.attributes("-zoomed", True)
E                           applied = True
E                       except Exception:
E                           pass
E                   if not applied:
E                       try:
E                           root.attributes("-fullscreen", True)
E                           applied = True
E                       except Exception:
E                           pass
E                   self._phase6_fullscreen = bool(applied)
E               else:
E                   restored = False
E                   try:
E                       root.attributes("-fullscreen", False)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   try:
E                       root.attributes("-zoomed", False)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   try:
E                       root.state("normal")
E                       restored = True
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   geometry = getattr(self, "_phase6_restore_geometry", None)
E                   if restored and geometry:
E                       try:
E                           root.geometry(geometry)
E                       except Exception:
E                           pass
E                   self._phase6_fullscreen = False
E               button = getattr(self, "fullscreen_button", None)
E               if button is not None:
E                   button.configure(text=("還原視窗" if self._phase6_fullscreen else "全螢幕"))
E               return self._phase6_fullscreen
E           
E

E               # BOTTOM WRAP is selected through the canonical 組合方式 preset (包覆貼外).
E               # Do not expose a second enable/disable control in the permanent 3D row.

E               _phase6_refresh_persistent_structure_controls(self)

E           def _phase6_return_to_2d_corner(self):
E               """Commit current live draft and hand control back to the main 2D corner view."""
E               callback = getattr(self, "_return_2d_callback", None)
E               if callback is None:
E                   return False
E               if getattr(self, "_phase6_pending_settings", None):
E                   self.flush_pending_settings()
E               if getattr(getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None), "active_part", None) is not None:
E                   self._save_current_part()
E               _phase6_publish_live_state(self, force=True)
E               key = str(getattr(self.designer_workspace, "active_part", "") or "box_body")
E               callback(key)
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_persistent_top_area(self):
E               """固定版面：最上列命令；其下兩行全域設定；左右工作區。"""
E               try:
E                   self.left.pack_forget()
E                   self.right.pack_forget()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E               self.top_persistent_bar = original.ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=(10, 8, 10, 4))
E               self.top_persistent_bar.pack(side=original.tk.TOP, fill=original.tk.X)
E           
E               self.top_command_row = original.ttk.Frame(self.top_persistent_bar)
E               self.top_command_row.pack(fill=original.tk.X)
E               _phase6_build_project_toolbar(self, self.top_command_row)
E               _phase6_build_transaction_buttons(self, self.top_command_row)
E           
E               self.top_settings_row = original.ttk.Frame(self.top_persistent_bar)
E               self.top_settings_row.pack(fill=original.tk.X, pady=(5, 0))
E               self.top_global_host = original.ttk.Frame(self.top_settings_row)
E               self.top_global_host.pack(fill=original.tk.X, expand=True)
E               panel = _phase6_ensure_settings_panel(self)
E               panel.build_left_global_controls(
E                   self.top_global_host,
E                   baseline_models=tuple(self._baseline_models),
E                   initial_model=self._phase6_baseline_initial_model,
E               )
E               _phase6_sync_settings_panel_compat(self)
E               _phase6_build_global_persistent_controls(self)
E           
E               # 第一列固定順序：檔案 → 3D 顯示 → 全螢幕；交易按鈕固定最右。
E               _phase6_build_visual_controls(self, self.top_command_row)
E               self.fullscreen_button = original.ttk.Button(
E                   self.top_command_row, text="全螢幕", command=lambda: _phase6_toggle_fullscreen(self)
E               )
E               self.fullscreen_button.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 4))
E               self.return_2d_button = original.ttk.Button(
E                   self.top_command_row, text="回2D截角", command=lambda: _phase6_return_to_2d_corner(self),
E               )
E               self.return_2d_button.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 8))
E               if getattr(self, "_return_2d_callback", None) is None:
E                   self.return_2d_button.configure(state="disabled")
E               _phase6_sync_settings_panel_compat(self)
E           
E               self.left.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, fill=original.tk.Y)
E               self.right.pack(side=original.tk.RIGHT, fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True)
E

E           def _phase6_reset_initial_values(self):
E               """Restore the immutable AE factory defaults inside the 3D transaction.
E           
E               The source is ``ae_engine.ae.default_config`` captured by the main GUI when
E               開啟此設計器時的工作區內容。基準型號與手動截角交易資料不屬於
E               of that mapping and therefore remain untouched.
E               """
E               self.flush_pending_settings()
E               factory = dict(getattr(self, "_factory_defaults", {}) or {})
E               if not factory:
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                       self.settings_status_var.set("找不到程式初始值")
E                   return False
E           
E               clean = {}
E               for key, raw in factory.items():
E                   if key not in self._settings_values:
E                       continue
E                   if isinstance(self._settings_values.get(key), bool):
E                       clean[key] = bool(raw)
E                   else:
E                       try:
E                           clean[key] = float(raw)
E                       except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                           continue
E               if not clean:
E                   return False
E           
E               self._phase6_pending_settings = {}
E               self._settings_values.update(clean)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot.update(clean)
E               if "t" in clean:
E                   self.state.phase6_thickness = float(clean["t"])
E               for key in ("w", "h", "d"):
E                   if key in clean:
E                       self._phase6_box_whd[key] = _ui_len(clean[key])
E           
E               # Recreate the standard profiles rather than merging into the edited ones;
E               # this is what makes added/changed fold segments truly return to defaults.
E               _phase6_recalculate_part_dimensions(self)
E               reset_snapshot = self._phase6_input_snapshot
E               self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"] = build_box_body_profile(reset_snapshot)
E               for part_key in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   if part_key == "box_body":
E                       continue
E                   defaults = (
E                       build_endcap_xy_profiles(reset_snapshot, part_key=part_key)
E                       if part_key in {"head", "tail"}
E                       else build_standard_part_profiles(reset_snapshot, part_key)
E                   )
E                   self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(part_key, defaults)
E           
E               active = self.designer_workspace.active_part
E               if active == "box_body":
E                   self.state.phase6_fold_ui_profiles = {"X": self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"]}
E               elif active in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   profiles = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(active, {}) or {}
E                   self.state.profiles["X"] = clone_profile(profiles.get("X", []))
E                   self.state.profiles["Y"] = clone_profile(profiles.get("Y", []))
E           
E               self._phase6_settings_guard = True
E               try:
E                   if "w" in clean:
E                       self.v_w.set(_setting_number_text(clean["w"]))
E                   if "h" in clean:
E                       self.v_h.set(_setting_number_text(clean["h"]))
E                   if "d" in clean:
E                       self.v_d.set(_setting_number_text(clean["d"]))
E                   for key, var in getattr(self, "left_global_vars", {}).items():
E                       if key not in clean:
E                           continue
E                       if isinstance(var, original.tk.BooleanVar):
E                           var.set(bool(clean[key]))
E                       else:
E                           var.set(_setting_number_text(clean[key]))
E                   for key, var in getattr(self, "setting_vars", {}).items():
E                       if key not in clean:
E                           continue
E                       if isinstance(var, original.tk.BooleanVar):
E                           var.set(bool(clean[key]))
E                       else:
E                           var.set(_setting_number_text(clean[key]))
E               finally:
E                   self._phase6_settings_guard = False
E           
E               self.designer_workspace.mark_dirty()
E               try:
E                   self.bend_ui.render()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               try:
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_panel"):
E                       self.settings_panel.refresh_baseline_data()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               try:
E                   self.do_update()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                   self.settings_status_var.set("已還原程式初始值並同步主畫面")
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_save_settings_context_as_defaults(self, context):
E               self.flush_pending_settings()
E               if self._save_defaults_callback is None:
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                       self.settings_status_var.set("未連接預設值儲存器")
E                   return False
E               try:
E                   self._save_defaults_callback(
E                       context,
E                       dict(self._settings_values),
E                       deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {})),
E                       deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_pair_same", {})),
E                   )
E               except Exception as exc:
E                   if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                       self.settings_status_var.set(f"儲存失敗：{exc}")
E                   return False
E               if hasattr(self, "settings_status_var"):
E                   self.settings_status_var.set("已儲存到 config.ini")
E               return True
E

E           def _phase6_scene_from_structural_result(result, features, surface_id):
E               from ae_engine.sheetmetal_drawing import DrawingScene, structural_result_to_primitives, resolved_features_to_primitives
E               from ae_engine.sheetmetal_features import feature_surface_from_structural_result, resolve_surface_features
E           
E               scene = DrawingScene()
E               scene.extend(structural_result_to_primitives(result))
E               if features:
E                   surface = feature_surface_from_structural_result(surface_id, result)
E                   scene.extend(resolved_features_to_primitives(
E                       resolve_surface_features(surface, list(features), float(result.width), float(result.height))
E                   ))
E               return scene
E

E           
E           def _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, part_key):
E               """Build draft PartSpec inputs for any saved Phase6 part without switching UI."""
E               key = str(part_key or "")
E               if not key:
E                   return {}
E               values = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               values.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               values.update(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_whd", {}) or {}))
E               try:
E                   if key == "box_body":
E                       profile = list((getattr(self.state, "profiles_vault", {}) or {}).get("箱身", ()) or ())
E                       values.update(read_box_body_profile(profile, values))
E                       values["fold_profile"] = clone_profile(profile)
E                   else:
E                       if key == self.designer_workspace.active_part:
E                           profiles = getattr(self.state, "profiles", {}) or {}
E                       else:
E                           profiles = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(key, {}) or {}
E                       if key in {"head", "tail"}:
E                           values.update(read_endcap_xy_profiles(profiles, values))
E                           values["box_body_profile"] = clone_profile(
E                               (getattr(self.state, "profiles_vault", {}) or {}).get("箱身", ()) or ()
E                           )
E                           values["fold_profiles"] = {
E                               "X": clone_profile(profiles.get("X", ())),
E                               "Y": clone_profile(profiles.get("Y", ())),
E                           }
E                       else:
E                           values.update(read_standard_part_profiles(key, profiles, values))
E               except Exception:
E                   # An in-progress row can be incomplete. Keep the last valid snapshot
E                   # values rather than inventing geometry.
E                   pass
E               model_var = getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None)
E               values["model"] = str(
E                   model_var.get() if model_var is not None
E                   else getattr(self, "_phase6_baseline_initial_model", "") or ""
E               ).strip()
E               values["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", normalize_endcap_fw_state(values)))
E               values["endcap_bottom_wrap"] = deepcopy(
E                   getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state", normalize_endcap_bottom_wrap_state(values))
E               )
E               if key in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS:
E                   values["fw"] = resolve_endcap_fw(values, key, state=values["endcap_fw"])
E               values["corner_state"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {}) or {})
E               # Normal single-part 3D must replay the same already-committed dynamic
E               # relief that 2D/DXF use. Assembly probing explicitly disables this flag so
E               # it can restore/probe the physical corner before deriving a new cut.
E               values["_use_committed_relief"] = True
E               values["features"] = self.designer_workspace.features_for(key)
E               values["face_features"] = self.designer_workspace.face_features_for(key)
E               values["box_body_structure"] = self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state()
E               source_graph = migrate_legacy_snapshot_joints(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}))
E               values["assembly_joint_schema_version"] = source_graph.get("assembly_joint_schema_version")
E               values["assembly_joints"] = deepcopy(source_graph.get("assembly_joints", ()))
E               if key in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS and cabinet_family_policy.supports_bottom_wrap_controls(values):
E                   try:
E                       item = resolve_endcap_bottom_wrap(
E                           values, key, state=values["endcap_bottom_wrap"]
E                       )
E                       # Relation is graph-owned. Family state contributes only
E                       # adjustable geometric reserves to the certified formula.
E                       structure = cabinet_family_policy.set_bottom_relief_reserves(
E                           values,
E                           values["box_body_structure"],
E                           reserve_u=item["reserve_u"],
E                           reserve_v=item["reserve_v"],
E                       )
E                       values["box_body_structure"] = structure
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E               if key == "box_body":
E                   for part_key, target in (("head", "head_ybottom1"), ("tail", "tail_ybottom1")):
E                       profiles = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(part_key, {}) or {}
E                       for row in list(dict(profiles).get("Y", ()) or ()):
E                           if str(row.get("phase6_key") or "") == "ybottom1":
E                               values[target] = float(engine_segment_length_to_ui(row))
E                               break
E               source = getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E               for name in (
E                   "indicator_layer_groups", "door_indicator_groups",
E                   "door_indicator_offset", "door_indicator_box_enabled",
E               ):
E                   if name not in values and name in source:
E                       values[name] = deepcopy(source[name])
E               return values
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_scene_query_payload(self):
E               """Return only current draft PartSpec inputs; no geometry is built here."""
E               return _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, self.designer_workspace.active_part)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_query_final_render_data(self):
E               """Return authoritative render data for the active sheet.
E           
E               Box Body / EndCaps and any sheet participating in a USER_ADDED Joint must
E               read the resolved assembly result so single-part 3D cannot drift from the
E               combined view.  Unrelated sheets (Door/Base Plate/etc.) have no assembly
E               relief dependency and should query their own manufacturing FinalScene
E               directly instead of forcing a whole-cabinet solve.
E               """
E               key = str(self.designer_workspace.active_part or "")
E               if not key:
E                   raise ValueError("no active part")
E           
E               snapshot = migrate_legacy_snapshot_joints(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}))
E               user_joint_parts = {
E                   str(raw.get(field) or "")
E                   for raw in tuple(snapshot.get("assembly_joints", ()) or ())
E                   if str(raw.get("source") or "") == AssemblyJointSource.USER_ADDED.value
E                   for field in ("subject_part", "target_part")
E               }
E               if key in {"box_body", "head", "tail"} or key in user_joint_parts:
E                   resolved = _phase6_resolve_manufacturing_geometry(self)
E                   return resolved.part(key).render_data
E           
E               callback = getattr(self, "_scene_query_callback", None)
E               if callback is None:
E                   raise RuntimeError("3D final-scene provider is not connected")
E               render_data = callback(key, _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, key))
E               if render_data is None:
E                   raise ValueError(f"manufacturing render data unavailable: {key}")
E               if getattr(render_data, "pieces", None):
E                   return render_data
E               if getattr(render_data, "scene", None) is None or getattr(render_data, "material", None) is None:
E                   raise TypeError("manufacturing render provider must return scene + material or physical pieces")
E               return render_data
E           
E           
E           
E           
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_active_mesh_profiles(self, material):
E               return _phase6_mesh_profiles_for_part(self, self.designer_workspace.active_part, material)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_mesh_profiles_for_part(self, part_key, material):
E               key = str(part_key or "")
E               if key == "box_body":
E                   x_profile = list((getattr(self.state, "profiles_vault", {}) or {}).get("箱身", ()) or ())
E                   minx, miny, maxx, maxy = material.bounds
E                   return x_profile, [{"len": float(maxy - miny)}]
E               if key == self.designer_workspace.active_part:
E                   profiles = getattr(self.state, "profiles", {}) or {}
E               else:
E                   profiles = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(key, {}) or {}
E               return list(profiles.get("X", ())), list(profiles.get("Y", ()))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_assembly_relief_clearance(self):
E               var = getattr(self, "assembly_relief_clearance_var", None)
E               raw = var.get() if var is not None else "0"
E               try:
E                   value = float(str(raw).strip() or "0")
E               except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                   value = 0.0
E               return max(0.0, value)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_make_assembly_scene_render_data(
E               *,
E               assembly_parts,
E               show_interference=False,
E               ignore_fixed_corner_relief=False,
E               interference_probe_parts=(),
E               joint_diagnostics=(),
E               selected_joint_id=None,
E               preserve_endcap_core_origin=False,
E           ):
E               """Construct the assembly bundle across old/new scene-view contracts.
E           
E               UPDATE packages may be applied over an older Phase6 tree.  Older
E               ``AssemblySceneRenderData`` constructors only accepted ``assembly_parts``
E               (and sometimes ``warnings``).  Filter optional keyword arguments against
E               the live constructor signature so a mixed-version install can still open
E               the assembly view instead of failing with ``unexpected keyword``.
E               """
E               from inspect import Parameter, signature
E           
E               values = {
E                   "assembly_parts": tuple(assembly_parts),
E                   "show_interference": bool(show_interference),
E                   "ignore_fixed_corner_relief": bool(ignore_fixed_corner_relief),
E                   "interference_probe_parts": tuple(interference_probe_parts or ()),
E                   "joint_diagnostics": tuple(joint_diagnostics or ()),
E                   "selected_joint_id": None if selected_joint_id is None else str(selected_joint_id),
E                   "preserve_endcap_core_origin": bool(preserve_endcap_core_origin),
E               }
E               try:
E                   params = signature(AssemblySceneRenderData).parameters
E               except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                   params = {}
E               accepts_kwargs = any(
E                   p.kind is Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for p in params.values()
E               )
E               if accepts_kwargs:
E                   kwargs = values
E               else:
E                   kwargs = {key: value for key, value in values.items() if key in params}
E                   kwargs.setdefault("assembly_parts", values["assembly_parts"])
E               return AssemblySceneRenderData(**kwargs)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_current_cabinet_family(self):
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               model_var = getattr(self, "baseline_model_var", None)
E               model = str(snapshot.get("model") or snapshot.get("cabinet_type") or "").strip()
E               try:
E                   live_model = str(model_var.get() or "").strip() if model_var is not None else ""
E               except Exception:
E                   live_model = ""
E               if live_model:
E                   model = live_model
E               try:
E                   from ae_engine.cabinet_types.registry import resolve_cabinet_type
E                   return resolve_cabinet_type(model).canonical_name
E               except Exception:
E                   # 自訂/舊基準型號仍可命中 cabinet_family=ANY 的已認證 Assembly 規則。
E                   return model or "金庫型"
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_solution_is_committable(solution):
E               if bool(getattr(solution, "verified", False)):
E                   return True
E               trust = str(getattr(solution, "trust_level", "") or "")
E               return bool(getattr(solution, "rule_id", None)) and trust in {
E                   "CERTIFIED", "CERTIFIED_FROM_3D", "ENGINE_CONFLICT"
E               }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_apply_resolved_cut_to_part(part, cut_polygon):
E               """Apply an already-verified Joint relief to canonical FinalScene without rebuilding PartSpec."""
E               from ae_engine.assembly_collision import _scene_with_replaced_primary_cutting
E               from ae_engine.manufacturing_api import (
E                   PartRenderData, material_polygon_from_final_scene, fold_guides_from_final_scene,
E                   _scene_with_authoritative_fold_profiles,
E               )
E               material = getattr(part.render_data, "material", None)
E               if material is None or getattr(material, "is_empty", True):
E                   raise ValueError(f"canonical material unavailable: {part.part_key}")
E               solved = material.difference(cut_polygon)
E               if not solved.is_valid:
E                   solved = solved.buffer(0)
E               if solved.is_empty:
E                   raise ValueError(f"Joint relief removed all material: {part.part_key}")
E               scene = _scene_with_replaced_primary_cutting(part.render_data.scene, solved)
E               scene = _scene_with_authoritative_fold_profiles(
E                   scene, tuple(part.x_profile or ()), tuple(part.y_profile or ())
E               )
E               render = PartRenderData(
E                   scene=scene,
E                   material=material_polygon_from_final_scene(scene),
E                   fold_guides=fold_guides_from_final_scene(scene),
E                   metadata=dict(getattr(part.render_data, "metadata", {}) or {}),
E                   unfolded_topology=getattr(part.render_data, "unfolded_topology", None),
E               )
E               return AssemblyScenePart(
E                   part_key=part.part_key, render_data=render,
E                   x_profile=part.x_profile, y_profile=part.y_profile,
E                   placement=part.placement, offset=part.offset,
E               )
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_apply_resolved_cut_to_owner(part, owner_key, cut_polygon):
E               """Apply a verified cut to a canonical part or one physical Box Body piece.
E           
E               ``owner_key`` may be the canonical part key (``box_body``) or a physical
E               piece geometry key such as ``box_body:left_side``.  Mechanical ownership
E               remains on the canonical part; this adapter only selects the UV/material
E               carrier used by the solver.
E               """
E               canonical_key = str(part.part_key)
E               geometry_key = str(owner_key or canonical_key)
E               if geometry_key == canonical_key:
E                   return _phase6_apply_resolved_cut_to_part(part, cut_polygon)
E           
E               prefix = canonical_key + ":"
E               if not geometry_key.startswith(prefix):
E                   raise ValueError(f"geometry owner {geometry_key!r} is not under {canonical_key!r}")
E               role = geometry_key[len(prefix):].strip().lower()
E               structure = part.render_data
E               pieces = tuple(getattr(structure, "pieces", ()) or ())
E               if not pieces:
E                   raise ValueError(f"piece-level geometry unavailable: {geometry_key}")
E           
E               from dataclasses import replace
E               from ae_engine.manufacturing_api import _exploded_box_body_preview
E           
E               replaced = []
E               found = False
E               for piece in pieces:
E                   if str(getattr(piece, "role", "") or "").strip().lower() != role:
E                       replaced.append(piece)
E                       continue
E                   found = True
E                   material = piece.render_data.material
E                   minx, miny, maxx, maxy = map(float, material.bounds)
E                   temp_part = AssemblyScenePart(
E                       part_key=geometry_key,
E                       render_data=piece.render_data,
E                       x_profile=tuple(getattr(piece, "fold_profile", ()) or ()),
E                       y_profile=({"len": max(0.0, maxy - miny), "core": True},),
E                       placement=part.placement,
E                       offset=part.offset,
E                   )
E                   solved_piece_part = _phase6_apply_resolved_cut_to_part(temp_part, cut_polygon)
E                   replaced.append(replace(piece, render_data=solved_piece_part.render_data))
E               if not found:
E                   raise ValueError(f"unknown Box Body piece role: {role!r}")
E           
E               replaced = tuple(replaced)
E               preview = _exploded_box_body_preview(replaced)
E               solved_structure = replace(structure, pieces=replaced, preview_render_data=preview)
E               return AssemblyScenePart(
E                   part_key=part.part_key, render_data=solved_structure,
E                   x_profile=part.x_profile, y_profile=part.y_profile,
E                   placement=part.placement, offset=part.offset,
E               )
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_side_wrap_target_corners(preserve_part_key):
E               """Return the two physical target-piece end corners touched by an EndCap side WRAP."""
E               key = str(preserve_part_key or "").strip().lower()
E               if key == "head":
E                   return ("top_left", "top_right")
E               if key == "tail":
E                   return ("bottom_left", "bottom_right")
E               raise ValueError(f"side WRAP preserve part must be head/tail, got {preserve_part_key!r}")
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_box_body_piece_solver_key(body_part, region, *, require_flat_uv):
E               """Resolve a Box Body Joint region to one physical piece solver key.
E           
E               Multi-piece structures may expose an aggregate world solid for preserve-side
E               collision checks, but relief/backprojection requires one unambiguous piece UV
E               plane.  This adapter deliberately maps only stable physical region names.
E               """
E               pieces = tuple(getattr(getattr(body_part, "render_data", None), "pieces", ()) or ())
E               if not pieces:
E                   return "box_body"
E               by_role = {str(getattr(piece, "role", "") or "").strip().lower(): piece for piece in pieces}
E               normalized = str(region or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_").replace(" ", "_")
E           
E               role = None
E               if normalized in {"rear_mating", "rear_panel", "back", "back_panel", "rear", "outer_surface", "wrap_zone"}:
E                   role = "back"
E               elif normalized in {"left_mating", "left_mating_zone", "left_side", "left", "top_left", "bottom_left"}:
E                   role = "left_side"
E               elif normalized in {"right_mating", "right_mating_zone", "right_side", "right", "top_right", "bottom_right"}:
E                   role = "right_side"
E           
E               if role is not None and role in by_role:
E                   return f"box_body:{role}"
E               if require_flat_uv:
E                   raise ValueError(
E                       f"piece-level relief region is ambiguous for multi-piece Box Body: {region!r}"
E                   )
E               return "box_body"
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_joint_world_geometry(parts, finished_dimensions, sheet_thickness):
E               """Build Joint Solver v2 world/UV maps from canonical AssemblyScenePart objects.
E           
E               Multi-piece Box Body structures expose one world-space aggregate for preserve
E               checks plus one UV-aware entry per physical piece.  The aggregate intentionally
E               has no flat-material map: unrelated piece UV planes must never be forged into a
E               single backprojection coordinate system.
E               """
E               from ae_engine.assembly_geometry import (
E                   folded_mesh_with_flat_uv_from_polygon,
E                   world_skin_with_flat_uv, endcap_world_skin_with_flat_uv,
E                   place_assembly_triangles, place_assembly_points,
E                   place_box_body_structure_points, MappedSkinTriangle, _triangle_unit_normal,
E               )
E           
E               by_key = {str(part.part_key): part for part in tuple(parts or ())}
E               flat_material_by_part = {}
E               mapped_skin_triangles_by_part = {}
E               world_triangles_by_part = {}
E               body_world_mid = ()
E           
E               body = by_key.get("box_body")
E               if body is not None:
E                   body_pieces = tuple(getattr(body.render_data, "pieces", ()) or ())
E                   if body_pieces:
E                       total_w = max(float(getattr(piece, "formed_w_end", 0.0)) for piece in body_pieces)
E                       mapped_rows = []
E                       structure_mid = []
E                       piece_counts = []
E                       for piece in body_pieces:
E                           data = piece.render_data
E                           _minx, miny, _maxx, maxy = map(float, data.material.bounds)
E                           y_profile = ({"len": max(0.0, maxy - miny), "core": True},)
E                           x_profile = tuple(getattr(piece, "fold_profile", ()) or ())
E                           mapped = folded_mesh_with_flat_uv_from_polygon(
E                               data.material, x_profile, y_profile,
E                               fold_guides=tuple(getattr(data, "fold_guides", ()) or ()),
E                           )
E                           piece_structure = []
E                           for item in mapped:
E                               placed = place_box_body_structure_points(
E                                   item.local, piece, total_w=total_w,
E                                   thickness=sheet_thickness, x_profile=x_profile,
E                               )
E                               piece_structure.append(tuple(placed))
E                           mapped_rows.append((piece, tuple(mapped), tuple(piece_structure)))
E                           structure_mid.extend(piece_structure)
E                           piece_counts.append(len(piece_structure))
E           
E                       structure_mid = tuple(structure_mid)
E                       if structure_mid:
E                           all_points = [point for tri in structure_mid for point in tri]
E                           placed_points = place_assembly_points(
E                               all_points, structure_mid, body.placement,
E                               finished_dimensions, body.offset,
E                           )
E                           body_world_mid = tuple(
E                               tuple(placed_points[i:i + 3])
E                               for i in range(0, len(placed_points), 3)
E                           )
E           
E                       half = max(0.0, float(sheet_thickness or 0.0)) / 2.0
E                       cursor = 0
E                       aggregate_skins = []
E                       for piece, mapped, _piece_structure in mapped_rows:
E                           count = len(mapped)
E                           world_mid_piece = body_world_mid[cursor:cursor + count]
E                           cursor += count
E                           skins = []
E                           for item, world_mid in zip(mapped, world_mid_piece):
E                               normal = _triangle_unit_normal(world_mid)
E                               if normal is None:
E                                   continue
E                               for side in (-1, 1):
E                                   delta = tuple(float(side) * half * value for value in normal)
E                                   world = tuple(
E                                       tuple(float(point[i]) + delta[i] for i in range(3))
E                                       for point in world_mid
E                                   )
E                                   skins.append(MappedSkinTriangle(flat=item.flat, world=world, side=side))
E                           piece_key = f"box_body:{str(getattr(piece, 'role', '') or '').strip().lower()}"
E                           flat_material_by_part[piece_key] = piece.render_data.material
E                           mapped_skin_triangles_by_part[piece_key] = tuple(skins)
E                           world_triangles_by_part[piece_key] = tuple(item.world for item in skins)
E                           aggregate_skins.extend(item.world for item in skins)
E                       world_triangles_by_part["box_body"] = tuple(aggregate_skins)
E                   else:
E                       mapped = folded_mesh_with_flat_uv_from_polygon(
E                           body.render_data.material, tuple(body.x_profile or ()), tuple(body.y_profile or ()),
E                           fold_guides=tuple(getattr(body.render_data, "fold_guides", ()) or ()),
E                       )
E                       body_world_mid = place_assembly_triangles(
E                           tuple(item.local for item in mapped), body.placement, finished_dimensions, body.offset
E                       )
E                       skins = world_skin_with_flat_uv(
E                           mapped, body.placement, finished_dimensions, offset=body.offset,
E                           sheet_thickness=sheet_thickness,
E                       )
E                       flat_material_by_part["box_body"] = body.render_data.material
E                       mapped_skin_triangles_by_part["box_body"] = tuple(skins)
E                       world_triangles_by_part["box_body"] = tuple(item.world for item in skins)
E           
E               for key, part in by_key.items():
E                   if key == "box_body" or getattr(part.render_data, "pieces", None):
E                       continue
E                   mapped = folded_mesh_with_flat_uv_from_polygon(
E                       part.render_data.material, tuple(part.x_profile or ()), tuple(part.y_profile or ()),
E                       fold_guides=tuple(getattr(part.render_data, "fold_guides", ()) or ()),
E                   )
E                   placement = str(part.placement or "offset")
E                   if placement in {"top", "head", "bottom", "tail"} and body_world_mid:
E                       skins = endcap_world_skin_with_flat_uv(
