E                           mapped, placement, body_world_mid, offset=part.offset,
E                           sheet_thickness=sheet_thickness,
E                           reference_triangles=tuple(item.local for item in mapped),
E                           preserve_core_origin=True,
E                       )
E                   else:
E                       skins = world_skin_with_flat_uv(
E                           mapped, placement, finished_dimensions, offset=part.offset,
E                           sheet_thickness=sheet_thickness,
E                       )
E                   flat_material_by_part[key] = part.render_data.material
E                   mapped_skin_triangles_by_part[key] = tuple(skins)
E                   world_triangles_by_part[key] = tuple(item.world for item in skins)
E           
E               return {
E                   "flat_material_by_part": flat_material_by_part,
E                   "mapped_skin_triangles_by_part": mapped_skin_triangles_by_part,
E                   "world_triangles_by_part": world_triangles_by_part,
E               }
E           
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_joint_relief_state_item_matches(item, joint, source_material):
E               """Return True only when a persisted provisional cut still targets the same raw geometry."""
E               if not isinstance(item, dict):
E                   return False
E               relation = str(getattr(getattr(joint, "relation", None), "value", getattr(joint, "relation", "")))
E               if (
E                   str(item.get("joint_id") or "") != str(joint.joint_id)
E                   or str(item.get("subject_part") or "") != str(joint.subject_part)
E                   or str(item.get("target_part") or "") != str(joint.target_part)
E                   or str(item.get("relation") or "") != relation
E                   or not bool(item.get("verified"))
E               ):
E                   return False
E               try:
E                   saved_bounds = tuple(float(v) for v in item.get("source_material_bounds", ()))
E                   saved_area = float(item.get("source_material_area"))
E                   current_bounds = tuple(float(v) for v in source_material.bounds)
E                   current_area = float(source_material.area)
E               except Exception:
E                   return False
E               return len(saved_bounds) == 4 and all(abs(a-b) <= 1e-6 for a,b in zip(saved_bounds,current_bounds)) and abs(saved_area-current_area) <= 1e-6
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_cut_geometry_from_state_item(item):
E               from shapely.geometry import Polygon
E               from shapely.ops import unary_union
E               polygons = []
E               for coords in tuple((item or {}).get("cut_polygons", ()) or ()):
E                   try:
E                       polygon = Polygon([(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in coords])
E                   except Exception:
E                       continue
E                   if not polygon.is_valid:
E                       polygon = polygon.buffer(0)
E                   if not polygon.is_empty and float(polygon.area) > 1e-9:
E                       polygons.append(polygon)
E               return unary_union(polygons) if polygons else None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_resolve_explicit_joint_reliefs(
E               parts, joints, *, finished_dimensions, sheet_thickness, clearance=0.0,
E               committed_state=None,
E           ):
E               """Resolve USER_ADDED Joint reliefs against canonical parts.
E           
E               Safety rules:
E               - WRAP preserves the wrapper (subject) and may only cut the wrapped target.
E               - Persisted provisional cuts replay only when the raw relief-owner material
E                 fingerprint still matches; dimensional edits invalidate them.
E               - 3D discovery requires an explicit physical corner and topology contract.
E                 Generic mating regions remain diagnostic-only (UNFITTED_REGION).
E               - A discovered cut is committed only after replay proves zero illegal
E                 penetration.  No candidate is promoted to CERTIFIED here.
E               """
E               from types import SimpleNamespace
E               from ae_engine.assembly_joint import AssemblyJointSource
E               from ae_engine.assembly_collision import (
E                   discover_joint_relief_candidate, verify_joint_candidate_replay,
E                   joint_relief_ownership, project_joint_interference_to_relief_owner,
E               )
E               from ae_engine.contracts import ResolvedJointDiagnostic
E           
E               current = {str(part.part_key): part for part in tuple(parts or ())}
E               diagnostics = []
E               old_items = dict((committed_state or {}).get("items", {}) or {}) if isinstance(committed_state, dict) else {}
E               new_state = {"schema_version": 1, "items": {}}
E           
E               explicit = [
E                   joint for joint in tuple(joints or ())
E                   if str(getattr(getattr(joint, "source", None), "value", getattr(joint, "source", "")))
E                   == AssemblyJointSource.USER_ADDED.value
E               ]
E           
E               def world_direction_segment(world_map, joint):
E                   def centroid(part_key):
E                       tris = tuple((world_map or {}).get(str(part_key), ()) or ())
E                       points = [p for tri in tris for p in tri if isinstance(p, (tuple, list)) and len(p) >= 3]
E                       if not points:
E                           return None
E                       try:
E                           return tuple(sum(float(p[i]) for p in points) / len(points) for i in range(3))
E                       except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                           return None
E                   a = centroid(joint.subject_part); b = centroid(joint.target_part)
E                   return (a, b) if a is not None and b is not None else None
E           
E               for joint in explicit:
E                   ownership = joint_relief_ownership(joint)
E                   relief_key = str(ownership.relief_part)
E                   preserve_key = str(ownership.preserve_part)
E                   relief_part = current.get(relief_key)
E                   preserve_part = current.get(preserve_key)
E                   relation = str(getattr(getattr(joint, "relation", None), "value", getattr(joint, "relation", "")))
E                   source = str(getattr(getattr(joint, "source", None), "value", getattr(joint, "source", "")))
E                   if relief_part is None or preserve_part is None:
E                       diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                           joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                           relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                           preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                           candidate_status="MISSING_PART_GEOMETRY", illegal_penetration=False,
E                           evidence={"reason":"JOINT_ENDPOINT_NOT_IN_CANONICAL_PARTS"},
E                       ))
E                       continue
E                   edge = str(getattr(joint, "edge", "") or "").strip().upper()
E                   is_piece_side_wrap = (
E                       relation == "WRAP"
E                       and relief_key == "box_body"
E                       and bool(getattr(relief_part.render_data, "pieces", None))
E                       and preserve_key in {"head", "tail"}
E                       and edge in {"LEFT", "RIGHT"}
E                   )
E                   if is_piece_side_wrap:
E                       from shapely.ops import unary_union
E           
E                       try:
E                           if relief_key == str(joint.target_part):
E                               relief_region = str(getattr(joint, "target_region", "") or "")
E                           else:
E                               relief_region = str(getattr(joint, "subject_region", "") or "")
E                           relief_geometry_key = _phase6_box_body_piece_solver_key(
E                               relief_part, relief_region or f"{edge.lower()}_mating_zone", require_flat_uv=True
E                           )
E                           raw_world = _phase6_build_joint_world_geometry(
E                               tuple(current.values()), finished_dimensions, sheet_thickness
E                           )
E                           raw_material = raw_world["flat_material_by_part"][relief_geometry_key]
E                       except Exception as exc:
E                           diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                               joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                               relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                               preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                               candidate_status="PIECE_LEVEL_UV_UNAVAILABLE", illegal_penetration=True,
E                               evidence={"reason":str(exc)},
E                           ))
E                           continue
E           
E                       saved = old_items.get(str(joint.joint_id))
E                       if (
E                           str((saved or {}).get("relief_geometry_key") or "") == relief_geometry_key
E                           and _phase6_joint_relief_state_item_matches(saved, joint, raw_material)
E                       ):
E                           cut = _phase6_cut_geometry_from_state_item(saved)
E                           if cut is not None and not getattr(cut, "is_empty", True):
E                               current[relief_key] = _phase6_apply_resolved_cut_to_owner(
E                                   current[relief_key], relief_geometry_key, cut
E                               )
E                               replayed = dict(saved)
E                               replayed["trust_level"] = "PROVISIONAL_3D"
E                               new_state["items"][str(joint.joint_id)] = replayed
E                               diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                                   joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                                   relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS", trust_level="PROVISIONAL_3D",
E                                   preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key, candidate_status="PROVISIONAL_3D_REPLAYED",
E                                   legal_contact=True, illegal_penetration=False,
E                                   pre_pair_count=int(dict(saved.get("evidence", {}) or {}).get("pre_pair_count", 0) or 0),
E                                   post_pair_count=int(dict(saved.get("evidence", {}) or {}).get("post_pair_count", 0) or 0),
E                                   relief_segments=tuple(), evidence={"replayed":True, **dict(saved.get("evidence", {}) or {})},
E                               ))
E                               continue
E           
E                       target_corners = _phase6_side_wrap_target_corners(preserve_key)
E                       base_relief_part = current[relief_key]
E                       working_world = raw_world
E                       accumulated_cut_polygons = []
E                       all_projection_segments = []
E                       pre_pairs = 0
E                       post_pairs = 0
E                       solver_iterations = 0
E                       proposed_relief = None
E                       residual = None
E                       failure_status = None
E                       failure_reason = ""
E                       no_initial_penetration = False
E                       max_iterations = 32
E                       progress_tolerance = 1e-7
E                       previous_cut_area = 0.0
E           
E                       while solver_iterations < max_iterations:
E                           solver_iterations += 1
E                           working_material = working_world["flat_material_by_part"].get(relief_geometry_key)
E                           if working_material is None or getattr(working_material, "is_empty", True):
E                               failure_status = "PIECE_LEVEL_UV_UNAVAILABLE"
E                               failure_reason = f"missing working material: {relief_geometry_key}"
E                               break
E           
E                           round_candidates = []
E                           blocked_candidate = None
E                           try:
E                               for corner_name in target_corners:
E                                   candidate = discover_joint_relief_candidate(
E                                       joint,
E                                       world_triangles_by_part=working_world["world_triangles_by_part"],
E                                       mapped_skin_triangles_by_part=working_world["mapped_skin_triangles_by_part"],
E                                       flat_material_by_part=working_world["flat_material_by_part"],
E                                       topology_levels=None,
E                                       relief_component=working_material,
E                                       clearance=float(clearance),
E                                       relief_geometry_key=relief_geometry_key,
E                                       source_geometry_key=preserve_key,
E                                       corner_name_override=corner_name,
E                                   )
E                                   projection = getattr(candidate, "projection", None)
E                                   flat_projection = getattr(projection, "projection", None)
E                                   pair_count = int(getattr(flat_projection, "pair_count", 0) or 0)
E                                   if solver_iterations == 1:
E                                       pre_pairs = max(pre_pairs, pair_count)
E                                   all_projection_segments.extend(tuple(getattr(flat_projection, "segments_world", ()) or ()))
E                                   status = str(getattr(candidate, "status", "UNKNOWN") or "UNKNOWN")
E                                   if status == "CANDIDATE":
E                                       round_candidates.append(candidate)
E                                   elif bool(getattr(projection, "illegal_penetration", False)):
E                                       blocked_candidate = candidate
E                                       break
E                           except Exception as exc:
E                               failure_status = "DISCOVERY_FAILED"
E                               failure_reason = str(exc)
E                               break
E           
E                           if blocked_candidate is not None:
E                               failure_status = str(getattr(blocked_candidate, "status", "UNKNOWN") or "UNKNOWN")
E                               failure_reason = str(dict(getattr(blocked_candidate, "evidence", {}) or {}).get("reason") or "ILLEGAL_UNFITTED_REGION")
E                               break
E           
E                           if not round_candidates:
E                               if solver_iterations == 1:
E                                   no_initial_penetration = True
E                               else:
E                                   failure_status = "REPLAY_FAILED"
E                                   failure_reason = "RESIDUAL_ILLEGAL_PENETRATION_WITHOUT_NEW_CANDIDATE"
E                               break
E           
E                           round_cut_polygons = tuple(
E                               candidate.cut_polygon_2d for candidate in round_candidates
E                               if getattr(candidate, "cut_polygon_2d", None) is not None
E                               and not getattr(candidate.cut_polygon_2d, "is_empty", True)
E                           )
E                           if not round_cut_polygons:
E                               failure_status = "FIT_FAILED"
E                               failure_reason = "CANDIDATE_WITHOUT_CUT"
E                               break
E                           accumulated_cut_polygons.extend(round_cut_polygons)
E                           atomic_cut = unary_union(tuple(accumulated_cut_polygons))
E                           cut_area = float(getattr(atomic_cut, "area", 0.0) or 0.0)
E                           if cut_area <= previous_cut_area + progress_tolerance:
E                               failure_status = "REPLAY_FAILED"
E                               failure_reason = "NO_GEOMETRIC_PROGRESS"
E                               break
E                           previous_cut_area = cut_area
E           
E                           try:
E                               # Every iteration replays the complete union against the exact
E                               # pre-cut owner.  This keeps the two physical end corners atomic
E                               # and prevents one discovery from seeing the other one's partial cut.
E                               proposed_relief = _phase6_apply_resolved_cut_to_owner(
E                                   base_relief_part, relief_geometry_key, atomic_cut
E                               )
E                               proposed_parts = tuple(
E                                   proposed_relief if key == relief_key else value
E                                   for key, value in current.items()
E                               )
E                               proposed_world = _phase6_build_joint_world_geometry(
E                                   proposed_parts, finished_dimensions, sheet_thickness
E                               )
E                               residual = project_joint_interference_to_relief_owner(
E                                   joint,
E                                   world_triangles_by_part=proposed_world["world_triangles_by_part"],
E                                   mapped_skin_triangles_by_part=proposed_world["mapped_skin_triangles_by_part"],
E                                   flat_material_by_part=proposed_world["flat_material_by_part"],
E                                   relief_geometry_key=relief_geometry_key,
E                                   source_geometry_key=preserve_key,
E                               )
E                           except Exception as exc:
E                               failure_status = "REPLAY_FAILED"
E                               failure_reason = str(exc)
E                               residual = None
E                               break
E           
E                           residual_projection = getattr(residual, "projection", None)
E                           post_pairs = int(getattr(residual_projection, "pair_count", 0) or 0)
E                           if not bool(getattr(residual, "illegal_penetration", False)):
E                               break
E                           working_world = proposed_world
E                       else:
E                           failure_status = "REPLAY_FAILED"
E                           failure_reason = "FIXED_POINT_MAX_ITERATIONS"
E           
E                       direction_segment = world_direction_segment(raw_world.get("world_triangles_by_part", {}), joint)
E                       if no_initial_penetration:
E                           diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                               joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                               relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                               preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key, candidate_status="NO_ILLEGAL_PENETRATION",
E                               legal_contact=True, illegal_penetration=False, pre_pair_count=pre_pairs, post_pair_count=pre_pairs,
E                               contact_segments=tuple(all_projection_segments), direction_segment=direction_segment,
E                               evidence={
E                                   "relief_geometry_key":relief_geometry_key,
E                                   "corner_names":list(target_corners),
E                                   "solver_iterations":solver_iterations,
E                               },
E                           ))
E                           continue
E           
E                       if failure_status is not None or residual is None or bool(getattr(residual, "illegal_penetration", False)):
E                           residual_projection = getattr(residual, "projection", None) if residual is not None else None
E                           diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                               joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                               relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS", preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                               candidate_status=str(failure_status or "REPLAY_FAILED"), illegal_penetration=True, pre_pair_count=pre_pairs,
E                               post_pair_count=int(getattr(residual_projection, "pair_count", post_pairs or pre_pairs) or 0),
E                               penetration_segments=tuple(all_projection_segments), direction_segment=direction_segment,
E                               evidence={
E                                   "reason":failure_reason or "RESIDUAL_ILLEGAL_PENETRATION",
E                                   "relief_geometry_key":relief_geometry_key,
E                                   "solver_iterations":solver_iterations,
E                               },
E                           ))
E                           continue
E           
E                       current[relief_key] = proposed_relief
E                       corner_names = tuple(target_corners)
E                       state_item = {
E                           "joint_id":str(joint.joint_id), "subject_part":str(joint.subject_part),
E                           "target_part":str(joint.target_part), "relation":relation, "source":source,
E                           "relief_part":relief_key, "relief_geometry_key":relief_geometry_key,
E                           "topology_levels":None, "verified":True, "trust_level":"PROVISIONAL_3D",
E                           "corner_names":list(corner_names),
E                           "source_material_bounds":[float(v) for v in raw_material.bounds],
E                           "source_material_area":float(raw_material.area),
E                           "cut_polygons":[
E                               coords
E                               for polygon in tuple(accumulated_cut_polygons)
E                               for coords in _phase6_relief_polygon_coords(polygon)
E                           ],
E                           "evidence":{
E                               "pre_pair_count":pre_pairs, "post_pair_count":post_pairs,
E                               "post_illegal_penetration":False,
E                               "relief_geometry_key":relief_geometry_key,
E                               "corner_names":list(corner_names),
E                               "solver_iterations":solver_iterations,
E                               "policy":"ATOMIC_MULTI_CORNER_FIXED_POINT_REPLAY_AND_ZERO_ILLEGAL_PENETRATION",
E                           },
E                       }
E                       new_state["items"][str(joint.joint_id)] = state_item
E                       diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                           joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                           relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS", trust_level="PROVISIONAL_3D",
E                           preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key, candidate_status="PROVISIONAL_3D",
E                           legal_contact=bool(getattr(residual, "has_contact", False)), illegal_penetration=False,
E                           pre_pair_count=pre_pairs, post_pair_count=post_pairs,
E                           penetration_segments=tuple(all_projection_segments), relief_segments=tuple(all_projection_segments),
E                           preserve_segments=tuple(all_projection_segments), direction_segment=direction_segment,
E                           evidence=deepcopy(state_item["evidence"]),
E                       ))
E                       continue
E           
E                   if getattr(relief_part.render_data, "pieces", None) or getattr(preserve_part.render_data, "pieces", None):
E                       diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                           joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                           relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                           preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                           candidate_status="PIECE_LEVEL_UV_UNAVAILABLE",
E                           evidence={"reason":"MULTI_PIECE_PART_REQUIRES_PIECE_LEVEL_UV_ADAPTER"},
E                       ))
E                       continue
E           
E                   raw_material = relief_part.render_data.material
E                   saved = old_items.get(str(joint.joint_id))
E                   if _phase6_joint_relief_state_item_matches(saved, joint, raw_material):
E                       cut = _phase6_cut_geometry_from_state_item(saved)
E                       if cut is not None and not getattr(cut, "is_empty", True):
E                           solved_part = _phase6_apply_resolved_cut_to_part(relief_part, cut)
E                           current[relief_key] = solved_part
E                           replayed = dict(saved)
E                           replayed["trust_level"] = "PROVISIONAL_3D"
E                           new_state["items"][str(joint.joint_id)] = replayed
E                           diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                               joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                               relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS", trust_level="PROVISIONAL_3D",
E                               preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                               candidate_status="PROVISIONAL_3D_REPLAYED", legal_contact=True, illegal_penetration=False,
E                               pre_pair_count=int(dict(saved.get("evidence", {}) or {}).get("pre_pair_count", 0) or 0),
E                               post_pair_count=int(dict(saved.get("evidence", {}) or {}).get("post_pair_count", 0) or 0),
E                               relief_segments=tuple(), evidence={"replayed":True, **dict(saved.get("evidence", {}) or {})},
E                           ))
E                           continue
E           
E                   constraints = dict(getattr(joint, "solver_constraints", {}) or {})
E                   topology_raw = constraints.get("topology_levels")
E                   try:
E                       topology_levels = int(topology_raw)
E                   except Exception:
E                       topology_levels = 0
E                   if topology_levels not in (1, 2):
E                       diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                           joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                           relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                           preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                           candidate_status="TOPOLOGY_UNSPECIFIED", illegal_penetration=False,
E                           evidence={"reason":"EXPLICIT_TOPOLOGY_LEVEL_REQUIRED_FOR_DISCOVERY"},
E                       ))
E                       continue
E                   if topology_levels == 2:
E                       # A two-stage search domain must come from a certified/known topology
E                       # component.  Never invent a second band from raw intersection data.
E                       diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                           joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                           relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                           preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                           candidate_status="TWO_STAGE_COMPONENT_REQUIRED", illegal_penetration=False,
E                           evidence={"reason":"CERTIFIED_TWO_STAGE_TOPOLOGY_COMPONENT_REQUIRED"},
E                       ))
E                       continue
E           
E                   try:
E                       world = _phase6_build_joint_world_geometry(tuple(current.values()), finished_dimensions, sheet_thickness)
E                       candidate = discover_joint_relief_candidate(
E                           joint,
E                           world_triangles_by_part=world["world_triangles_by_part"],
E                           mapped_skin_triangles_by_part=world["mapped_skin_triangles_by_part"],
E                           flat_material_by_part=world["flat_material_by_part"],
E                           topology_levels=topology_levels,
E                           relief_component=raw_material,
E                           clearance=float(clearance),
E                       )
E                   except Exception as exc:
E                       diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                           joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                           relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                           preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                           candidate_status="DISCOVERY_FAILED", illegal_penetration=True,
E                           evidence={"reason":str(exc)},
E                       ))
E                       continue
E           
E                   projection = getattr(candidate, "projection", None)
E                   flat_projection = getattr(projection, "projection", None)
E                   pre_pairs = int(getattr(flat_projection, "pair_count", 0) or 0)
E                   penetration_segments = tuple(getattr(flat_projection, "segments_world", ()) or ())
E                   direction_segment = world_direction_segment(world.get("world_triangles_by_part", {}), joint)
E                   status = str(getattr(candidate, "status", "UNKNOWN") or "UNKNOWN")
E                   if status != "CANDIDATE":
E                       diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                           joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                           relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                           preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key, candidate_status=status,
E                           legal_contact=bool(getattr(projection, "has_contact", False) and not getattr(projection, "illegal_penetration", False)),
E                           illegal_penetration=bool(getattr(projection, "illegal_penetration", False)),
E                           pre_pair_count=pre_pairs, post_pair_count=pre_pairs,
E                           penetration_segments=penetration_segments,
E                           contact_segments=(penetration_segments if bool(getattr(projection, "has_contact", False)) and not bool(getattr(projection, "illegal_penetration", False)) else ()),
E                           direction_segment=direction_segment,
E                           evidence=dict(getattr(candidate, "evidence", {}) or {}),
E                       ))
E                       continue
E           
E                   def rebuild_mapped_skins(part_key, solved_material):
E                       base = current[str(part_key)]
E                       temp_render = SimpleNamespace(
E                           scene=base.render_data.scene, material=solved_material,
E                           fold_guides=tuple(getattr(base.render_data, "fold_guides", ()) or ()), metadata={},
E                       )
E                       temp_part = AssemblyScenePart(
E                           part_key=base.part_key, render_data=temp_render,
E                           x_profile=base.x_profile, y_profile=base.y_profile,
E                           placement=base.placement, offset=base.offset,
E                       )
E                       temp_parts = tuple(temp_part if key == str(part_key) else value for key, value in current.items())
E                       rebuilt = _phase6_build_joint_world_geometry(temp_parts, finished_dimensions, sheet_thickness)
E                       return tuple(rebuilt["mapped_skin_triangles_by_part"].get(str(part_key), ()) or ())
E           
E                   try:
E                       verification = verify_joint_candidate_replay(
E                           joint, candidate,
E                           world_triangles_by_part=world["world_triangles_by_part"],
E                           flat_material_by_part=world["flat_material_by_part"],
E                           rebuild_mapped_skins=rebuild_mapped_skins,
E                       )
E                   except Exception as exc:
E                       verification = None
E                       verify_error = str(exc)
E                   else:
E                       verify_error = ""
E                   if verification is None or not bool(getattr(verification, "verified", False)):
E                       residual = getattr(verification, "residual", None) if verification is not None else None
E                       residual_projection = getattr(residual, "projection", None)
E                       post_pairs = int(getattr(residual_projection, "pair_count", pre_pairs) or 0)
E                       diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                           joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                           relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS",
E                           preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key,
E                           candidate_status="REPLAY_FAILED", illegal_penetration=True,
E                           pre_pair_count=pre_pairs, post_pair_count=post_pairs,
E                           penetration_segments=penetration_segments,
E                           direction_segment=direction_segment,
E                           evidence={"reason":verify_error or "RESIDUAL_ILLEGAL_PENETRATION"},
E                       ))
E                       continue
E           
E                   cut = candidate.cut_polygon_2d
E                   current[relief_key] = _phase6_apply_resolved_cut_to_part(current[relief_key], cut)
E                   residual = verification.residual
E                   residual_projection = getattr(residual, "projection", None)
E                   post_pairs = int(getattr(residual_projection, "pair_count", 0) or 0)
E                   measurement = getattr(getattr(candidate, "corner_relief", None), "measurement", None)
E                   state_item = {
E                       "joint_id": str(joint.joint_id),
E                       "subject_part": str(joint.subject_part), "target_part": str(joint.target_part),
E                       "relation": relation, "source": source, "relief_part": relief_key,
E                       "topology_levels": topology_levels, "verified": True,
E                       "trust_level": "PROVISIONAL_3D",
E                       "corner_name": str(getattr(measurement, "corner_name", "") or ""),
E                       "source_material_bounds": [float(v) for v in raw_material.bounds],
E                       "source_material_area": float(raw_material.area),
E                       "cut_polygons": _phase6_relief_polygon_coords(cut),
E                       "evidence": {**dict(getattr(candidate, "evidence", {}) or {}), **dict(getattr(verification, "evidence", {}) or {})},
E                   }
E                   new_state["items"][str(joint.joint_id)] = state_item
E                   diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                       joint_id=str(joint.joint_id), subject_part=str(joint.subject_part), target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                       relation=relation, source=source, registry_status="MISS", trust_level="PROVISIONAL_3D",
E                       preserve_part=preserve_key, relief_part=relief_key, candidate_status="PROVISIONAL_3D",
E                       legal_contact=bool(getattr(residual, "has_contact", False)), illegal_penetration=False,
E                       pre_pair_count=pre_pairs, post_pair_count=post_pairs,
E                       penetration_segments=penetration_segments,
E                       relief_segments=penetration_segments,
E                       preserve_segments=penetration_segments,
E                       direction_segment=direction_segment,
E                       evidence=deepcopy(state_item["evidence"]),
E                   ))
E           
E               ordered = tuple(current[str(part.part_key)] for part in tuple(parts or ()))
E               return ordered, tuple(diagnostics), new_state
E           
E           def _phase6_signature_canonical_value(value):
E               """Normalize representation-only differences before manufacturing caching."""
E               if isinstance(value, bool) or value is None or isinstance(value, str):
E                   return value
E               if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
E                   return round(float(value), 12)
E               if isinstance(value, Mapping):
E                   return {str(key): _phase6_signature_canonical_value(item) for key, item in value.items()}
E               if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
E                   return tuple(_phase6_signature_canonical_value(item) for item in value)
E               if isinstance(value, set):
E                   normalized = [_phase6_signature_canonical_value(item) for item in value]
E                   return tuple(sorted(normalized, key=repr))
E               enum_value = getattr(value, "value", None)
E               if enum_value is not None:
E                   return _phase6_signature_canonical_value(enum_value)
E               return repr(value)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_manufacturing_state_signature(self):
E               """Stable key for canonical manufacturing inputs; persistence/view mirrors excluded."""
E               workspace = getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None)
E               available = tuple(getattr(workspace, "available_parts", ()) or ())
E               profiles = {}
E               if workspace is not None:
E                   for key in available:
E                       try:
E                           profiles[key] = deepcopy(workspace.profiles_for(key, {}) or {})
E                       except Exception:
E                           profiles[key] = {}
E           
E               settings = deepcopy(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               # Typography is UI-only and must never invalidate canonical manufacturing.
E               settings.pop("ui_text_size", None)
E               snapshot = deepcopy(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}))
E               # These fields are either owned independently below or derived/persistence
E               # mirrors. Saving an unchanged editor may materialize them, but that is not a
E               # manufacturing mutation and must not destroy an otherwise valid cache hit.
E               mirror_keys = set(settings) | {
E                   "settings", "endcap_fw", "endcap_bottom_wrap", "assembly_type",
E                   "corner_state", "corner_pair_same", "active_part", "part_dimensions",
E               }
E               snapshot = {key: value for key, value in snapshot.items() if key not in mirror_keys}
E           
E               state = (
E                   snapshot,
E                   settings,
E                   deepcopy(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_box_whd", {}) or {})),
E                   deepcopy(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {}) or {})),
E                   deepcopy(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", {}) or {})),
E                   deepcopy(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state", {}) or {})),
E                   available,
E                   profiles,
E                   getattr(getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", None), "value", getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", None)),
E                   bool(getattr(getattr(self, "assembly_ignore_fixed_corner_var", None), "get", lambda: False)()),
E                   _phase6_assembly_relief_clearance(self),
E               )
E               return stable_fingerprint(_phase6_signature_canonical_value(state))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_joint_registry_diagnostic_info(joint, render_by_part, solution_by_part):
E               """Resolve registry/verification evidence for exactly one AssemblyJoint."""
E               edge = str(getattr(joint, "edge", "") or "").upper()
E               relation = str(getattr(getattr(joint, "relation", None), "value", getattr(joint, "relation", "")) or "")
E               endcap_part = next((p for p in (str(getattr(joint, "subject_part", "")), str(getattr(joint, "target_part", ""))) if p in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS), "")
E           
E               if edge == "BOTTOM":
E                   if relation == "WRAP" and endcap_part:
E                       render = render_by_part.get(endcap_part)
E                       trace = dict(dict(getattr(render, "metadata", {}) or {}).get("receiving_bottom_relief_rule") or {})
E                       if trace:
E                           evidence = deepcopy(trace.get("geometry_evidence") or {})
E                           return {
E                               "registry_status": "HIT",
E                               "rule_id": trace.get("rule_id"),
E                               "revision": trace.get("revision"),
E                               "trust_level": str(trace.get("trust_level") or ""),
E                               "candidate_status": "CERTIFIED",
E                               "verified": True,
E                               "pre_pair_count": 0,
E                               "post_pair_count": 0,
E                               "evidence": evidence,
E                           }
E                       return {
E                           "registry_status": "MISS", "rule_id": None, "revision": None,
E                           "trust_level": "", "candidate_status": "UNSUPPORTED", "verified": False,
E                           "pre_pair_count": 0, "post_pair_count": 0, "evidence": None,
E                       }
E                   # No BOTTOM semantic delta: STANDARD is the canonical mother geometry.
E                   return {
E                       "registry_status": "MISS", "rule_id": None, "revision": None,
E                       "trust_level": "STANDARD", "candidate_status": "STANDARD", "verified": True,
E                       "pre_pair_count": 0, "post_pair_count": 0, "evidence": {"owner": "STANDARD"},
E                   }
E           
E               solution = solution_by_part.get(endcap_part) if endcap_part else None
E               if solution is None:
E                   return {
E                       "registry_status": "MISS", "rule_id": None, "revision": None,
E                       "trust_level": "", "candidate_status": "UNKNOWN", "verified": False,
E                       "pre_pair_count": 0, "post_pair_count": 0, "evidence": None,
E                   }
E               rule_id = getattr(solution, "rule_id", None)
E               pre_pairs = sum(int(getattr(p, "pair_count", 0) or 0) for p in tuple(getattr(solution, "projections", ()) or ()))
E               post_pairs = int(getattr(getattr(solution, "residual_projection", None), "pair_count", 0) or 0)
E               trust = str(getattr(solution, "trust_level", "") or "")
E               return {
E                   "registry_status": "HIT" if rule_id else "MISS",
E                   "rule_id": rule_id,
E                   "revision": getattr(solution, "rule_revision", None),
E                   "trust_level": trust,
E                   "candidate_status": "CERTIFIED" if rule_id else trust,
E                   "verified": bool(getattr(solution, "verified", False)),
E                   "pre_pair_count": pre_pairs, "post_pair_count": post_pairs,
E                   "evidence": deepcopy(getattr(solution, "shadow_validation", None)),
E               }
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_resolve_manufacturing_geometry(self):
E               """Resolve one canonical manufacturing result consumed by every downstream view/export."""
E               signature = _phase6_manufacturing_state_signature(self)
E               cached = getattr(self, "_phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_geometry", None)
E               if cached is not None and signature == getattr(self, "_phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_signature", None):
E                   return cached
E               callback = getattr(self, "_scene_query_callback", None)
E               if callback is None:
E                   raise RuntimeError("3D final-scene provider is not connected")
E           
E               fallback_var = getattr(self, "assembly_ignore_fixed_corner_var", None)
E               fallback_enabled = bool(fallback_var.get()) if fallback_var is not None else False
E               available = set(self.designer_workspace.available_parts)
E               snapshot_for_joints = migrate_legacy_snapshot_joints(dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}))
E               joints = tuple(
E                   raw if isinstance(raw, AssemblyJoint) else AssemblyJoint.from_dict(raw)
E                   for raw in tuple(snapshot_for_joints.get("assembly_joints", ()) or ())
E                   if str(getattr(raw, "subject_part", raw.get("subject_part", "") if isinstance(raw, dict) else "")) in available
E                   and str(getattr(raw, "target_part", raw.get("target_part", "") if isinstance(raw, dict) else "")) in available
E               )
E               resolved_joint_graph = ResolvedAssemblyGraph(tuple(sorted(available)), joints)
E               parts = []
E               for key in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   if key not in available:
E                       continue
E                   payload = _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, key)
E                   # Solver base must be pre-dynamic-relief material. If solving is off,
E                   # display the current canonical committed relief directly.
E                   payload["_use_committed_relief"] = False
E                   render_data = callback(key, payload)
E                   if render_data is None:
E                       raise ValueError(f"manufacturing render data unavailable: {key}")
E                   if getattr(render_data, "pieces", None):
E                       x_profile, y_profile = (), ()
E                   else:
E                       if getattr(render_data, "scene", None) is None or getattr(render_data, "material", None) is None:
E                           raise TypeError("manufacturing render provider must return scene + material or physical pieces")
E                       x_profile, y_profile = _phase6_mesh_profiles_for_part(self, key, render_data.material)
E                   parts.append(AssemblyScenePart(
E                       part_key=key, render_data=render_data,
E                       x_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in x_profile),
E                       y_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in y_profile),
E                       placement=_PHASE6_ASSEMBLY_PLACEMENTS.get(key, "offset"),
E                   ))
E               if not parts:
E                   raise ValueError("no parts available for assembly 3D display")
E           
E               # Preserve the pre-dynamic-relief EndCap geometry for the collision overlay.
E               # Visible parts may be replaced by solved canonical geometry below.
E               pre_solve_probe_parts = tuple(
E                   part for part in parts if part.part_key in {"head", "tail"}
E               )
E           
E               solutions = {}
E               errors = {}
E               required = [key for key in ("head", "tail") if key in available]
E               if required:
E                   body_part = next((part for part in parts if part.part_key == "box_body"), None)
E                   if body_part is not None:
E                       from ae_engine.assembly_collision import solve_world_backprojected_endcap_relief
E                       dims = _phase6_operator_finished_dimensions(self)
E                       snapshot = getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E                       settings = getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}
E                       thickness = _num(settings.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2.0)), 2.0)
E                       clearance = _phase6_assembly_relief_clearance(self)
E                       by_key = {part.part_key: part for part in parts}
E                       for key in required:
E                           part = by_key[key]
E                           try:
E                               solution = solve_world_backprojected_endcap_relief(
E                                   box_body_render_data=body_part.render_data,
E                                   endcap_render_data=part.render_data,
E                                   box_body_x_profile=body_part.x_profile,
E                                   endcap_x_profile=part.x_profile,
E                                   endcap_y_profile=part.y_profile,
E                                   finished_dimensions=dims,
E                                   endcap_placement=part.placement,
E                                   sheet_thickness=thickness,
E                                   clearance=clearance,
E                                   assembly_intent=assembly_intent_value(getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", None)),
E                                   assembly_graph=resolved_joint_graph,
E                                   endcap_part=key,
E                                   cabinet_family=_phase6_current_cabinet_family(self),
E                                   allow_3d_fallback=fallback_enabled,
E                                   # Extra USER_ADDED joints are resolved by Solver v2 after
E                                   # the standard EndCap intent geometry is canonical.  Do
E                                   # not feed WRAP into the legacy EndCap-only solver: WRAP
E                                   # owns relief on its target and that solver can only cut
E                                   # the EndCap subject.
E                                   assembly_joint=None,
E                               )
E                               solutions[key] = solution
E                               if not bool(getattr(solution, "verified", False)):
E                                   if _phase6_solution_is_committable(solution):
E                                       errors[key] = "已認證公式與3D影子驗證衝突；正式結果仍採CERTIFIED公式"
E                                   else:
E                                       reason = dict(getattr(solution, "shadow_validation", {}) or {}).get("reason")
E                                       errors[key] = str(reason or "3D 回折驗證仍有材料穿透")
E                           except Exception as exc:
E                               errors[key] = str(exc)
E           
E                       atomic_committable = all(
E                           key in solutions and _phase6_solution_is_committable(solutions[key])
E                           for key in required
E                       )
E                       self._phase6_last_relief_solutions = solutions
E                       self._phase6_last_relief_errors = errors
E           
E                       if atomic_committable:
E                           # Publish both cuts as one canonical state update before any
E                           # solved geometry is displayed.
E                           _phase6_publish_live_state(self, force=True)
E                           solved_parts = []
E                           for part in parts:
E                               if part.part_key not in required:
E                                   solved_parts.append(part)
E                                   continue
E                               solution = solutions[part.part_key]
E                               replay_payload = _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, part.part_key)
E                               replay_payload["_use_committed_relief"] = False
E                               replay_payload["resolved_assembly_relief_cuts"] = tuple(
E                                   tuple((float(x), float(y)) for x, y in polygon)
E                                   for polygon in _phase6_relief_polygon_coords(
E                                       getattr(solution, "cut_polygon_2d", None)
E                                   )
E                               )
E                               canonical_render = callback(part.part_key, replay_payload)
E                               if canonical_render is None or getattr(canonical_render, "material", None) is None:
E                                   raise ValueError(f"authoritative relief replay unavailable: {part.part_key}")
E                               solver_material = getattr(getattr(solution, "solved_render_data", None), "material", None)
E                               if solver_material is not None:
E                                   mismatch = float(canonical_render.material.symmetric_difference(solver_material).area)
E                                   if mismatch > 1e-5:
E                                       raise ValueError(
E                                           f"2D/3D relief replay mismatch: {part.part_key} area={mismatch:.6f}"
E                                       )
E                               solved_parts.append(AssemblyScenePart(
E                                   part_key=part.part_key, render_data=canonical_render,
E                                   x_profile=part.x_profile, y_profile=part.y_profile,
E                                   placement=part.placement, offset=part.offset,
E                               ))
E                           parts = solved_parts
E                       else:
E                           # Atomic rollback: display current canonical 2D geometry for
E                           # BOTH EndCaps. Never show a verified Head with an old Tail.
E                           canonical_parts = []
E                           for part in parts:
E                               if part.part_key not in required:
E                                   canonical_parts.append(part)
E                                   continue
E                               payload = _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, part.part_key)
E                               payload["_use_committed_relief"] = True
E                               canonical_render = callback(part.part_key, payload)
E                               canonical_parts.append(AssemblyScenePart(
E                                   part_key=part.part_key, render_data=canonical_render,
E                                   x_profile=part.x_profile, y_profile=part.y_profile,
E                                   placement=part.placement, offset=part.offset,
E                               ))
E                           parts = canonical_parts
E               else:
E                   self._phase6_last_relief_solutions = {}
E                   self._phase6_last_relief_errors = {}
E           
E               # Resolve extra USER_ADDED joints (including WRAP) only after the high-level
E               # EndCap intent has produced canonical parts.  This generalized path can
E               # relief either endpoint according to Joint semantics and persists only
E               # replay-verified provisional cuts.
E               explicit_joint_diagnostics = ()
E               explicit_joint_state = {"schema_version": 1, "items": {}}
E               if any(
E                   str(getattr(getattr(joint, "source", None), "value", getattr(joint, "source", ""))) == "USER_ADDED"
E                   for joint in joints
E               ):
E                   snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E                   settings = dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {})
E                   thickness = _num(settings.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2.0)), 2.0)
E                   parts, explicit_joint_diagnostics, explicit_joint_state = _phase6_resolve_explicit_joint_reliefs(
E                       tuple(parts), joints,
E                       finished_dimensions=_phase6_operator_finished_dimensions(self),
E                       sheet_thickness=thickness,
E                       clearance=_phase6_assembly_relief_clearance(self),
E                       committed_state=snapshot.get("joint_relief_state"),
E                   )
E                   # Runtime state is versioned and serializable; export/save starts from
E                   # this snapshot, so verified provisional cuts survive reload.
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot["joint_relief_state"] = deepcopy(explicit_joint_state)
E           
E               from ae_engine.contracts import (
E                   ResolvedManufacturingGeometry, ResolvedManufacturingPart, ResolvedReliefRuleTrace,
E               )
E               resolved_parts = tuple(
E                   ResolvedManufacturingPart(
E                       part_key=part.part_key,
E                       render_data=part.render_data,
E                       x_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in tuple(part.x_profile or ())),
E                       y_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in tuple(part.y_profile or ())),
E                       placement=part.placement,
E                       offset=tuple(part.offset),
E                   )
E                   for part in parts
E               )
E               traces = []
E               solution_by_part = dict(solutions or {})
E               for part_key, solution in solution_by_part.items():
E                   trust = str(getattr(solution, "trust_level", "") or "")
E                   rule_id = getattr(solution, "rule_id", None)
E                   revision = getattr(solution, "rule_revision", None)
E                   for relief in tuple(getattr(solution, "corner_reliefs", ()) or ()):
E                       shadow = dict(getattr(solution, "shadow_validation", {}) or {})
E                       traces.append(ResolvedReliefRuleTrace(
E                           part_key=str(part_key),
E                           corner_name=str(getattr(relief, "corner_name", "") or ""),
E                           rule_id=rule_id,
E                           revision=revision,
E                           trust_level=trust,
E                           signature=str(getattr(relief, "signature", "") or ""),
E                           geometry_inputs=tuple(str(v) for v in shadow.get("geometry_inputs", ()) or ()),
E                           geometry_evidence=deepcopy(shadow.get("geometry_evidence")),
E                       ))
E           
E               # Family-specific BOTTOM certified traces live on the canonical FinalScene
E               # metadata because they are resolved while building the EndCap PartSpec.
E               for resolved_part in resolved_parts:
E                   metadata = dict(getattr(resolved_part.render_data, "metadata", {}) or {})
E                   bottom_trace = dict(metadata.get("receiving_bottom_relief_rule") or {})
E                   if not bottom_trace:
E                       continue
E                   evidence = deepcopy(bottom_trace.get("geometry_evidence") or {})
E                   corners = tuple(dict(evidence.get("projection_by_corner") or {}).keys()) or ("bottom",)
E                   for corner_name in corners:
E                       traces.append(ResolvedReliefRuleTrace(
E                           part_key=str(resolved_part.part_key), corner_name=str(corner_name),
E                           rule_id=bottom_trace.get("rule_id"), revision=bottom_trace.get("revision"),
E                           trust_level=str(bottom_trace.get("trust_level") or ""),
E                           signature="BOTTOM:WRAP",
E                           geometry_inputs=tuple(str(v) for v in tuple(evidence.get("geometry_inputs", ()) or ())),
E                           geometry_evidence=evidence,
E                       ))
E           
E               from ae_engine.contracts import ResolvedJointDiagnostic
E               from ae_engine.assembly_collision import joint_relief_ownership
E               diagnostics = []
E               for joint in joints:
E                   if str(getattr(getattr(joint, "source", None), "value", getattr(joint, "source", ""))) == "USER_ADDED":
E                       continue
E                   ownership = joint_relief_ownership(joint)
E                   render_by_part = {str(part.part_key): part.render_data for part in resolved_parts}
E                   info = _phase6_joint_registry_diagnostic_info(joint, render_by_part, solution_by_part)
E                   diagnostics.append(ResolvedJointDiagnostic(
E                       joint_id=str(joint.joint_id),
E                       subject_part=str(joint.subject_part),
E                       target_part=str(joint.target_part),
E                       relation=str(getattr(joint.relation, "value", joint.relation)),
E                       source=str(getattr(joint.source, "value", joint.source)),
E                       registry_status=info["registry_status"],
E                       rule_id=info["rule_id"], revision=info["revision"], trust_level=info["trust_level"],
E                       preserve_part=str(ownership.preserve_part), relief_part=str(ownership.relief_part),
E                       candidate_status=info["candidate_status"],
E                       legal_contact=bool(info["verified"] and int(info["post_pair_count"]) > 0),
E                       illegal_penetration=bool(not info["verified"]),
E                       pre_pair_count=int(info["pre_pair_count"]), post_pair_count=int(info["post_pair_count"]),
E                       evidence=deepcopy(info["evidence"]),
E                   ))
E               diagnostics.extend(tuple(explicit_joint_diagnostics or ()))
E               resolved = ResolvedManufacturingGeometry(
E                   parts=resolved_parts,
E                   joints=joints,
E                   relief_rules=tuple(traces),
E                   diagnostics=tuple(diagnostics),
E               )
E               self._phase6_last_interference_probe_parts = tuple(pre_solve_probe_parts)
E               self._phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_geometry = resolved
E               # Publishing certified/provisional relief may update assembly_relief metadata;
E               # store the post-publish signature so subsequent readers reuse this exact result.
E               self._phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_signature = _phase6_manufacturing_state_signature(self)
E               return resolved
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_query_assembly_render_data(self):
E               """UI adapter: read the already-resolved canonical manufacturing geometry."""
E               resolved = _phase6_resolve_manufacturing_geometry(self)
E               # The initial assembly render may solve certified relief while live-sync is
E               # intentionally disabled.  Once READY, make sure the host snapshot receives
E               # that exact canonical relief even when this query reuses the cached solve.
E               # Equivalent host state remains a strict no-op inside publish_live_state.
E               _phase6_publish_live_state(self, force=True)
E               parts = [
E                   AssemblyScenePart(
E                       part_key=part.part_key,
E                       render_data=part.render_data,
E                       x_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in tuple(part.x_profile or ())),
E                       y_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in tuple(part.y_profile or ())),
E                       placement=part.placement,
E                       offset=part.offset,
E                   )
E                   for part in resolved.parts
E               ]
E               self._phase6_last_assembly_corner_dimension_texts = {
E                   part.part_key: _phase6_render_data_corner_dimension_text(part.render_data)
E                   for part in parts
E               }
E               for key, text in self._phase6_last_assembly_corner_dimension_texts.items():
E                   var = (getattr(self, "assembly_part_corner_vars", {}) or {}).get(key)
E                   if var is not None and callable(getattr(var, "set", None)):
E                       var.set(text)
E               for part in parts:
E                   var = (getattr(self, "assembly_part_blank_vars", {}) or {}).get(part.part_key)
E                   if var is not None and callable(getattr(var, "set", None)):
E                       var.set(_phase6_format_unfolded_blank_text(part.render_data, part_key=part.part_key))
E           
E               visible_vars = getattr(self, "assembly_part_visible_vars", {}) or {}
E               visible_parts = [
E                   part for part in parts
E                   if bool(getattr(visible_vars.get(part.part_key), "get", lambda: True)())
E               ]
E               if not visible_parts and parts:
E                   fallback = next((part for part in parts if part.part_key == "box_body"), parts[0])
E                   visible_parts = [fallback]
E                   var = visible_vars.get(fallback.part_key)
E                   if var is not None and callable(getattr(var, "set", None)):
E                       var.set(True)
E           
E               visible_keys = {part.part_key for part in visible_parts}
E               visible_probe_parts = tuple(
E                   part for part in tuple(getattr(self, "_phase6_last_interference_probe_parts", ()) or ())
E                   if part.part_key in visible_keys
E               )
E               return _phase6_make_assembly_scene_render_data(
E                   assembly_parts=tuple(visible_parts),
E                   show_interference=bool(getattr(self, "assembly_show_interference_var", None).get())
E                       if getattr(self, "assembly_show_interference_var", None) is not None else True,
E                   ignore_fixed_corner_relief=False,
E                   interference_probe_parts=visible_probe_parts,
E                   # Joint Registry diagnostics remain available on ResolvedManufacturingGeometry
E                   # for dedicated debug/registry tools, but are not operator assembly drawing
E                   # layers.  Keep the production combined view free of solver overlays.
E                   joint_diagnostics=(),
E                   selected_joint_id=None,
E                   preserve_endcap_core_origin=(_phase6_current_cabinet_family(self) == "受電箱"),
E               )
E           
E           def _phase6_assembly_unfolded_blank_text(render_data):
E               rows = []
E               for part in tuple(getattr(render_data, "assembly_parts", ()) or ()):
E                   text = _phase6_format_unfolded_blank_text(part.render_data, part_key=part.part_key)
E                   if text.startswith("展開料："):
E                       text = text[len("展開料："):]
E                   rows.append(f"{PART_LABELS.get(part.part_key, part.part_key)}：{text}")
E               return "展開尺寸：\n" + "\n".join(rows) if rows else "展開尺寸：-"
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_final_scene_view_request(self):
E               """Adapt current designer draft state into the FinalScene View interface."""
E               if not self.designer_workspace.active_part:
E                   return None
E               snapshot = getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E               settings = getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}
E               view_mode = str(getattr(self, "_phase6_3d_display_mode", "single") or "single")
E               if view_mode == "assembly":
E                   assembly_render_data = _phase6_query_assembly_render_data(self)
E                   return FinalSceneViewRequest(
E                       render_data=assembly_render_data,
E                       x_profile=(),
E                       y_profile=(),
E                       part_key="assembly",
E                       alpha_bend=float(getattr(self.state, "alpha_bend", 0.85)),
E                       finished_dimensions=_phase6_operator_finished_dimensions(self),
E                       thickness=_num(settings.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2.0)), 2.0),
E                       unfolded_blank_text=_phase6_assembly_unfolded_blank_text(assembly_render_data),
E                   )
E               render_data = _phase6_query_final_render_data(self)
E               if getattr(render_data, "pieces", None):
E                   x_profile, y_profile = (), ()
E               else:
E                   x_profile, y_profile = _phase6_active_mesh_profiles(self, render_data.material)
E               return FinalSceneViewRequest(
E                   render_data=render_data,
E                   x_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in x_profile),
E                   y_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in y_profile),
E                   part_key=str(self.designer_workspace.active_part),
E                   alpha_bend=float(getattr(self.state, "alpha_bend", 0.85)),
E                   finished_dimensions=_phase6_operator_finished_dimensions(self),
E                   thickness=_num(settings.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2.0)), 2.0),
E                   corner_dimension_text=_phase6_render_data_corner_dimension_text(render_data),
E                   unfolded_blank_text=_phase6_format_unfolded_blank_text(
E                       render_data, part_key=str(self.designer_workspace.active_part)
E                   ),
E               )
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_render_true_cutting_mesh(self):
E               """Compatibility adapter; production rendering is owned by FinalSceneView."""
E               view = getattr(self, "final_scene_view", None)
E               if view is None:
E                   view = Phase6FinalSceneView(self.renderer, number_text=_setting_number_text)
E                   self.final_scene_view = view
E               triangles = view.render(_phase6_final_scene_view_request(self))
E               if not isinstance(self, Phase6FoldDesignerApp):
E                   self._phase6_last_cutting_mesh = view.last_cutting_mesh
E                   self._phase6_last_cutting_material = view.last_cutting_material
E                   self._phase6_cutting_mesh_error = view.cutting_mesh_error
E               return triangles
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_3d_scroll(self, event):
E               view = getattr(self, "final_scene_view", None)
E               if view is not None:
E                   return view.on_scroll(event)
E               return None

E           def _phase6_install_renderer_view(self):
E               view = Phase6FinalSceneView(self.renderer, number_text=_setting_number_text)
E               self.final_scene_view = view
E               view.install(
E                   lambda: _phase6_final_scene_view_request(self),
E                   after_render=lambda: (
E                       _phase6_update_unfolded_size_label(self),
E                       _phase6_update_assembly_diagnostic_status(self),
E                   ),
E               )
E               return view
