E           def _phase6_profile_material_total(profile):
E               return float(sum(abs(_num(seg.get("len", 0.0))) for seg in (profile or ())))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_corner_policy_for(self, part_key):
E               raw_state = dict((getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {}) or {}).get(part_key, {}) or {})
E               if not all(key in raw_state for key in _CORNER_KEYS):
E                   return None
E               selections = {key: _phase6_selection_from_raw(raw_state[key]) for key in _CORNER_KEYS}
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               snapshot.update(dict(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}))
E               snapshot["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", normalize_endcap_fw_state(snapshot)))
E               fw = resolve_endcap_fw(snapshot, part_key) if str(part_key) in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS else _num(snapshot.get("fw", 25), 25)
E           
E               # 受電箱下方的等價 FW 不是封頭/尾名義 FW，而是「側板後折 + 1T」。
E               # 保留目前四角 selection（使用者仍可調截角方式/T 倍數），只把 family
E               # geometry Source of Truth 注入 bottom_fw，讓 2D/3D/輸出共用同一 policy。
E               try:
E                   if cabinet_family_policy.supports_bottom_wrap_controls(snapshot) and str(part_key) in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS:
E                       thickness = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2.0), 2.0)
E                       return FourCornerTypePolicy(
E                           bottom_left=selections["bottom_left"],
E                           bottom_right=selections["bottom_right"],
E                           top_left=selections["top_left"],
E                           top_right=selections["top_right"],
E                           fw=float(fw),
E                           bottom_fw=cabinet_family_policy.effective_endcap_bottom_fw(
E                               snapshot,
E                               snapshot.get("box_body_structure"),
E                               thickness=thickness,
E                               default_fw=float(fw),
E                           ),
E                       )
E               except Exception:
E                   # 非受電箱與舊 snapshot 仍沿用既有通用 policy；真正的幾何錯誤會在
E                   # downstream manufacturing resolver fail closed，不在 UI adapter 猜值。
E                   pass
E               return policy_from_corner_state(selections, fw=fw)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_draw_operator_dimensions(self, x_profile, y_profile, *, triangles=None):
E               """Legacy adapter to the FinalSceneView dimension drawing implementation."""
E               view = getattr(self, "final_scene_view", None)
E               if view is None:
E                   view = Phase6FinalSceneView(self.renderer, number_text=_setting_number_text)
E               snapshot = getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E               settings = getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}
E               request = FinalSceneViewRequest(
E                   render_data=None,
E                   x_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in (x_profile or ())),
E                   y_profile=tuple(dict(seg) for seg in (y_profile or ())),
E                   part_key=str(getattr(self, "active_part_key", "") or ""),
E                   alpha_bend=float(getattr(getattr(self, "state", None), "alpha_bend", 0.85)),
E                   finished_dimensions=_phase6_operator_finished_dimensions(self),
E                   thickness=_num(settings.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2.0)), 2.0),
E               )
E               return view._draw_operator_dimensions(request, list(triangles or ()))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_render_data_for_blank(self, part_key=None):
E               """Return canonical final material for blank reporting without a second geometry path."""
E               key = str(part_key or self.designer_workspace.active_part or "")
E               if not key:
E                   return None
E               active = str(getattr(getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None), "active_part", "") or "")
E               if key == active:
E                   return _phase6_query_final_render_data(self)
E               if key in {"box_body", "head", "tail"}:
E                   return _phase6_resolve_manufacturing_geometry(self).part(key).render_data
E               callback = getattr(self, "_scene_query_callback", None)
E               if callback is None:
E                   return None
E               return callback(key, _phase6_scene_query_payload_for_part(self, key))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_format_unfolded_blank_text(render_data, *, part_key=""):
E               from ae_engine.manufacturing_api import measure_unfolded_blanks
E           
E               if render_data is None:
E                   return "展開料：-"
E               try:
E                   blanks = measure_unfolded_blanks(render_data, part_key=str(part_key or "part"))
E               except Exception:
E                   return "展開料：-"
E               if not blanks:
E                   return "展開料：-"
E           
E               piece_labels = {
E                   "left_side": "左側板", "back": "後面板", "right_side": "右側板",
E                   "left": "左箱身", "middle": "中箱身", "right": "右箱身",
E               }
E               rows = []
E               multi = len(blanks) > 1
E               for blank in blanks:
E                   label = ""
E                   if multi:
E                       suffix = str(blank.part_key).rsplit(":", 1)[-1]
E                       label = f"{piece_labels.get(suffix, suffix)} "
E                   rows.append(
E                       f"{label}{_setting_number_text(blank.width)} × {_setting_number_text(blank.height)} mm"
E                   )
E               return "展開料：" + ("；".join(rows))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_current_unfolded_size(self, part_key=None):
E               """Compatibility tuple measured from canonical final material only."""
E               from ae_engine.manufacturing_api import measure_unfolded_blanks
E           
E               key = str(part_key or self.designer_workspace.active_part or "")
E               render_data = _phase6_render_data_for_blank(self, key)
E               if render_data is None:
E                   return None
E               blanks = measure_unfolded_blanks(render_data, part_key=key)
E               if not blanks:
E                   return None
E               blank = blanks[0]
E               return float(blank.width), float(blank.height)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_update_unfolded_size_label(self):
E               var = getattr(self, "unfolded_size_var", None)
E               if var is None:
E                   return
E               key = str(getattr(getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None), "active_part", "") or "")
E               try:
E                   if getattr(self, "_phase6_initializing", False):
E                       # The authoritative startup render already attempted the canonical
E                       # manufacturing resolve. Annotation must consume that committed
E                       # result if available; it must not start a second startup solve.
E                       resolved = getattr(self, "_phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_geometry", None)
E                       render_data = resolved.part(key).render_data if (resolved is not None and key) else None
E                   else:
E                       render_data = _phase6_render_data_for_blank(self, key) if key else None
E               except Exception:
E                   render_data = None
E               var.set(_phase6_format_unfolded_blank_text(render_data, part_key=key))
E           
E           
E           _PHASE6_DEFAULT_VIEW = (50.0, -90.0)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_prepare_text_scale_controller(root, value, *, controller=None):
E               """Reuse the main GUI text scale without rescanning its whole widget tree."""
E               controller = controller or TextScaleController.for_widget(root)
E               # When Phase6 is a Toplevel, for_widget() returns the already-applied main
E               # controller whose root is the main window. Calling apply() here would walk
E               # every main-GUI widget again just to open 3D. A standalone Phase6 root still
E               # owns its controller and therefore applies the requested size once.
E               if getattr(controller, "root", None) is root:
E                   controller.apply(value)
E               return controller
E           
E           
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_operator_finished_dimensions(self, part_key=None, *, triangles=None):
E               """Return folded finished outside dimensions for the active single part."""
E               key = str(part_key or getattr(self, "active_part_key", "") or "")
E               snapshot = getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E               settings = getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {}
E               if triangles:
E                   envelope = _phase6_folded_outside_envelope(
E                       triangles, _num(settings.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2.0)), 2.0)
E                   )
E                   if envelope is not None:
E                       measured, _bounds = envelope
E                       if key == "box_body":
E                           return tuple(float(value) for value in measured)
E                       # Other individual panels are operator-facing as their two primary
E                       # finished axes; their folded flange depth stays visible in 3D but
E                       # is not mislabeled as cabinet D/H.
E                       return float(measured[0]), float(measured[1])
E               dims = dict((snapshot.get("part_dimensions") or {}).get(key, {}) or {})
E               if key == "box_body":
E                   w = _num(settings.get("w", snapshot.get("w", 0.0)), 0.0)
E                   h = _num(settings.get("h", snapshot.get("h", 0.0)), 0.0)
E                   d = _num(settings.get("d", snapshot.get("d", 0.0)), 0.0)
E                   t = _num(settings.get("t", snapshot.get("t", 2.0)), 2.0)
E                   head_policy = _phase6_corner_policy_for(self, "head")
E                   tail_policy = _phase6_corner_policy_for(self, "tail")
E                   if head_policy is not None and tail_policy is not None:
E                       try:
E                           h = box_body_height_from_corner_policies(
E                               h, t, head_corner_policy=head_policy, tail_corner_policy=tail_policy,
E                           )
E                       except Exception:
E                           pass
E                   return float(w), float(h), float(d)
E               if dims.get("width") and dims.get("height"):
E                   return float(dims["width"]), float(dims["height"])
E               if key in {"head", "tail"}:
E                   return float(snapshot.get("w", 0.0)), float(snapshot.get("d", 0.0))
E               return None
E

E           def _phase6_on_assembly_diagnostic_changed(self):
E               if str(getattr(self, "_phase6_3d_display_mode", "single") or "single") == "assembly":
E                   self.do_update()
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_create_relief_promotion_candidates(self):
E               """Build non-mutating manifests for verified PROVISIONAL_3D solutions."""
E               from ae_engine.certified_relief_registry import build_relief_promotion_candidate
E           
E               solutions = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_last_relief_solutions", {}) or {})
E               snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {})
E               intent = getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY)
E               family = _phase6_current_cabinet_family(self)
E               candidates = {}
E               for part_key in ("head", "tail"):
E                   solution = solutions.get(part_key)
E                   if solution is None:
E                       continue
E                   if not bool(getattr(solution, "verified", False)):
E                       continue
E                   if str(getattr(solution, "trust_level", "") or "") != "PROVISIONAL_3D":
E                       continue
E                   candidates[part_key] = build_relief_promotion_candidate(
E                       solution,
E                       cabinet_family=family,
E                       part_role=part_key,
E                       joint_face="TOP",
E                       assembly_intent=intent,
E                       source_signature=snapshot,
E                   )
E               self._phase6_last_relief_promotion_candidates = candidates
E               status_var = getattr(self, "assembly_collision_status_var", None)
E               if status_var is not None and callable(getattr(status_var, "set", None)):
E                   if candidates:
E                       status_var.set(
E                           "認證候選：" + " / ".join("封頭" if key == "head" else "封尾" for key in candidates)
E                           + "（僅建立候選，不修改正式資料庫）"
E                       )
E                   else:
E                       status_var.set("認證候選：目前沒有 verified PROVISIONAL_3D 結果")
E               return candidates
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_refresh_joint_diagnostic_menu(self, resolved=None):
E               var = getattr(self, "assembly_joint_diag_var", None)
E               button = getattr(self, "assembly_joint_diag_button", None)
E               if var is None or button is None:
E                   return ()
E               resolved = resolved or getattr(self, "_phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_geometry", None)
E               diagnostics = tuple(getattr(resolved, "diagnostics", ()) or ()) if resolved is not None else ()
E               menu = getattr(self, "assembly_joint_diag_menu", None)
E               if menu is None:
E                   menu_name = str(button.cget("menu") or "")
E                   if not menu_name:
E                       return ()
E                   try:
E                       menu = button.nametowidget(menu_name)
E                   except Exception:
E                       return ()
E               menu.delete(0, "end")
E               ids = [str(getattr(item, "joint_id", "")) for item in diagnostics if str(getattr(item, "joint_id", ""))]
E               current = str(var.get() or "")
E               if current not in ids:
E                   current = ids[0] if ids else ""
E                   var.set(current)
E               for joint_id in ids:
E                   menu.add_radiobutton(
E                       label=joint_id, value=joint_id, variable=var,
E                       command=lambda: _phase6_on_assembly_diagnostic_changed(self),
E                   )
E               button.configure(text=(current or "Joint"))
E               return tuple(ids)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_selected_joint_diagnostic(self):
E               resolved = getattr(self, "_phase6_last_resolved_manufacturing_geometry", None)
E               if resolved is None:
E                   return None
E               joint_id = str(getattr(getattr(self, "assembly_joint_diag_var", None), "get", lambda: "")() or "")
E               if not joint_id:
E                   return None
E               try:
E                   return resolved.joint_diagnostic(joint_id)
E               except Exception:
E                   return None
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_assembly_diagnostics(self):
E               frame = original.ttk.LabelFrame(self.right, text="組合體診斷", padding=6)
E               self.assembly_diagnostics_frame = frame
E               self.assembly_ignore_fixed_corner_var = original.tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
E               self.assembly_show_interference_var = original.tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
E               self.assembly_relief_clearance_var = original.tk.StringVar(value="0")
E               self.assembly_relief_size_var = original.tk.StringVar(value="實際截角尺寸：等待計算")
E               self.assembly_collision_status_var = original.tk.StringVar(value="3D驗證：等待計算")
E               original.ttk.Checkbutton(
E                   frame, text="未知組合允許3D求截角",
E                   variable=self.assembly_ignore_fixed_corner_var,
E                   command=lambda: _phase6_on_assembly_diagnostic_changed(self),
E               ).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 10))
E               original.ttk.Label(frame, text="淨空 A").pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 4))
E               self.assembly_relief_clearance_entry = original.ttk.Entry(
E                   frame, textvariable=self.assembly_relief_clearance_var, width=7
E               )
E               self.assembly_relief_clearance_entry.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 10))
E               self.assembly_relief_clearance_entry.bind(
E                   "<Return>", lambda _event: _phase6_on_assembly_diagnostic_changed(self)
E               )
E               self.assembly_relief_clearance_entry.bind(
E                   "<FocusOut>", lambda _event: _phase6_on_assembly_diagnostic_changed(self)
E               )
E               original.ttk.Checkbutton(
E                   frame, text="顯示干涉碰撞區",
E                   variable=self.assembly_show_interference_var,
E                   command=lambda: _phase6_on_assembly_diagnostic_changed(self),
E               ).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 10))
E               self.assembly_relief_promotion_button = original.ttk.Button(
E                   frame, text="建立認證候選",
E                   command=lambda: _phase6_create_relief_promotion_candidates(self),
E               )
E               self.assembly_relief_promotion_button.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 10))
E               original.ttk.Label(frame, textvariable=self.assembly_relief_size_var).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 10))
E               original.ttk.Label(frame, textvariable=self.assembly_collision_status_var).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
E               frame.pack_forget()
E               return frame
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_update_assembly_diagnostic_status(self):
E               status_var = getattr(self, "assembly_collision_status_var", None)
E               size_var = getattr(self, "assembly_relief_size_var", None)
E               if status_var is None:
E                   return
E           
E               enabled_var = getattr(self, "assembly_ignore_fixed_corner_var", None)
E               fallback_enabled = bool(enabled_var.get()) if enabled_var is not None else True
E               # Main operator status stays manufacturing-focused. Joint registry/debug
E               # metadata is queried from the dedicated diagnostics tooling, not appended here.
E               solutions = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_last_relief_solutions", {}) or {})
E               if not fallback_enabled and not solutions:
E                   if size_var is not None:
E                       size_var.set("實際截角尺寸：等待資料庫查詢")
E                   status_var.set("截角來源：CERTIFIED優先；未知組合3D fallback已停用")
E                   return
E           
E               errors = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_last_relief_errors", {}) or {})
E               labels = {"head": "封頭", "tail": "封尾"}
E               if solutions:
E                   size_parts = []
E                   verify_parts = []
E                   for key in ("head", "tail"):
E                       solution = solutions.get(key)
E                       if solution is None:
E                           continue
E                       measurements = [
E                           getattr(item, "measurement", None)
E                           for item in tuple(getattr(solution, "corner_reliefs", ()) or ())
E                       ]
E                       measurements = [item for item in measurements if item is not None]
E                       texts = []
E                       for measurement in measurements:
E                           text = _phase6_relief_measurement_text(measurement)
E                           if text not in texts:
E                               texts.append(text)
E                       if texts:
E                           size_parts.append(f"{labels.get(key, key)}：{' / '.join(texts)}")
E                       verify_parts.append(f"{labels.get(key, key)}{'✓' if bool(getattr(solution, 'verified', False)) else '✗'}")
E                   if size_var is not None:
E                       size_var.set("實際截角尺寸：" + ("；".join(size_parts) if size_parts else "無需截角"))
E                   if errors:
E                       detail = "；".join(f"{labels.get(k, k)}：{v}" for k, v in errors.items())
E                       status_var.set("3D驗證：" + " ".join(verify_parts) + f"（{detail}）")
E                   elif verify_parts and all(bool(getattr(solutions[k], "verified", False)) for k in solutions):
E                       status_var.set("3D驗證：" + " ".join(verify_parts) + "（零材料穿透）")
E                   else:
E                       status_var.set("3D驗證：" + " ".join(verify_parts))
E                   return
E           
E               if errors:
E                   if size_var is not None:
E                       size_var.set("實際截角尺寸：求解失敗")
E                   detail = "；".join(f"{labels.get(k, k)}：{v}" for k, v in errors.items())
E                   status_var.set(f"3D驗證：{detail}")
E                   return
E           
E               if size_var is not None:
E                   size_var.set("實際截角尺寸：等待計算")
E               status_var.set("3D驗證：等待計算")
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_build_settings_center(self):
E               renderer_widget = self.renderer.canvas.get_tk_widget()
E               renderer_widget.pack_forget()
E               panel = _phase6_ensure_settings_panel(self)
E               panel.build_settings_center(self.right)
E               _phase6_build_assembly_diagnostics(self)
E               renderer_widget.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True)
E               _phase6_sync_settings_panel_compat(self)
E

E           def _phase6_refresh_persistent_structure_controls(self):
E               state = _phase6_box_structure_state(self)
E               active = BoxBodyStructureType(state["active_type"])
E               var = getattr(self, "structure_type_var", None)
E               if var is not None:
E                   value = _BOX_STRUCTURE_LABELS[active]
E                   if var.get() != value:
E                       var.set(value)
E               button = getattr(self, "structure_choice_button", None)
E               if button is not None:
E                   fixed_family = cabinet_family_policy.family_fixes_box_body_structure(
E                       getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E                   )
E                   button.configure(state=("disabled" if fixed_family else "normal"))
E           
E               assembly_var = getattr(self, "assembly_type_var", None)
E               if assembly_var is not None:
E                   label = ASSEMBLY_TYPE_LABELS[getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY)]
E                   if assembly_var.get() != label:
E                       assembly_var.set(label)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_pack_right_panel_above_canvas(self, widget):
E               """Pack a right-side settings/diagnostic panel before the expanding 3D canvas.
E           
E               Tk pack order matters: if the canvas is already packed with fill=BOTH and
E               expand=True, packing a settings frame afterwards can leave the frame at
E               1x1 pixels even though winfo_manager() reports "pack".
E               """
E               if widget is None:
E                   return False
E               canvas_widget = self.renderer.canvas.get_tk_widget()
E               options = dict(side=original.tk.TOP, fill=original.tk.X, pady=(0, 6))
E               if canvas_widget.winfo_manager() == "pack" and canvas_widget.master is widget.master:
E                   widget.pack(before=canvas_widget, **options)
E               else:
E                   widget.pack(**options)
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_toggle_parameter_panel(self):
E               self._phase6_parameters_unlocked = not bool(getattr(self, "_phase6_parameters_unlocked", False))
E               unlocked = self._phase6_parameters_unlocked
E               button = getattr(self, "parameter_lock_button", None)
E               if button is not None:
E                   button.configure(text=("參數解鎖" if unlocked else "參數鎖定"))
E           
E               center = getattr(self, "settings_center", None)
E               active = str(getattr(self, "active_part_key", None) or "box_body")
E               assembly_selected = str(getattr(self, "_phase6_3d_display_mode", "single") or "single") == "assembly"
E               diagnostics = getattr(self, "assembly_diagnostics_frame", None)
E               if unlocked and assembly_selected:
E                   if center is not None and center.winfo_manager():
E                       center.pack_forget()
E                   if diagnostics is not None and not diagnostics.winfo_manager():
E                       _phase6_pack_right_panel_above_canvas(self, diagnostics)
E                   _phase6_update_assembly_diagnostic_status(self)
E               elif unlocked:
E                   if diagnostics is not None and diagnostics.winfo_manager():
E                       diagnostics.pack_forget()
E                   _phase6_invalidate_settings_page(self, active)
E                   _phase6_render_settings_context(self, active)
E                   if center is not None and not center.winfo_manager():
E                       _phase6_pack_right_panel_above_canvas(self, center)
E               else:
E                   if center is not None and center.winfo_manager():
E                       center.pack_forget()
E                   if diagnostics is not None and diagnostics.winfo_manager():
E                       diagnostics.pack_forget()
E               return unlocked
E           
E           
E           def _hide_original_structure_mode_controls(root_widget):
E               """Hide only the prototype's user-visible mode chooser; keep its internals."""
E               targets = {"標準十字型", "金庫型(三件)", "結構:"}
E               for child in root_widget.winfo_children():
E                   try:
E                       text = str(child.cget("text"))
E                   except Exception:
E                       text = ""
E                   if text in targets:
E                       manager = child.winfo_manager()
E                       if manager == "pack":
E                           child.pack_forget()
E                       elif manager == "grid":
E                           child.grid_remove()
E                       elif manager == "place":
E                           child.place_forget()
E                   _hide_original_structure_mode_controls(child)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_on_assembly_part_visibility_changed(self):
E               if str(getattr(self, "_phase6_3d_display_mode", "single") or "single") == "assembly":
E                   self.do_update()
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_scroll_assembly_parts(self, event):
E               canvas = getattr(self, "assembly_parts_canvas", None)
E               if canvas is None:
E                   return "break"
E               delta = int(getattr(event, "delta", 0) or 0)
E               number = int(getattr(event, "num", 0) or 0)
E               if number == 4:
E                   steps = -1
E               elif number == 5:
E                   steps = 1
E               elif delta:
E                   steps = -1 if delta > 0 else 1
E               else:
E                   return "break"
E               try:
E                   canvas.yview_scroll(steps, "units")
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               return "break"
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_bind_assembly_scroll(widget, self):
E               try:
E                   widget.bind("<MouseWheel>", lambda e: _phase6_scroll_assembly_parts(self, e))
E                   widget.bind("<Button-4>", lambda e: _phase6_scroll_assembly_parts(self, e))
E                   widget.bind("<Button-5>", lambda e: _phase6_scroll_assembly_parts(self, e))
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               for child in tuple(getattr(widget, "winfo_children", lambda: ())()):
E                   _phase6_bind_assembly_scroll(child, self)
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_refresh_assembly_parts_panel(self):
E               panel = getattr(self, "assembly_parts_content", None) or getattr(self, "assembly_parts_panel", None)
E               if panel is None:
E                   return
E               old_visible = {
E                   key: bool(var.get()) for key, var in dict(getattr(self, "assembly_part_visible_vars", {}) or {}).items()
E               }
E               old_text = {
E                   key: str(var.get()) for key, var in dict(getattr(self, "assembly_part_corner_vars", {}) or {}).items()
E               }
E               old_blank = {
E                   key: str(var.get()) for key, var in dict(getattr(self, "assembly_part_blank_vars", {}) or {}).items()
E               }
E               for child in panel.winfo_children():
E                   child.destroy()
E               self.assembly_part_visible_vars = {}
E               self.assembly_part_corner_vars = {}
E               self.assembly_part_blank_vars = {}
E               self.assembly_part_checkbuttons = {}
E               for key in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   row = original.ttk.Frame(panel)
E                   row.pack(fill=original.tk.X, pady=(0, 4))
E                   visible = original.tk.BooleanVar(value=old_visible.get(key, True))
E                   size_text = original.tk.StringVar(value=old_text.get(key, "截角尺寸：等待3D"))
E                   blank_text = original.tk.StringVar(value=old_blank.get(key, "展開料：等待3D"))
E                   check = original.ttk.Checkbutton(
E                       row, text=_phase6_part_label(key), variable=visible,
E                       command=lambda: _phase6_on_assembly_part_visibility_changed(self),
E                   )
E                   check.pack(anchor=original.tk.W)
E                   original.ttk.Label(
E                       row, textvariable=size_text, justify=original.tk.LEFT, wraplength=300,
E                   ).pack(fill=original.tk.X, padx=(20, 0))
E                   original.ttk.Label(
E                       row, textvariable=blank_text, justify=original.tk.LEFT, wraplength=300,
E                   ).pack(fill=original.tk.X, padx=(20, 0))
E                   self.assembly_part_visible_vars[key] = visible
E                   self.assembly_part_corner_vars[key] = size_text
E                   self.assembly_part_blank_vars[key] = blank_text
E                   self.assembly_part_checkbuttons[key] = check
E                   _phase6_bind_assembly_scroll(row, self)
E               _phase6_bind_assembly_scroll(panel, self)
E               canvas = getattr(self, "assembly_parts_canvas", None)
E               if canvas is not None:
E                   try:
E                       canvas.configure(scrollregion=canvas.bbox("all"))
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E

E           # Patch methods onto the FIX10 class instead of touching the user's original file.
E           _FIX10_INIT = Phase6FoldDesignerApp.__init__
E           _FIX10_EXPORT = Phase6FoldDesignerApp.export_phase6_snapshot
E

E           def _fix11_init(self, root, snapshot: Mapping[str, object], on_settings_change=None, on_save_defaults=None, on_corner_change=None, on_transaction_confirm=None, on_transaction_cancel=None, on_live_sync=None, on_baseline_data_query=None, on_scene_query=None, on_ui_text_size_change=None, on_project_load=None, on_project_path_change=None, on_project_save=None, on_return_2d=None):
E               # Atomic lifecycle: inherited Tk construction may invoke traced callbacks and
E               # legacy do_update() methods, but none of those bootstrap intermediates are
E               # authoritative live-sync state. Publish is disabled until the final Phase6
E               # workspace has ingested the current application snapshot and reached READY.
E               self._phase6_initializing = True
E               snapshot = migrate_legacy_snapshot_joints(dict(snapshot or {}))
E               initial_parts, initial_active = normalize_part_selection(
E                   snapshot.get("existing_parts", ("box_body", "head", "tail")),
E                   snapshot.get("active_part"),
E               )
E               workspace_snapshot = dict(snapshot)
E               workspace_snapshot["existing_parts"] = list(initial_parts)
E               workspace_snapshot["active_part"] = initial_active
E               workspace_snapshot["part_features"] = {
E                   key: _copy_features(snapshot, key) for key in initial_parts
E               }
E               workspace_snapshot["part_face_features"] = deepcopy(
E                   dict(snapshot.get("part_face_features") or {})
E               )
E               self.designer_workspace = Phase6DesignerWorkspace.from_snapshot(workspace_snapshot)
E               self._phase6_box_whd = {
E                   "w": _ui_len(snapshot.get("w", 500)),
E                   "h": _ui_len(snapshot.get("h", 600)),
E                   "d": _ui_len(snapshot.get("d", 200)),
E               }
E               self._settings_change_callback = on_settings_change
E               self._phase6_transactional_mode = on_transaction_confirm is not None and on_live_sync is None
E               self._live_sync_callback = on_live_sync
E               self._phase6_live_sync_guard = False
E               self._phase6_last_live_payload = None
E               self._phase6_last_live_state = None
E               self._phase6_last_live_fingerprint = None
E               self._phase6_sync_revision = 0
E               self._phase6_last_external_revision = 0
E               self._phase6_last_external_transaction_id = ""
E               self._phase6_active_transaction_id = ""
E               self._save_defaults_callback = on_save_defaults
E               # Kept only for backwards constructor compatibility. Corner edits are now
E               # 這些資料採交易式提交，因此絕不能觸發這個舊版即時 callback。
E               self._corner_change_callback = on_corner_change
E               self._transaction_confirm_callback = on_transaction_confirm
E               self._transaction_cancel_callback = on_transaction_cancel
E               self._baseline_data_query_callback = on_baseline_data_query
E               self._scene_query_callback = on_scene_query
E               self._ui_text_size_change_callback = on_ui_text_size_change
E               self._project_load_callback = on_project_load
E               self._project_path_change_callback = on_project_path_change
E               self._project_save_callback = on_project_save
E               self._return_2d_callback = on_return_2d
E               self._phase6_current_project_path = str(snapshot.get("_runtime_project_path") or "").strip() or None
E               self._factory_defaults = dict(snapshot.get("factory_defaults") or {})
E               self._baseline_models = list(snapshot.get("baseline_models") or ())
E               self._phase6_baseline_initial_model = normalize_custom_model_name(snapshot.get("model"))
E               if self._phase6_baseline_initial_model and self._phase6_baseline_initial_model not in self._baseline_models:
E                   self._baseline_models.append(self._phase6_baseline_initial_model)
E               self._baseline_unknown_value = str(snapshot.get("baseline_unknown_value") or CUSTOM_MODEL_NAME).strip()
E               if self._baseline_unknown_value and self._baseline_unknown_value not in self._baseline_models:
E                   self._baseline_models.append(self._baseline_unknown_value)
E               self._phase6_baseline_last_model = self._phase6_baseline_initial_model
E               self._phase6_baseline_guard = False
E               self._phase6_settings_guard = False
E               self._phase6_corner_guard = False
E               # UI-only fine-parameter locks. Never serialize into .p6fold.
E               self._phase6_corner_param_unlocked = {}
E               self.corner_pair_checkbuttons = {}
E               self._phase6_pending_settings = {}
E               self._phase6_settings_debounce_job = None
E               self._phase6_settings_rendering = False
E               self._settings_values = dict(snapshot.get("settings") or {})
E               for key, value in snapshot.items():
E                   if key not in self._settings_values and any(spec.key == key for spec in settings_for_context(GLOBAL_CONTEXT) + sum((settings_for_context(p) for p in KNOWN_PARTS), ())):
E                       self._settings_values[key] = value
E               # Constructor top-level dimensions are the current authoritative application
E               # state. Nested settings may be persisted defaults from an older save, so a
E               # stale mirror must never win over the values the operator just entered.
E               for key in ("w", "h", "d", "t", "fw"):
E                   if key in snapshot:
E                       self._settings_values[key] = snapshot[key]
E               self._phase6_corner_state = deepcopy(snapshot.get("corner_state") or {})
E               self._phase6_corner_pair_same = deepcopy(snapshot.get("corner_pair_same") or {})
E               self._phase6_assembly_type = resolve_box_assembly_type(snapshot)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot = dict(getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or snapshot)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot.pop("_runtime_project_path", None)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["assembly_type"] = assembly_intent_value(self._phase6_assembly_type)
E               self._phase6_endcap_fw_state = normalize_endcap_fw_state(snapshot)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(self._phase6_endcap_fw_state)
E               self._phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state = normalize_endcap_bottom_wrap_state(snapshot)
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["endcap_bottom_wrap"] = deepcopy(self._phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state)
E               self._corner_transaction_unknown_state = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_state)
E               self._corner_transaction_unknown_pairs = deepcopy(self._phase6_corner_pair_same)
E               self._corner_editable = _phase6_is_unknown_baseline(self, self._phase6_baseline_initial_model)
E               self._ui_text_controller = _phase6_prepare_text_scale_controller(
E                   root, self._settings_values.get("ui_text_size", "small")
E               )
E           
E               # During the inherited/original MainApp constructor, do_update() is invoked
E               # twice before Phase6 installs its Final Part Geometry renderer.  Suppress
E               # those legacy geometry draws completely; opening the 3D workspace should
E               # construct UI state only and render nothing until a Phase6 part is selected.
E               self.preview_3d_enabled = False
E               _FIX10_INIT(self, root, snapshot)
E               # FIX10 marks itself ready as soon as its legacy snapshot is loaded. Phase6
E               # still has to build the persistent controls/workspace, so keep the public
E               # lifecycle in INITIALIZING until the complete view state is settled.
E               self._phase6_sync_ready = False
E               self.state.phase6_thickness = _num(snapshot.get("t", (snapshot.get("settings") or {}).get("t", 2.0)), 2.0)
E               self.state.ui_text_scale = self._ui_text_controller.factor
E               self.preview_3d_enabled = True
E               _hide_original_structure_mode_controls(self.left)
E               _hide_original_global_dimension_controls(self.left)
E               _phase6_hide_original_visual_controls(self.left)
E               self._phase6_parameters_unlocked = False
E               self._phase6_3d_display_mode = "assembly"
E               self._phase6_fullscreen = False
E               self.right_control_bar = None
E               _phase6_build_persistent_top_area(self)
E               try:
E                   self.root.title("Phase6 折彎 / 3D 設計")
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E               # 為了讓未修改的 Renderer 繼續運作，保留舊 notebook 與 HolesUI/state；
E               # 但畫面上不再顯示整層舊「module」介面。
E               tabs = list(self.main_nb.tabs())
E               if len(tabs) > 1:
E                   self.main_nb.forget(tabs[1])
E               self.main_nb.pack_forget()
E           
E               stored_profiles = snapshot.get("part_profiles") or {}
E               for key in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   if key == "box_body":
E                       continue
E                   source = stored_profiles.get(key)
E                   if source:
E                       loaded = {
E                           "X": clone_profile(source.get("X", [])),
E                           "Y": clone_profile(source.get("Y", [])),
E                       }
E                       normalized = (
E                           _phase6_normalize_endcap_profile_order(loaded, snapshot, key)
E                           if key in {"head", "tail"} else loaded
E                       )
E                       self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, normalized)
E                   elif key in {"head", "tail"}:
E                       self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, build_endcap_xy_profiles(snapshot, part_key=key))
E                   else:
E                       self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, build_standard_part_profiles(snapshot, key))
E           
E               # Head/tail mating fold topology is derived from the box chain. Stored
E               # project copies are diagnostic only and may come from an older topology.
E               _phase6_rebuild_linked_endcaps(self)
E               _phase6_sync_authoritative_derived_parts(self)
E           
E               # 進入 3D 直接進箱身；不再建立首頁 landing state。
E               self.designer_workspace.active_part = None
E               self.designer_workspace.selected_part = None
E           
E               # 板件選擇／新增／刪除固定同一列，永不因切換板件消失。
E               self.part_selector = original.ttk.Frame(self.left)
E               self.part_selector.pack(fill=original.tk.X, pady=(0, 4))
E               self.part_var = original.tk.StringVar(master=self.part_selector, value="組合體")
E               self.part_buttons = {}
E               self.part_choice_button = original.ttk.Menubutton(self.part_selector, textvariable=self.part_var)
E               self.part_choice_menu = original.tk.Menu(self.part_choice_button, tearoff=False)
E               self.part_choice_button.configure(menu=self.part_choice_menu)
E               self.part_choice_button.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, fill=original.tk.X, expand=True, padx=(0, 4))
E           
E               self.add_part_button = original.ttk.Menubutton(self.part_selector, text="新增 ▼")
E               self.add_part_menu = original.tk.Menu(self.add_part_button, tearoff=False)
E               self.add_part_button.configure(menu=self.add_part_menu)
E               self.add_part_button.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=4)
E               self.remove_part_button = original.ttk.Button(
E                   self.part_selector, text="刪除", command=self.remove_selected_part, state="disabled"
E               )
E               self.remove_part_button.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(4, 0))
E           
E               # 組合體內容直接使用左側剩餘空間；不再用帶標題/框線的 LabelFrame
E               # 切出一塊獨立「組合圖」區域。
E               self.assembly_parts_panel = original.ttk.Frame(self.left, padding=6)
E               assembly_scroll_host = original.ttk.Frame(self.assembly_parts_panel)
E               assembly_scroll_host.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True)
E               self.assembly_parts_canvas = original.tk.Canvas(
E                   assembly_scroll_host, height=1, highlightthickness=0, borderwidth=0
E               )
E               self.assembly_parts_scrollbar = original.ttk.Scrollbar(
E                   assembly_scroll_host, orient=original.tk.VERTICAL, command=self.assembly_parts_canvas.yview
E               )
E               self.assembly_parts_canvas.configure(yscrollcommand=self.assembly_parts_scrollbar.set)
E               self.assembly_parts_scrollbar.pack(side=original.tk.RIGHT, fill=original.tk.Y)
E               self.assembly_parts_canvas.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True)
E               self.assembly_parts_content = original.ttk.Frame(self.assembly_parts_canvas)
E               self._assembly_parts_window = self.assembly_parts_canvas.create_window(
E                   (0, 0), window=self.assembly_parts_content, anchor="nw"
E               )
E               self.assembly_parts_content.bind(
E                   "<Configure>",
E                   lambda _e: self.assembly_parts_canvas.configure(scrollregion=self.assembly_parts_canvas.bbox("all")),
E               )
E               self.assembly_parts_canvas.bind(
E                   "<Configure>",
E                   lambda e: self.assembly_parts_canvas.itemconfigure(self._assembly_parts_window, width=max(1, e.width)),
E               )
E               _phase6_bind_assembly_scroll(self.assembly_parts_canvas, self)
E               _phase6_bind_assembly_scroll(self.assembly_parts_content, self)
E               self.assembly_part_visible_vars = {}
E               self.assembly_part_corner_vars = {}
E               self.assembly_part_blank_vars = {}
E               self.assembly_part_checkbuttons = {}
E               _phase6_refresh_assembly_parts_panel(self)
E           
E               self._refresh_part_buttons()
E               self._refresh_add_part_menu()
E               try:
E                   self.root.bind("<Delete>", lambda _e: self.remove_selected_part())
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E           
E               # 左側輸入區永久存在；右側只有參數面板受鎖定控制。
E               self.fold_editor_host = original.ttk.Frame(self.left)
E               self.bend_ui = Phase6BendingUI(self.fold_editor_host, self.state, self.queue_update)
E               self.fold_editor_host.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True, pady=(0, 10))
E               _phase6_build_settings_center(self)
E               _phase6_install_renderer_view(self)
E               self.settings_center.pack_forget()
E               self.activate_part("box_body", initial=True)
E               _phase6_show_assembly(self, initial=True)
E               self.designer_workspace.mark_clean()
E           
E               # READY starts from the exact authoritative state already displayed. Seed the
E               # live fingerprint without invoking the callback so opening 3D never creates
E               # a synthetic revision, and a no-edit close/force-publish remains a no-op.
E               initial_live_state = _phase6_corner_transaction_payload(self)
E               self._phase6_last_live_state = deepcopy(initial_live_state)
E               self._phase6_last_live_fingerprint = stable_fingerprint(initial_live_state)
E               self._phase6_last_live_payload = None
E               self._phase6_initializing = False
E               self._phase6_sync_ready = True
E
