E           def _fix11_refresh_part_buttons(self):
E               self.part_buttons = {}
E               menu = getattr(self, "part_choice_menu", None)
E               if menu is not None:
E                   menu.delete(0, original.tk.END)
E                   menu.add_radiobutton(
E                       label="組合體",
E                       variable=self.part_var,
E                       value="組合體",
E                       command=lambda: _phase6_show_assembly(self),
E                   )
E                   for key in self.available_parts:
E                       menu.add_radiobutton(
E                           label=_phase6_part_label(key),
E                           variable=self.part_var,
E                           value=_phase6_part_label(key),
E                           command=lambda k=key: self.activate_part(k),
E                       )
E               mode = str(getattr(self, "_phase6_3d_display_mode", "assembly") or "assembly")
E               active = getattr(self, "active_part_key", None)
E               if hasattr(self, "part_var"):
E                   if mode == "assembly":
E                       self.part_var.set("組合體")
E                   elif active in self.available_parts:
E                       self.part_var.set(_phase6_part_label(active))
E               self._refresh_part_button_states()
E               if getattr(self, "assembly_parts_panel", None) is not None:
E                   _phase6_refresh_assembly_parts_panel(self)
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_refresh_part_button_states(self):
E               selected = getattr(self, "selected_part_key", None)
E               for button in self.part_buttons.values():
E                   button.state(["!disabled"])
E               delete = getattr(self, "remove_part_button", None)
E               if delete is not None:
E                   delete.configure(
E                       state=("normal" if selected in self.available_parts and selected != "box_body" and not _phase6_is_derived_physical_part_key(selected) else "disabled")
E                   )
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_show_assembly(self, initial=False):
E               """Show the structural cabinet assembly while retaining a real active part as geometry backing."""
E               if not initial and getattr(self, "_phase6_pending_settings", None):
E                   self.flush_pending_settings()
E               before_signature = None
E               if not initial:
E                   try:
E                       before_signature = _phase6_manufacturing_state_signature(self)
E                   except Exception:
E                       before_signature = None
E               if not initial and self.designer_workspace.active_part is not None:
E                   try:
E                       self._save_current_part()
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E               self.designer_workspace.selected_part = None
E               self._phase6_3d_display_mode = "assembly"
E               if hasattr(self, "part_var"):
E                   self.part_var.set("組合體")
E               if getattr(self, "fold_editor_host", None) is not None and self.fold_editor_host.winfo_manager():
E                   self.fold_editor_host.pack_forget()
E               assembly_panel = getattr(self, "assembly_parts_panel", None)
E               if assembly_panel is not None and not assembly_panel.winfo_manager():
E                   assembly_panel.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True, pady=(0, 8))
E               center = getattr(self, "settings_center", None)
E               if center is not None and center.winfo_manager():
E                   center.pack_forget()
E               diagnostics = getattr(self, "assembly_diagnostics_frame", None)
E               if diagnostics is not None:
E                   if bool(getattr(self, "_phase6_parameters_unlocked", False)):
E                       if not diagnostics.winfo_manager():
E                           _phase6_pack_right_panel_above_canvas(self, diagnostics)
E                   elif diagnostics.winfo_manager():
E                       diagnostics.pack_forget()
E               delete = getattr(self, "remove_part_button", None)
E               if delete is not None:
E                   delete.configure(state="disabled")
E               canvas_widget = self.renderer.canvas.get_tk_widget()
E               if not canvas_widget.winfo_manager():
E                   canvas_widget.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True)
E               if not initial:
E                   try:
E                       after_signature = _phase6_manufacturing_state_signature(self)
E                   except Exception:
E                       after_signature = None
E                   reason = "display" if before_signature is not None and before_signature == after_signature else "geometry"
E                   submit = getattr(self, "submit_update_intent", None)
E                   if callable(submit):
E                       submit(reason, commit=True)
E                   else:
E                       self.do_update()
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_select_part(self, key):
E               key = str(key or "")
E               if not self.designer_workspace.select_part(key):
E                   return False
E               if hasattr(self, "part_var"):
E                   self.part_var.set(_phase6_part_label(key))
E               self._refresh_part_button_states()
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_activate_selected_part(self):
E               key = getattr(self, "selected_part_key", None)
E               if key not in self.available_parts:
E                   return False
E               self.activate_part(key)
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_remove_selected_part(self):
E               key = self.designer_workspace.selected_part
E               if key not in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   return False
E               result = self.remove_part(key)
E               if result:
E                   self._refresh_part_button_states()
E               return result
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_refresh_add_part_menu(self):
E               self.add_part_menu.delete(0, original.tk.END)
E               missing = [key for key in KNOWN_PARTS if key not in self.available_parts]
E               if not missing:
E                   self.add_part_menu.add_command(label="沒有可新增板件", state="disabled")
E                   return
E               for key in missing:
E                   self.add_part_menu.add_command(label=_phase6_part_label(key), command=lambda k=key: self.add_part(k))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_refresh_linked_part_profiles(self, changed_keys):
E               """Refresh non-active parts from one shared cabinet state.
E           
E               Head/Tail Y is derived from the authoritative BoxBody Fold Chain.  Their FW
E               is resolved independently through the per-endcap follow/override state, so
E               changing the box FW never overwrites a detached end cap.
E               """
E               if not {"w", "h", "d", "t", "fw"}.intersection(set(changed_keys or ())):
E                   return
E               snapshot = self._phase6_input_snapshot
E               snapshot.update(self._settings_values)
E               snapshot["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(
E                   getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", normalize_endcap_fw_state(snapshot))
E               )
E               _phase6_recalculate_part_dimensions(self)
E               active = self.designer_workspace.active_part
E           
E               if active != "box_body" and "box_body" in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   current = self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])
E                   self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"] = merge_box_body_profile(current, snapshot)
E           
E               box_profile = self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])
E               linked = build_linked_endcap_xy_profiles(snapshot, box_profile)
E               for key in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   if key == active or key == "box_body":
E                       continue
E                   existing = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(key, {}) or {}
E                   if key in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS:
E                       # X remains the end-cap's local flange axis; Y is mating topology
E                       # derived from the current box chain and must never be replaced by a
E                       # stale independently stored profile.
E                       defaults = linked[key]
E                       x_merged = _merge_keyed_profiles(
E                           {"X": existing.get("X", ())}, {"X": defaults.get("X", ())}
E                       ).get("X", clone_profile(defaults.get("X", ())))
E                       self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, {
E                           "X": x_merged,
E                           "Y": clone_profile(defaults.get("Y", ())),
E                       })
E                   else:
E                       defaults = build_standard_part_profiles(snapshot, key)
E                       self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, _merge_keyed_profiles(existing, defaults))
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_store_editor_values(self, values, *, notify=True):
E               """Accept authoritative X/Y/FoldChain edits into the shared settings state.
E           
E               Part switching calls this to persist the outgoing editor.  Do not wake the
E               main GUI when the persisted values are unchanged, and when something did
E               change send only those keys.  The main GUI callback accepts partial setting
E               payloads; sending the whole settings dictionary forced a full AE/main-preview
E               recalculation on every part switch even when the operator changed nothing.
E               """
E               clean = dict(values or {})
E               if not clean:
E                   return
E           
E               changed_settings = {}
E               for key, value in list(clean.items()):
E                   if key not in self._settings_values:
E                       continue
E                   old = self._settings_values[key]
E                   if key == "ui_text_size":
E                       normalized = normalize_ui_text_size(value)
E                   elif isinstance(old, bool):
E                       normalized = bool(value)
E                   else:
E                       normalized = float(value)
E                   # Keep the snapshot/settings transaction on the same normalized value.
E                   # Family snapshots include string-valued ui_text_size alongside numeric
E                   # W/H/D; treating every non-bool as float aborted receiving rebases.
E                   clean[key] = normalized
E                   if key == "ui_text_size":
E                       different = normalize_ui_text_size(old) != normalized
E                   elif isinstance(old, bool):
E                       different = bool(old) != normalized
E                   else:
E                       try:
E                           different = abs(float(old) - float(normalized)) > 1e-9
E                       except (TypeError, ValueError):
E                           different = old != normalized
E                   if different:
E                       changed_settings[key] = normalized
E           
E               # A real box-FW edit is an explicit operator takeover.  Do this before
E               # rebuilding linked EndCaps so Head/Tail resolve from the same state.
E               if "fw" in changed_settings:
E                   commit_box_fw(self._phase6_endcap_fw_state, float(changed_settings["fw"]))
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(self._phase6_endcap_fw_state)
E           
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot.update(clean)
E               for key, value in clean.items():
E                   if key not in self._settings_values:
E                       continue
E                   if key == "ui_text_size":
E                       self._settings_values[key] = normalize_ui_text_size(value)
E                   elif isinstance(self._settings_values[key], bool):
E                       self._settings_values[key] = bool(value)
E                   else:
E                       self._settings_values[key] = float(value)
E               for key in ("w", "h", "d"):
E                   if key in clean:
E                       self._phase6_box_whd[key] = original.get_int(clean[key])
E               if hasattr(self, "left_global_vars"):
E                   self._phase6_settings_guard = True
E                   try:
E                       for key in ("w", "h", "d", "t", "fw"):
E                           if key not in clean or key not in self.left_global_vars:
E                               continue
E                           text = _setting_number_text(clean[key])
E                           if self.left_global_vars[key].get() != text:
E                               self.left_global_vars[key].set(text)
E                   finally:
E                       self._phase6_settings_guard = False
E               _phase6_recalculate_part_dimensions(self)
E               if {"w", "h", "d", "t", "fw"}.intersection(changed_settings):
E                   _phase6_refresh_linked_part_profiles(self, set(changed_settings))
E               if (
E                   notify
E                   and changed_settings
E                   and not getattr(self, "_phase6_applying_settings", False)
E                   and not getattr(self, "_phase6_transactional_mode", False)
E                   and self._settings_change_callback is not None
E               ):
E                   self._settings_change_callback(changed_settings)
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_save_current_part(self, notify=True):
E               if self.designer_workspace.switching:
E                   return
E               key = self.designer_workspace.active_part
E               if not key:
E                   return
E               try:
E                   self.bend_ui.save()
E               except Exception:
E                   return
E           
E               self._phase6_box_whd = {
E                   "w": original.get_int(self.v_w.get()),
E                   "h": original.get_int(self.v_h.get()),
E                   "d": original.get_int(self.v_d.get()),
E               }
E               if _phase6_is_derived_physical_part_key(key):
E                   _phase6_sync_authoritative_derived_parts(self)
E                   return
E           
E               if key == "box_body":
E                   if getattr(self, "_phase6_sync_ready", False):
E                       self._sync_dwd_with_top_whd()
E                   values = read_box_body_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"], self._phase6_input_snapshot)
E                   values["h"] = self._phase6_box_whd["h"]
E                   _phase6_store_editor_values(self, values, notify=notify)
E                   # Box FW changed: followers resolve the new value; detached end caps keep
E                   # their overrides. Rebuild both derived mating profiles once.
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(self._phase6_endcap_fw_state)
E                   _phase6_rebuild_linked_endcaps(self)
E                   return
E           
E               profiles = {
E                   "X": clone_profile(self.state.profiles.get("X", [])),
E                   "Y": clone_profile(self.state.profiles.get("Y", [])),
E               }
E               self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, profiles)
E               if key in ENDCAP_FW_PARTS:
E                   x = {str(seg.get("phase6_key")): seg for seg in profiles.get("X", ()) if seg.get("phase6_key")}
E                   y = {str(seg.get("phase6_key")): seg for seg in profiles.get("Y", ()) if seg.get("phase6_key")}
E                   required_x = {"yl1", "endcap_w_core", "yr1"}
E                   flat_x = len(profiles.get("X", ())) == 1 and profiles["X"][0].get("phase6_key") == "endcap_w_flat"
E                   if not required_x.issubset(x) and not flat_x:
E                       raise ValueError("封頭/封尾 X 折彎資料不完整")
E           
E                   # FW is cabinet frame-width semantics with per-endcap link/override. It
E                   # must never be routed through _phase6_store_editor_values(), because
E                   # that function owns the shared box settings and would back-write box FW.
E                   fw_item = self._phase6_endcap_fw_state.setdefault(
E                       key, {"follow_box": True, "value": _num(self._phase6_input_snapshot.get("fw", 25), 25)}
E                   )
E                   if "fw" in y:
E                       # Receiving EndCap Y profiles store FW in MATERIAL space
E                       # (29 outside -> 25 span at T=2).  Treating that span as the
E                       # operator value makes every Head/Tail save detach FW to 25, then
E                       # the next rebuild subtracts 2T again and the blank height drifts
E                       # by 4 mm per switch.  Vault legacy profiles still use the stored
E                       # length as the editable FW value.
E                       edited_fw = (
E                           engine_segment_length_to_ui(y["fw"])
E                           if cabinet_family_policy.endcap_fw_profile_uses_material_dimensions(
E                               self._phase6_input_snapshot
E                           )
E                           else _ui_len(y["fw"].get("len"))
E                       )
E                       effective_before = resolve_endcap_fw(
E                           self._phase6_input_snapshot, key, state=self._phase6_endcap_fw_state
E                       )
E                       if abs(float(edited_fw) - float(effective_before)) > 1e-9:
E                           commit_endcap_fw(
E                               self._phase6_endcap_fw_state, key, edited_fw,
E                               box_fw=_num(self._phase6_input_snapshot.get("fw", 25), 25),
E                           )
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(self._phase6_endcap_fw_state)
E           
E                   canonical_y_keys = {"ytop1", "fw", "endcap_d_core", "ybottom1"}
E                   if canonical_y_keys.issubset(y):
E                       values = read_endcap_xy_profiles(profiles, self._phase6_input_snapshot)
E                       values.pop("fw", None)
E                   else:
E                       # Arbitrary linked Y is derived. Only independently editable X values
E                       # are persisted here; the box chain rebuild below regenerates Y.
E                       values = (
E                           {}
E                           if flat_x else {
E                               "yl1": _ui_len(x["yl1"].get("len")),
E                               "yr1": _ui_len(x["yr1"].get("len")),
E                           }
E                       )
E                   _phase6_store_editor_values(self, values, notify=notify)
E           
E                   if "w" in values:
E                       self._phase6_box_whd["w"] = original.get_int(values["w"])
E                   if "d" in values:
E                       self._phase6_box_whd["d"] = original.get_int(values["d"])
E                   w_text = str(self._phase6_box_whd["w"])
E                   d_text = str(self._phase6_box_whd["d"])
E                   if self.v_w.get() != w_text:
E                       self.v_w.set(w_text)
E                   if self.v_d.get() != d_text:
E                       self.v_d.set(d_text)
E           
E                   # Both head and tail are regenerated from the one authoritative box
E                   # topology. Tail retains its native ordering inside the derivation.
E                   _phase6_rebuild_linked_endcaps(self)
E                   legacy = build_endcap_profile(self._phase6_input_snapshot)
E                   self.state.profiles_vault["封頭"] = clone_profile(legacy)
E                   self.state.profiles_vault["封尾"] = clone_profile(legacy)
E               else:
E                   _phase6_store_editor_values(
E                       self, read_standard_part_profiles(key, profiles, self._phase6_input_snapshot), notify=notify
E                   )
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_load_part_holes(self, key):
E               self.state.holes = {face: [] for face in self.state.faces}
E               if key == "box_body":
E                   w = _num(self._phase6_input_snapshot.get("w", 500), 500)
E                   h = _num(self._phase6_input_snapshot.get("h", 600), 600)
E                   d = _num(self._phase6_input_snapshot.get("d", 200), 200)
E                   face_dims = {"left": (d, h), "back": (w, h), "right": (d, h)}
E                   face_map = {"left": "左面", "back": "正面", "right": "右面"}
E                   for face_key, features in self._phase6_part_face_features.get("box_body", {}).items():
E                       if face_key not in face_dims or face_key not in face_map:
E                           continue
E                       fw, fh = face_dims[face_key]
E                       self.state.holes[face_map[face_key]].extend(
E                           project_features_to_original_holes(features, fw, fh)
E                       )
E                   # Legacy/unfolded surface features are still carried losslessly. For
E                   # preview, supported simple holes are appended to the front view only.
E                   self.state.holes["正面"].extend(
E                       project_features_to_original_holes(self._phase6_part_features.get(key, ()), w, h)
E                   )
E               elif _phase6_is_derived_physical_part_key(key):
E                   self.state.holes["正面"] = []
E               else:
E                   width, height = _part_preview_size(self._phase6_input_snapshot, key)
E                   self.state.holes["正面"] = project_features_to_original_holes(
E                       self._phase6_part_features.get(key, ()), width, height
E                   )
E               self.state.active_face = "正面"
E               # HolesUI is deliberately hidden in the Phase6 workspace.  Its render()
E               # performs a separate Matplotlib canvas.draw(), so drawing it on every part
E               # switch wastes time without changing any visible UI.  The shared hole state
E               # above is sufficient for the visible 3D renderer.
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_show_home(self):
E               """Legacy compatibility: 新版沒有首頁，任何舊首頁動作都回到箱身。"""
E               return self.activate_part("box_body")
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_activate_part(self, key, initial=False):
E               if key not in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   return
E               before_signature = None
E               if not initial:
E                   try:
E                       before_signature = _phase6_manufacturing_state_signature(self)
E                   except Exception:
E                       before_signature = None
E               # Assembly keeps a real part (normally box_body) as geometry backing.  A Tk
E               # Menu radiobutton updates part_var before invoking this callback, so neither
E               # active_part nor part_var can tell us that the user is leaving assembly.
E               # Capture the display mode first: assembly -> same backing part is still a
E               # real mode transition and must rebuild/show the single-part editor.
E               was_assembly = str(getattr(self, "_phase6_3d_display_mode", "single") or "single") == "assembly"
E               if not initial:
E                   self._phase6_3d_display_mode = "single"
E                   diagnostics = getattr(self, "assembly_diagnostics_frame", None)
E                   if diagnostics is not None and diagnostics.winfo_manager():
E                       diagnostics.pack_forget()
E               if not initial and getattr(self, "_phase6_pending_settings", None):
E                   self.flush_pending_settings()
E               if key == self.designer_workspace.active_part and not initial and not was_assembly:
E                   return
E           
E               # A delayed edit/preview update from the previous part must never run after
E               # the new part becomes active.  Cancel it before saving/switching state.
E               pending = getattr(self, "_job", None)
E               if pending:
E                   try:
E                       self.root.after_cancel(pending)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   self._job = None
E           
E               if not initial and self.designer_workspace.active_part is not None:
E                   self._save_current_part()
E                   # Saving head/tail can normalize W/D through traced Tk variables.  That
E                   # work belongs to the outgoing part and may enqueue a delayed update;
E                   # cancel it before the new part becomes active.
E                   pending = getattr(self, "_job", None)
E                   if pending:
E                       try:
E                           self.root.after_cancel(pending)
E                       except Exception:
E                           pass
E                       self._job = None
E           
E               assembly_panel = getattr(self, "assembly_parts_panel", None)
E               if assembly_panel is not None and assembly_panel.winfo_manager():
E                   assembly_panel.pack_forget()
E               if getattr(self, "fold_editor_host", None) is not None and not self.fold_editor_host.winfo_manager():
E                   self.fold_editor_host.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True, pady=(0, 10))
E               canvas_widget = self.renderer.canvas.get_tk_widget()
E               # Do not expose the Matplotlib canvas yet. Build/select the editor and the
E               # right settings page first so Tk settles on one final viewport size before
E               # the first visible model render.
E           
E               self.designer_workspace.begin_switch(key)
E               try:
E                   if hasattr(self, "part_var"):
E                       self.part_var.set(_phase6_part_label(key))
E                   if hasattr(self, "part_buttons"):
E                       self._refresh_part_button_states()
E                   if hasattr(self, "remove_part_button"):
E                       self.remove_part_button.configure(state=("disabled" if key == "box_body" or _phase6_is_derived_physical_part_key(key) else "normal"))
E           
E                   if key == "box_body":
E                       # Prepare the custom X-only editor BEFORE changing v_mode.  v_mode
E                       # owns one trace that rebuilds the notebook; the old code changed the
E                       # mode first and then rebuilt a second time after installing these
E                       # profiles.
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_profiles = {"X": self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"]}
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs = ["X"]
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_vault_key = "箱身"
E                       self.state.struct_mode = "vault"
E                       self.state.active_bend = "X"
E                       target_mode = "vault"
E                   else:
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_profiles = None
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_vault_key = None
E                       if key in {"head", "tail"}:
E                           default_profiles = build_endcap_xy_profiles(self._phase6_input_snapshot, part_key=key)
E                       elif _phase6_is_derived_physical_part_key(key):
E                           default_profiles = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(key, {}) or {}
E                       else:
E                           default_profiles = build_standard_part_profiles(self._phase6_input_snapshot, key)
E                       profiles = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(key)
E                       if profiles is None:
E                           profiles = default_profiles
E                           self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, profiles)
E                       self.state.profiles["X"] = clone_profile(profiles.get("X", []))
E                       self.state.profiles["Y"] = clone_profile(profiles.get("Y", []))
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs = _phase6_fold_tabs_for_part(
E                           self._phase6_input_snapshot, key
E                       )
E                       self.state.struct_mode = "standard"
E                       self.state.active_bend = (
E                           self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs[0]
E                           if self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs else "X"
E                       )
E                       self.state.enable_y = bool(self.state.profiles["Y"])
E                       if bool(self.v_ey.get()) != self.state.enable_y:
E                           self.v_ey.set(self.state.enable_y)
E                       target_mode = "standard"
E           
E                   # Rebuild exactly once.  When the Tk variable really changes, its
E                   # existing on_mode_change trace performs the rebuild; otherwise do it
E                   # explicitly.  queue_update() is suppressed while switching.
E                   if self.v_mode.get() != target_mode:
E                       self.v_mode.set(target_mode)
E                   elif (
E                       target_mode == "standard"
E                       and self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs is None
E                       and list(getattr(self.bend_ui, "tabs", ())) == ["X", "Y"]
E                   ):
E                       # Standard parts all use the same X/Y notebook shell.  Rebuilding it
E                       # destroys/recreates dozens of Tk widgets and is especially costly on
E                       # Windows.  Select X and refresh values in-place instead.
E                       try:
E                           if self.bend_ui.nb.index("current") != 0:
E                               self.bend_ui.nb.select(0)
E                       except Exception:
E                           pass
E                       self.bend_ui.refresh_active_profile()
E                   else:
E                       self.bend_ui.rebuild_tabs()
E           
E                   # W/H/D at the top always remain the cabinet-global dimensions.  Avoid
E                   # no-op StringVar.set calls because each one owns a trace callback.
E                   for var, value in (
E                       (self.v_w, self._phase6_box_whd["w"]),
E                       (self.v_h, self._phase6_box_whd["h"]),
E                       (self.v_d, self._phase6_box_whd["d"]),
E                   ):
E                       text = str(value)
E                       if var.get() != text:
E                           var.set(text)
E                   self._load_part_holes(key)
E               finally:
E                   self.designer_workspace.finish_switch()
E           
E               # Finish the variable-height settings page before making the canvas visible;
E               # otherwise Tk/Matplotlib renders once at the tall pre-settings size and once
E               # again after the settings panel changes the viewport height.
E               if hasattr(self, "settings_center"):
E                   _phase6_render_settings_context(self, key)
E                   if bool(getattr(self, "_phase6_parameters_unlocked", False)):
E                       if not self.settings_center.winfo_manager():
E                           _phase6_pack_right_panel_above_canvas(self, self.settings_center)
E                   elif self.settings_center.winfo_manager():
E                       self.settings_center.pack_forget()
E               # Settings/editor changes can leave a Matplotlib draw_idle queued even while
E               # the preview widget is hidden. Cancel that stale paint before asking Tk to
E               # settle non-canvas layout; otherwise it becomes an unnecessary first draw.
E               canvas = self.renderer.canvas
E               pending_draw = getattr(canvas, "_idle_draw_id", None)
E               if pending_draw is not None:
E                   try:
E                       self.root.after_cancel(pending_draw)
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E                   try:
E                       canvas._idle_draw_id = None
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E               try:
E                   self.root.update_idletasks()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               if not canvas_widget.winfo_manager():
E                   canvas_widget.pack(fill=original.tk.BOTH, expand=True)
E           
E               # Let Tk/Matplotlib establish the final pixel viewport before rendering the
E               # model, but suppress the resize handler's paint.  FigureCanvasTkAgg.resize()
E               # updates the Figure size first and only then calls draw_idle(); skipping that
E               # one blank/stale paint means the authoritative model is painted exactly once
E               # at the final viewport size.
E               resize_draw_idle = getattr(canvas, "draw_idle", None)
E               if callable(resize_draw_idle):
E                   canvas.draw_idle = lambda *args, **kwargs: None
E               try:
E                   self.root.update_idletasks()
E               except Exception:
E                   pass
E               finally:
E                   if callable(resize_draw_idle):
E                       canvas.draw_idle = resize_draw_idle
E           
E               try:
E                   after_signature = _phase6_manufacturing_state_signature(self)
E               except Exception:
E                   after_signature = None
E               reason = (
E                   "display"
E                   if (not initial and before_signature is not None and before_signature == after_signature)
E                   else "geometry"
E               )
E               submit = getattr(self, "submit_update_intent", None)
E               if callable(submit):
E                   submit(reason, commit=True)
E               else:
E                   self.do_update()
E               _phase6_refresh_persistent_structure_controls(self)
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_add_part(self, key):
E               key = str(key)
E               if key not in PART_LABELS:
E                   raise ValueError(f"不支援的板件: {key}")
E               if key not in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
E                   if key in {"head", "tail"}:
E                       linked = build_linked_endcap_xy_profiles(
E                           self._phase6_input_snapshot, self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])
E                       )
E                       defaults = linked[key]
E                   elif key != "box_body":
E                       defaults = build_standard_part_profiles(self._phase6_input_snapshot, key)
E                   else:
E                       defaults = None
E                   self.designer_workspace.add_part(
E                       key,
E                       default_profiles=defaults,
E                       default_features=(),
E                   )
E                   self._refresh_part_buttons()
E                   self._refresh_add_part_menu()
E               self.activate_part(key)
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_remove_part(self, key):
E               key = str(key or "")
E               was_active = self.designer_workspace.active_part == key
E               result = self.designer_workspace.remove_part(key)
E               if not result:
E                   return False
E               if was_active:
E                   try:
E                       self.activate_part("box_body")
E                   except Exception:
E                       pass
E               self._refresh_part_buttons()
E               self._refresh_add_part_menu()
E               _phase6_publish_live_state(self, force=True)
E               return True
E           
E           
E           def _fix11_export(self):
E               # Closing/exporting owns one complete snapshot handoff.  Do not also fire a
E               # live settings callback here or the main GUI recalculates twice before the
E               # designer can close.
E               self._save_current_part(notify=False)
E               current = self.designer_workspace.active_part
E               if current not in {"box_body", "head", "tail"}:
E                   self.designer_workspace.switching = True
E                   try:
E                       self.state.struct_mode = "vault"
E                       self.v_mode.set("vault")
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_profiles = None
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs = None
E                       self.state.phase6_fold_ui_vault_key = None
E                       self.state.active_bend = "箱身"
E                       self.v_w.set(str(self._phase6_box_whd["w"]))
E                       self.v_h.set(str(self._phase6_box_whd["h"]))
E                       self.v_d.set(str(self._phase6_box_whd["d"]))
E                       self.bend_ui.rebuild_tabs()
E                   finally:
E                       self.designer_workspace.switching = False
E           
E               # FIX10 仍透過舊箱身／金庫型 adapter 匯出；為了相容保留它，
E               # 但不可讓其中過期的箱身 profile 覆蓋新值
E               # that were edited in the X/Y EndCap editor. Global W/H/D are owned by the
E               # bridge, while EndCap fold/FW values are owned by the shared Phase6 snapshot.
E               result = _FIX10_EXPORT(self)
E               result["w"] = _ui_len(self._phase6_box_whd["w"])
E               result["h"] = _ui_len(self._phase6_box_whd["h"])
E               result["d"] = _ui_len(self._phase6_box_whd["d"])
E               for name in ("yl1", "yr1", "ytop1", "ybottom1"):
E                   if name in self._phase6_input_snapshot:
E                       result[name] = _ui_len(self._phase6_input_snapshot[name])
E           
E               if current in {"head", "tail"}:
E                   profiles = self.designer_workspace.profiles_for(current)
E                   if profiles:
E                       endcap_values = read_endcap_xy_profiles(profiles, self._phase6_input_snapshot)
E                       result.update(endcap_values)
E                       self._phase6_input_snapshot.update(endcap_values)
E                       self._phase6_box_whd["w"] = _ui_len(endcap_values["w"])
E                       self._phase6_box_whd["d"] = _ui_len(endcap_values["d"])
E               elif current == "box_body":
E                   # The box body is authoritative for the shared FW when it is the part
E                   # being edited. Preserve that value for later EndCap sessions.
E                   self._phase6_input_snapshot["fw"] = _ui_len(result["fw"])
E               elif "fw" in self._phase6_input_snapshot:
E                   result["fw"] = _ui_len(self._phase6_input_snapshot["fw"])
E           
E               owner_workspace = self.designer_workspace.snapshot()
E               for key, profiles in owner_workspace["part_profiles"].items():
E                   result.update(read_standard_part_profiles(key, profiles, self._phase6_input_snapshot))
E               result["assembly_type"] = assembly_intent_value(getattr(self, "_phase6_assembly_type", CornerTypeId.INSERT_OVERLAY))
E               result["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", normalize_endcap_fw_state(result)))
E               result["endcap_bottom_wrap"] = deepcopy(
E                   getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_bottom_wrap_state", normalize_endcap_bottom_wrap_state(result))
E               )
E               result["existing_parts"] = list(owner_workspace["existing_parts"])
E               result["active_part"] = current
E               result["part_profiles"] = deepcopy(owner_workspace["part_profiles"])
E               result["part_features"] = deepcopy(owner_workspace["part_features"])
E               result["part_face_features"] = deepcopy(owner_workspace["part_face_features"])
E               result["settings"] = dict(self._settings_values)
E               result["corner_state"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {}))
E               result["corner_pair_same"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_pair_same", {}))
E               result.update(self._settings_values)
E               return result
E           
E

E           def _phase6_view_property(name, default=None):
E               def getter(self):
E                   view = getattr(self, "final_scene_view", None)
E                   return getattr(view, name, default) if view is not None else default
E               def setter(self, value):
E                   view = getattr(self, "final_scene_view", None)
E                   if view is not None:
E                       setattr(view, name, value)
E               return property(getter, setter)
E

E           def _set_profile_phase6_len(profile, key, value):
E               for seg in profile or ():
E                   if seg.get("phase6_key") == key:
E                       seg["len"] = _ui_len(value)
E                       return True
E               return False
E           
E           
E           def _get_profile_phase6_len(profile, key, default=None):
E               for seg in profile or ():
E                   if seg.get("phase6_key") == key:
E                       return _ui_len(seg.get("len"))
E               return default
E           
E           
E           def _phase6_endcap_depth_comp_t(self):
E               snapshot = getattr(self, "_phase6_input_snapshot", {}) or {}
E               return cabinet_family_policy.endcap_depth_comp_t(snapshot)
E           
E           
E           def _propagate_endcap_derived_cores(self, w, d):
E               t = _num(self._phase6_input_snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
E               x_core = max(0.0, float(w) - 4.0 * t)
E               y_core = max(0.0, float(d) - _phase6_endcap_depth_comp_t(self) * t)
E               for key in ("head", "tail"):
E                   profiles = self._phase6_part_profiles.get(key)
E                   if profiles:
E                       _set_profile_phase6_len(profiles.get("X"), "endcap_w_core", x_core)
E                       _set_profile_phase6_len(profiles.get("Y"), "endcap_d_core", y_core)
E               if getattr(self, "active_part_key", None) in {"head", "tail"}:
E                   _set_profile_phase6_len(self.state.profiles.get("X"), "endcap_w_core", x_core)
E                   _set_profile_phase6_len(self.state.profiles.get("Y"), "endcap_d_core", y_core)
E           
E           
E           def _sync_active_endcap_and_global_whd(self):
E               top_w = original.get_int(self.v_w.get())
E               top_d = original.get_int(self.v_d.get())
E               last_w = original.get_int(self._phase6_last_w if self._phase6_last_w is not None else top_w)
E               last_d = original.get_int(self._phase6_last_d if self._phase6_last_d is not None else top_d)
E               t = _num(self._phase6_input_snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
E               depth_comp_t = _phase6_endcap_depth_comp_t(self)
E           
E               x_core = _get_profile_phase6_len(self.state.profiles.get("X"), "endcap_w_core")
E               y_core = _get_profile_phase6_len(self.state.profiles.get("Y"), "endcap_d_core")
E               expected_x = _ui_len(last_w - 4.0 * t)
E               expected_y = _ui_len(last_d - depth_comp_t * t)
E           
E               if top_w != last_w:
E                   new_w = top_w
E               elif x_core is not None and x_core != expected_x:
E                   new_w = _ui_len(x_core + 4.0 * t)
E                   self.v_w.set(str(new_w))
E               else:
E                   new_w = last_w
E           
E               if top_d != last_d:
E                   new_d = top_d
E               elif y_core is not None and y_core != expected_y:
E                   new_d = _ui_len(y_core + depth_comp_t * t)
E                   self.v_d.set(str(new_d))
E               else:
E                   new_d = last_d
E           
E               self._phase6_box_whd["w"] = new_w
E               self._phase6_box_whd["h"] = original.get_int(self.v_h.get())
E               self._phase6_box_whd["d"] = new_d
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["w"] = new_w
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["h"] = self._phase6_box_whd["h"]
E               self._phase6_input_snapshot["d"] = new_d
E               self._phase6_last_w = new_w
E               self._phase6_last_d = new_d
E               _propagate_endcap_derived_cores(self, new_w, new_d)
E           
E           _FIX10_DO_UPDATE = Phase6FoldDesignerApp.do_update
E           
E           def _fix11_do_update(self):
E               key = getattr(self, "active_part_key", "box_body")
E               if key == "box_body":
E                   # 折彎編輯器的軸向是唯一基準；舊金庫型 Renderer 曾使用
E                   # active_bend="箱身" only as a visual highlight flag; mutating the shared
E                   # editor state here can race Tk notebook callbacks and makes the custom
E                   # X-only profile get indexed by "箱身" (KeyError).

    def _legacy_available_parts_set(self, values):
        ws = _designer_workspace(self)

        if ws.selected_part not in ws._available_parts:
            ws._selected_part = None

    def project_features_to_original_holes(features, width, height):
        """Project supported Phase6 features into the original Renderer's hole DTO.
    
        Raw Phase6 feature objects are never edited. Profile/custom holes stay in the
        bundle but are intentionally not falsified as circles/rectangles for preview.
        """
        from ae_engine.sheetmetal_features import CircleFeature, RectFeature, feature_finished_point
    
        holes = []
        for index, feature in enumerate(features or (), start=1):
            if not isinstance(feature, (CircleFeature, RectFeature)):
                continue
            center = feature_finished_point(feature, float(width), float(height))
            if isinstance(feature, CircleFeature):
                hole = {
                    "type": "圓孔", "name": f"孔{len(holes)+1}",
                    "x": _ui_len(center.x), "y": _ui_len(center.y),
                    "d1": _ui_len(feature.diameter), "d2": 0,
                }
            else:
                hole = {
                    "type": "方孔", "name": f"孔{len(holes)+1}",
                    "x": _ui_len(center.x), "y": _ui_len(center.y),
                    "d1": _ui_len(feature.width), "d2": _ui_len(feature.height),
                }
            holes.append(hole)
        return holes
    
    
    
    def _phase6_rebuild_linked_endcaps(self):
        """Rebuild only existing head/tail derived profiles from current box topology."""
        box_profile = self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])
        snapshot = dict(self._phase6_input_snapshot)
        snapshot.update(getattr(self, "_settings_values", {}) or {})
        snapshot["corner_state"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_state", {}) or {})
        snapshot["corner_pair_same"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_corner_pair_same", {}) or {})
        snapshot["assembly_type"] = assembly_intent_value(getattr(
            self, "_phase6_assembly_type", resolve_box_assembly_type(snapshot)
        ))
        snapshot["endcap_fw"] = deepcopy(getattr(self, "_phase6_endcap_fw_state", normalize_endcap_fw_state(snapshot)))
        linked = build_linked_endcap_xy_profiles(snapshot, box_profile)
        for key in ("head", "tail"):
            if key in self.designer_workspace.available_parts:
                self.designer_workspace.stash_profiles(key, linked[key])
    
        # If Head/Tail is the live editor, replace that live cache in the same
        # transaction.  Otherwise OVERLAY can correctly update Workspace while the
        # active editor/Single3D keeps stale yl1/core/yr1 X folds until a part switch.
        active = str(getattr(getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None), "active_part", "") or "")
        if active in linked:
            fresh = linked[active]
            self.state.profiles["X"] = clone_profile(fresh.get("X", ()))
            self.state.profiles["Y"] = clone_profile(fresh.get("Y", ()))
            tabs = _phase6_fold_tabs_for_part(snapshot, active)
            self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs = list(tabs) if tabs is not None else None
            self.state.active_bend = self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs[0] if self.state.phase6_fold_ui_tabs else "X"
            if hasattr(self.state, "enable_x"):
                self.state.enable_x = bool(self.state.profiles["X"])
            self.state.enable_y = bool(self.state.profiles["Y"])
            for attr, enabled in (("v_ex", bool(self.state.profiles["X"])), ("v_ey", self.state.enable_y)):
                var = getattr(self, attr, None)
                if var is not None:
                    try:
                        if bool(var.get()) != bool(enabled):
                            var.set(bool(enabled))
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            bend_ui = getattr(self, "bend_ui", None)
            root = getattr(self, "root", None)
            if bend_ui is not None:
                scheduled_part = active
    
                def refresh_live_tabs():
                    # This callback may outlive an outgoing EndCap save during a
                    # part switch. Never rebuild the new part's editor from stale
                    # Head/Tail state; that also prevents a second queued redraw.
                    current_part = str(
                        getattr(getattr(self, "designer_workspace", None), "active_part", "") or ""
                    )
                    if current_part != scheduled_part:
                        return
                    try:
                        bend_ui.rebuild_tabs()
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                if root is not None and hasattr(root, "after_idle"):
                    root.after_idle(refresh_live_tabs)
                else:
                    refresh_live_tabs()
        return linked
    
    
    def _phase6_resolve_profile_key(active_dict: Mapping[str, object], requested: object) -> str:
        """Resolve stale notebook/renderer labels to a real editable profile key."""
        key = str(requested)
        if key in active_dict:
            return key
        if "X" in active_dict:
            return "X"
        try:
            return next(iter(active_dict))
        except StopIteration:
            raise KeyError(key)
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Thin Tk bridge: input grid stays the user's original implementation.
    # Only metadata preservation / D-W-D row locking are added here.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    import fold_designer_original as original
    
    
    class Phase6BendingUI(original.BendingUI):
        """Original BendingUI with Phase6 metadata and boundary-only conversion."""
    
        def __init__(self, parent, state, update_cb):
            super().__init__(parent, state, update_cb)
            owner = getattr(update_cb, "__self__", None)
            self.phase6_symmetry_bar = original.ttk.Frame(parent)
            self.phase6_symmetry_var = getattr(owner, "v_sy", None)
            if self.phase6_symmetry_var is None:
                self.phase6_symmetry_var = original.tk.BooleanVar(value=bool(getattr(state, "symmetric", True)))
            self.phase6_symmetry_check = original.ttk.Checkbutton(
                self.phase6_symmetry_bar,
                text="對稱折彎",
                variable=self.phase6_symmetry_var,
                command=self._phase6_on_symmetry_toggle,
            )
            self.phase6_symmetry_check.pack(side=original.tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 8))
            original.ttk.Label(
                self.phase6_symmetry_bar,
                text="開啟時，箱身兩側對應折彎同步修改／刪除",
            ).pack(side=original.tk.LEFT)
            self._phase6_refresh_symmetry_bar()
    
        def _phase6_on_symmetry_toggle(self):
            owner = getattr(self.update_cb, "__self__", None)
            if owner is not None and hasattr(owner, "v_sy"):
                _phase6_on_box_symmetry_changed(owner)
            else:
                self.state.symmetric = bool(self.phase6_symmetry_var.get())
                self._mark_workspace_dirty()
                self.update_cb()
    
        def _phase6_refresh_symmetry_bar(self):
            bar = getattr(self, "phase6_symmetry_bar", None)
            if bar is None:
                return
            show = getattr(self.state, "phase6_fold_ui_vault_key", None) == "箱身"
            if show:
                if not bar.winfo_manager():
                    bar.pack(fill=original.tk.X, pady=(0, 4), before=self.container)
                try:
                    self.phase6_symmetry_var.set(bool(getattr(self.state, "symmetric", True)))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            elif bar.winfo_manager():
                bar.pack_forget()
    
        def rebuild_tabs(self):
            custom_tabs = getattr(self.state, "phase6_fold_ui_tabs", None)
            if not custom_tabs:
                return super().rebuild_tabs()
            for tab in self.nb.tabs():
                self.nb.forget(tab)
            self.tabs.clear()
            labels = {"X": " X 軸折彎 ", "Y": " Y 軸折彎 "}
            for key in custom_tabs:
                self.nb.add(original.ttk.Frame(self.nb), text=labels.get(key, f" {key} "))
                self.tabs.append(key)
            if self.state.active_bend not in self.tabs:
                self.state.active_bend = self.tabs[0]
            self.nb.select(self.tabs.index(self.state.active_bend))
            self.render()
            self._phase6_refresh_symmetry_bar()
    
        def _mark_workspace_dirty(self):
            owner = getattr(self.update_cb, "__self__", None)
            if owner is not None and hasattr(owner, "designer_workspace"):
                owner.designer_workspace.mark_dirty()
    
        def apply_mirror(self, idx, key):
            if getattr(self, "_phase6_refreshing_controls", False):
                return
            self._mark_workspace_dirty()
            if getattr(self.state, "symmetric", False):
                active = self.get_active_dict()
                profile_key = self._active_profile_key(active)
                segs = active.get(profile_key, ())
                is_box = getattr(self.state, "phase6_fold_ui_vault_key", None) == "箱身"
                pair = {
                    "zl1": "zr1", "zr1": "zl1",
                    "zl2": "zr2", "zr2": "zl2",
                    "fw_left": "fw_right", "fw_right": "fw_left",
                    "d_left": "d_right", "d_right": "d_left",
                    "w": "w",
                }
                try:
                    source_key = str(segs[idx].get("phase6_key") or "")
                    target_idx = None
                    if is_box and source_key in pair:
                        if key == "len":
                            target_key = pair[source_key]
                            target_idx = next(
                                (i for i, seg in enumerate(segs) if str(seg.get("phase6_key") or "") == target_key),
                                None,
                            )
                        elif key == "angle" and idx + 1 < len(segs):
                            next_key = str(segs[idx + 1].get("phase6_key") or "")
                            if next_key in pair:
                                target_left = pair[next_key]
                                target_right = pair[source_key]
                                target_idx = next(
                                    (
                                        i for i in range(len(segs) - 1)
                                        if str(segs[i].get("phase6_key") or "") == target_left
                                        and str(segs[i + 1].get("phase6_key") or "") == target_right
                                    ),
                                    None,
                                )
                    if target_idx is not None and target_idx != idx and key in self.controls[target_idx]:
                        value = original.get_int(self.controls[idx][key].get())
                        self.controls[target_idx][key].set(str(value))
                        self.save(); self.update_cb()
                        return None
    
                    if is_box:
                        # Never fall back from a known structural Phase6 row to raw
                        # positional mirroring: after asymmetric add/remove history
                        # that is exactly how FW/D/Z fields became cross-wired.
                        if source_key in pair:
                            self.save(); self.update_cb()
                            return None
                        # Unkeyed operator-added folds may still mirror by position,
                        # but only when the opposite candidate is also unkeyed.
                        legacy_idx = (
                            len(self.controls) - 1 - idx
                            if key == "len" else len(self.controls) - 2 - idx
                        )
                        if 0 <= legacy_idx < len(segs):
                            candidate_key = str(segs[legacy_idx].get("phase6_key") or "")
                            if candidate_key:
                                self.save(); self.update_cb()
                                return None
                except (TypeError, ValueError, IndexError, KeyError):
                    pass
                return super().apply_mirror(idx, key)
            # Original BendingUI returns immediately when symmetry is off, which
            # leaves typed edits only in Tk variables. Phase6 must persist every
            # asymmetric edit into the authoritative Fold Chain before recomputing.
            self.save()
            self.update_cb()
            return None
    
        def refresh_active_profile(self):
            """Refresh existing editor vars when the X/Y widget topology is unchanged."""
            active_dict = self.get_active_dict()
            active_key = self._active_profile_key(active_dict)
            segs = active_dict[active_key]
            apply_outside_dimension_compensation(segs, getattr(self.state, "phase6_thickness", 2.0))
            if len(self.controls) != len(segs):
                self.render(); return False
            for ctrl, seg in zip(self.controls, segs):
                if (("angle" in ctrl) != ("angle" in seg)) or "len" not in ctrl:
                    self.render(); return False
            self._phase6_refreshing_controls = True
            try:
                for index, (ctrl, seg) in enumerate(zip(self.controls, segs)):
                    if "angle" in ctrl:
                        text = str(original.get_int(engine_angle_to_ui(seg.get("angle", 0))))
                        if ctrl["angle"].get() != text:
                            ctrl["angle"].set(text)
                    length_text = str(original.get_int(engine_segment_length_to_ui(seg)))
                    if ctrl["len"].get() != length_text:
                        ctrl["len"].set(length_text)
                    labels = self.container.grid_slaves(row=index + 1, column=5)
                    if labels:
                        core = seg.get("core")
                        material_text = f"料 {_ui_len(seg.get('len'))}"
                        labels[0].configure(text=f"{material_text} / {core}" if core else material_text)
            finally:
                self._phase6_refreshing_controls = False
            return True
    
        def get_active_dict(self):
            custom = getattr(self.state, "phase6_fold_ui_profiles", None)
            if custom is not None:
                return custom
            return super().get_active_dict()
    
        def on_tab(self, event):
            # Programmatic tab selection during part switching already renders the
            # selected profile in rebuild_tabs().  Tk still emits TabChanged later;
            # do not redraw/schedule another full 3D update when the key did not
            # actually change.  Real operator tab clicks still follow the original
            # path below.
            try:
                idx = self.nb.index("current")
            except Exception:
                return
            if not (0 <= idx < len(self.tabs)):
                return
            key = self.tabs[idx]
            if key == self.state.active_bend:
                return
            self.state.active_bend = key
            self.render()
            self._phase6_refresh_symmetry_bar()
            self.update_cb()
    
        def _active_profile_key(self, active_dict=None):
            active_dict = self.get_active_dict() if active_dict is None else active_dict
            key = _phase6_resolve_profile_key(active_dict, self.state.active_bend)
            if self.state.active_bend != key:
                self.state.active_bend = key
            return key
    
        def render(self):
            active_dict = self.get_active_dict()
            active_key = self._active_profile_key(active_dict)
            segs = active_dict[active_key]
            apply_outside_dimension_compensation(segs, getattr(self.state, "phase6_thickness", 2.0))
            saved = []
            for seg in segs:
                original_values = {}
                if "angle" in seg:
                    original_values["angle"] = seg["angle"]
                    seg["angle"] = engine_angle_to_ui(seg["angle"])
                if _num(seg.get("ui_len_add")):
                    original_values["len"] = seg["len"]
                    seg["len"] = engine_segment_length_to_ui(seg)
                if original_values:
                    saved.append((seg, original_values))
            try:
                super().render()
            finally:
                for seg, values in saved:
                    seg.update(values)
    
            for index, seg in enumerate(segs):
                row = index + 1
                # The editable value is always operator outside dimension; show the
                # authoritative material segment beside it for cutting/corner work.
                length_labels = self.container.grid_slaves(row=row, column=3)
                if length_labels:
                    try:
                        length_labels[0].configure(text="包外:")
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                core = seg.get("core")
                material_text = f"料 {_ui_len(seg.get('len'))}"
                label = f"{material_text} / {core}" if core else material_text
                original.ttk.Label(self.container, text=label).grid(row=row, column=5, padx=4)
                if core:
                    delete_widgets = self.container.grid_slaves(row=row, column=6)
                    if delete_widgets:
                        delete_widgets[0].configure(state="disabled")
    
        def save(self):
            active_dict = self.get_active_dict()
            active_key = self._active_profile_key(active_dict)
            old_segs = list(active_dict[active_key])
            thickness = getattr(self.state, "phase6_thickness", 2.0)
    
            # Build the edited bend topology first, so changing an angle and a length
            # in the same row uses the NEW adjacent-bend count for outside -> material.
            topology = []
            for ctrl in self.controls:
                seg = {"len": 0}
                if "angle" in ctrl:
                    seg["angle"] = ui_angle_to_engine(original.get_int(ctrl["angle"].get()))
                topology.append(seg)
            apply_outside_dimension_compensation(topology, thickness)
    
            new_segs = []
            for index, ctrl in enumerate(self.controls):
                old = old_segs[index] if index < len(old_segs) else {}
                ui_length = original.get_int(ctrl["len"].get())
                conversion = dict(old)
                conversion["ui_len_add"] = topology[index].get("ui_len_add", 0)
                length = ui_segment_length_to_engine(conversion, ui_length)
                seg = {"len": length}
                if "angle" in topology[index]:
                    seg["angle"] = topology[index]["angle"]
                for key in ("core", "phase6_key"):
                    if key in old:
                        seg[key] = old[key]
                new_segs.append(seg)
            apply_outside_dimension_compensation(new_segs, thickness)
            active_dict[active_key] = new_segs
            vault_key = getattr(self.state, "phase6_fold_ui_vault_key", None)
            if vault_key and active_key == "X":
                self.state.profiles_vault[vault_key] = new_segs
    
        def add(self, pos):
            self._mark_workspace_dirty()
            self.save()
            active_dict = self.get_active_dict()
            segs = active_dict[self._active_profile_key(active_dict)]
            if pos == 0:
                segs.insert(0, {"angle": 90, "len": 50, "ui_len_add": getattr(self.state, "phase6_thickness", 2.0)})
            else:
                if segs:
                    segs[-1]["angle"] = -90
                segs.append({"len": 50})
            apply_outside_dimension_compensation(segs, getattr(self.state, "phase6_thickness", 2.0))
            self.render(); self.update_cb()
    
        def remove(self, idx):
            self._mark_workspace_dirty()
            self.save()
            active_dict = self.get_active_dict()
            segs = active_dict[self._active_profile_key(active_dict)]
            if not (0 <= idx < len(segs)):
                return
            if not can_remove_segment(segs[idx]):
                return
    
            remove_indexes = [idx]
            is_symmetric_box = (
                bool(getattr(self.state, "symmetric", False))
                and getattr(self.state, "phase6_fold_ui_vault_key", None) == "箱身"
            )
            if is_symmetric_box:
                mirror_idx = len(segs) - 1 - idx
                if mirror_idx != idx:
                    if not (0 <= mirror_idx < len(segs)) or not can_remove_segment(segs[mirror_idx]):
                        return
                    remove_indexes.append(mirror_idx)
    
            for remove_idx in sorted(set(remove_indexes), reverse=True):
                segs.pop(remove_idx)
            if segs and "angle" in segs[-1]:
                del segs[-1]["angle"]
            apply_outside_dimension_compensation(segs, getattr(self.state, "phase6_thickness", 2.0))
            self.render(); self.update_cb()
    
    
    class Phase6FoldDesignerApp(original.MainApp):
        """Original MainApp loaded with Phase6 data; Renderer is untouched."""
    
        def __init__(self, root, snapshot: Mapping[str, object]):
            self._phase6_input_snapshot = dict(snapshot)
            self._phase6_sync_ready = False
            self._phase6_last_w = None
            self._phase6_last_d = None
            self._phase6_destroying = False
            saved_bending_ui = original.BendingUI
            original.BendingUI = Phase6BendingUI
            try:
                super().__init__(root)
            finally:
                original.BendingUI = saved_bending_ui
            # Tk ``after`` jobs belong to the Tcl interpreter, not to the Toplevel
            # that scheduled them.  Destroying the designer therefore does not
            # automatically clear Python-side job ownership.  Bind cleanup to the
            # designer root itself so direct ``Toplevel.destroy()`` callers and the
            # normal GUI close path share the same teardown invariant.
            self.root.bind("<Destroy>", self._phase6_on_root_destroy, add="+")
            self.load_phase6_snapshot(snapshot)
    
        def _phase6_cancel_owned_tk_jobs(self):
            for attr in ("_job", "_phase6_settings_debounce_job"):
                job = getattr(self, attr, None)
                if job is not None:
                    try:
                        self.root.after_cancel(job)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                setattr(self, attr, None)
            if hasattr(self, "_phase6_pending_settings"):
                self._phase6_pending_settings = {}
    
        def _phase6_on_root_destroy(self, event):
            if getattr(event, "widget", None) is not self.root:
                return
            self._phase6_destroying = True
            self._phase6_cancel_owned_tk_jobs()
    
        def load_phase6_snapshot(self, snapshot: Mapping[str, object]):
            self._phase6_input_snapshot = dict(snapshot)
            stored = snapshot.get("box_body_profile")
            if stored:
                self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"] = merge_box_body_profile(stored, snapshot)
            else:
                self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"] = build_box_body_profile(snapshot)
            endcap = build_endcap_profile(snapshot)
            self.state.profiles_vault["封頭"] = clone_profile(endcap)
            self.state.profiles_vault["封尾"] = clone_profile(endcap)
            self.state.w = original.get_int(snapshot.get("w", self.state.w))
            self.state.h = original.get_int(snapshot.get("h", self.state.h))
            self.state.d = original.get_int(snapshot.get("d", self.state.d))
            self.v_w.set(str(self.state.w))
            self.v_h.set(str(self.state.h))
            self.v_d.set(str(self.state.d))
            self.state.struct_mode = "vault"
            self.v_mode.set("vault")
            self.bend_ui.rebuild_tabs()
            self._phase6_last_w = self.state.w
            self._phase6_last_d = self.state.d
            self._phase6_sync_ready = True
            self.do_update()
    
        def _sync_dwd_with_top_whd(self):
            profile = self.state.profiles_vault.get("箱身", [])
            w_segments = [seg for seg in profile if seg.get("core") == "W"]
            d_segments = [seg for seg in profile if seg.get("core") == "D"]
            if len(w_segments) != 1 or len(d_segments) != 2:
                return
    
            top_w = original.get_int(self.v_w.get())
            top_d = original.get_int(self.v_d.get())
            core_w = original.get_int(engine_segment_length_to_ui(w_segments[0]))
            d_values = [original.get_int(engine_segment_length_to_ui(seg)) for seg in d_segments]
    
            last_w = self._phase6_last_w
            last_d = self._phase6_last_d
            if top_w != last_w:
                w_segments[0]["len"] = ui_segment_length_to_engine(w_segments[0], top_w)
                core_w = top_w
            elif core_w != last_w:
                self.v_w.set(str(core_w))
                top_w = core_w
    
            if top_d != last_d:
                for seg in d_segments:
                    seg["len"] = ui_segment_length_to_engine(seg, top_d)
                d_value = top_d
            else:
                changed = [value for value in d_values if value != last_d]
                d_value = changed[0] if changed else d_values[0]
                for seg in d_segments:
                    seg["len"] = ui_segment_length_to_engine(seg, d_value)
                if d_value != top_d:
                    self.v_d.set(str(d_value))
    
            self._phase6_last_w = top_w
            self._phase6_last_d = d_value
    
        def do_update(self):
            if getattr(self, "_phase6_sync_ready", False):
                self._sync_dwd_with_top_whd()
            return original.MainApp.do_update(self)
    
        def export_phase6_snapshot(self) -> dict:
            self.bend_ui.save()
            result = dict(self._phase6_input_snapshot)
            result.update(read_box_body_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"], self._phase6_input_snapshot))
            head = read_endcap_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["封頭"])
            tail = read_endcap_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["封尾"])
            if head != tail:
                raise ValueError("Phase6 目前封頭/封尾共用同一組折彎值，兩個分頁必須一致")
            result.update(head)
            result["h"] = original.get_int(self.v_h.get())
            result["box_body_profile"] = clone_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["箱身"])
            result["head_profile"] = clone_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["封頭"])
            result["tail_profile"] = clone_profile(self.state.profiles_vault["封尾"])
            if hasattr(self, "designer_workspace"):
                result["box_body_structure"] = self.designer_workspace.box_body_structure_state()
            return result
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # FIX11 multi-part bundle helpers. These adapt data only; original Renderer is
    # deliberately untouched.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    
    def _three_segment_profile(left, center, right, *, left_key=None, center_key=None, right_key=None):
        items = [
            {"len": _ui_len(left), "angle": -90},
            {"len": _ui_len(center), "angle": -90},
            {"len": _ui_len(right)},
        ]
        for item, key in zip(items, (left_key, center_key, right_key)):
            if key:
                item["phase6_key"] = key
        return items
    
    
    def build_standard_part_profiles(snapshot: Mapping[str, object], part_key: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
        """Build the original designer's X/Y profiles for one non-vault panel."""
        dims = dict((snapshot.get("part_dimensions") or {}).get(part_key, {}) or {})
        w = _num(dims.get("width", snapshot.get("w", 500)), 500)
        h = _num(dims.get("height", snapshot.get("h", 600)), 600)
        if part_key == "door":
            t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
            outside_add = max(0.0, 2.0 * t)
            profiles = {
                "X": _three_segment_profile(
                    snapshot.get("door_fold_l", 20), max(0.0, w - outside_add), snapshot.get("door_fold_r", 20),
                    left_key="door_fold_l", center_key="door_face_w", right_key="door_fold_r",
                ),
                "Y": _three_segment_profile(
                    snapshot.get("door_fold_b", 20), max(0.0, h - outside_add), snapshot.get("door_fold_t", 20),
                    left_key="door_fold_b", center_key="door_face_h", right_key="door_fold_t",
                ),
            }
            profiles["X"][1]["core"] = "門包外 W"
            profiles["Y"][1]["core"] = "門包外 H"
            profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
            profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
            return profiles
        if part_key == "base_plate":
            bend = snapshot.get("base_plate_bend", 20)
            profiles = {
                "X": _three_segment_profile(bend, w, bend, left_key="base_bend_l", center_key="base_face_w", right_key="base_bend_r"),
                "Y": _three_segment_profile(bend, h, bend, left_key="base_bend_b", center_key="base_face_h", right_key="base_bend_t"),
            }
            t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
            profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
            profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
            return profiles
        if part_key == "indicator_box":
            # part_dimensions carries the real unfolded blank size.  The original
            # Renderer must receive the real BEND-line span (blank - both folds),
            # while FIX13 shows the operator the outside finished-face dimension.
            # For this four-side box that outside dimension is BEND span + 1T.
            fold = _num(snapshot.get("indicator_box_fold", 49), 49)
            t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
            x_core = max(0.0, w - 2.0 * fold)
            y_core = max(0.0, h - 2.0 * fold)
            profiles = {
                "X": _three_segment_profile(fold, x_core, fold, left_key="ib_fold_l", center_key="ib_face_w", right_key="ib_fold_r"),
                "Y": _three_segment_profile(fold, y_core, fold, left_key="ib_fold_b", center_key="ib_face_h", right_key="ib_fold_t"),
            }
            profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
            profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
            return profiles
        if part_key == "indicator_door":
            # The small door's part_dimensions is likewise the unfolded blank.
            # Its Door contract defines finished outside size as BEND span + 2T.
            fold = _num(snapshot.get("indicator_door_fold", 19), 19)
            t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
            x_core = max(0.0, w - 2.0 * fold)
            y_core = max(0.0, h - 2.0 * fold)
            profiles = {
                "X": _three_segment_profile(fold, x_core, fold, left_key="id_fold_l", center_key="id_face_w", right_key="id_fold_r"),
                "Y": _three_segment_profile(fold, y_core, fold, left_key="id_fold_b", center_key="id_face_h", right_key="id_fold_t"),
            }
            profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
            profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
            return profiles
        # Generic Unknown add: start from a simple editable X/Y panel without
        # inventing manufacturing policy.
        t = _num(snapshot.get("t", 2), 2)
        profiles = {
            "X": _three_segment_profile(25, w, 25),
            "Y": _three_segment_profile(25, h, 25),
        }
        profiles["X"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["X"], t)
        profiles["Y"] = apply_outside_dimension_compensation(profiles["Y"], t)
        return profiles
    
    
    def _profile_value(profile, key, default=0):
        for seg in profile or ():
            if seg.get("phase6_key") == key:
                return _ui_len(seg.get("len"))
        return _ui_len(default)
    
    
    def read_standard_part_profiles(part_key, profiles, original_snapshot):
        """Return only Phase6 values that have an authoritative reverse mapping."""
        x = list((profiles or {}).get("X", ()))
        y = list((profiles or {}).get("Y", ()))
        if part_key == "door":
            return {
                "door_fold_l": _profile_value(x, "door_fold_l", original_snapshot.get("door_fold_l", 20)),
                "door_fold_r": _profile_value(x, "door_fold_r", original_snapshot.get("door_fold_r", 20)),
                "door_fold_b": _profile_value(y, "door_fold_b", original_snapshot.get("door_fold_b", 20)),
                "door_fold_t": _profile_value(y, "door_fold_t", original_snapshot.get("door_fold_t", 20)),
            }
        if part_key == "base_plate":
            vals = [
                _profile_value(x, "base_bend_l", original_snapshot.get("base_plate_bend", 20)),
